
===== FILE backend/app/config.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path

from dotenv import load_dotenv


PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
load_dotenv(PROJECT_ROOT / ".env")


def _default_data_dir(
    *,
    platform_name: str | None = None,
    home: Path | None = None,
) -> Path:
    effective_platform = platform_name or sys.platform
    if effective_platform == "darwin":
        return (home or Path.home()) / "Library" / "Application Support" / "3d66-label-system"
    local_app_data = os.getenv("LOCALAPPDATA")
    if local_app_data:
        return Path(local_app_data) / "3d66-label-system"
    return PROJECT_ROOT / "data"


def _production_feedback_token(data_dir: Path) -> str | None:
    environment_token = os.getenv("PRODUCTION_FEEDBACK_TOKEN", "").strip()
    if environment_token:
        return environment_token
    token_file = Path(
        os.getenv(
            "PRODUCTION_FEEDBACK_TOKEN_FILE",
            str(data_dir / "secrets" / "production-feedback.token"),
        )
    ).expanduser()
    if not token_file.is_file():
        return None
    token = token_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    return token or None


def _integration_token(
    data_dir: Path,
    *,
    environment_name: str,
    file_name: str,
) -> str | None:
    environment_token = os.getenv(environment_name, "").strip()
    if environment_token:
        return environment_token
    token_file = Path(
        os.getenv(
            f"{environment_name}_FILE",
            str(data_dir / "secrets" / file_name),
        )
    ).expanduser()
    if not token_file.is_file():
        return None
    token = token_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    return token or None


def _optional_path(environment_name: str) -> Path | None:
    """Read an optional mounted path without creating or probing it."""
    value = os.getenv(environment_name, "").strip()
    if not value:
        return None
    return Path(value).expanduser().resolve()


def _bounded_int(
    environment_name: str,
    *,
    default: int,
    minimum: int,
    maximum: int,
) -> int:
    raw_value = os.getenv(environment_name, str(default)).strip()
    try:
        value = int(raw_value)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{environment_name} 必须是整数") from exc
    if not minimum <= value <= maximum:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{environment_name} 必须在 {minimum} 到 {maximum} 之间"
        )
    return value


def _optional_bounded_int(
    environment_name: str,
    *,
    maximum: int,
) -> int | None:
    raw_value = os.getenv(environment_name, "").strip()
    if not raw_value or raw_value == "0":
        return None
    return _bounded_int(
        environment_name,
        default=0,
        minimum=1,
        maximum=maximum,
    )


def _strict_optional_bounded_int(
    environment_name: str,
    *,
    maximum: int,
) -> int | None:
    raw_value = os.getenv(environment_name, "").strip()
    if not raw_value:
        return None
    return _bounded_int(
        environment_name,
        default=0,
        minimum=1,
        maximum=maximum,
    )


def _strict_bool(environment_name: str, *, default: bool) -> bool:
    raw_value = os.getenv(
        environment_name,
        "true" if default else "false",
    ).strip().lower()
    if raw_value == "true":
        return True
    if raw_value == "false":
        return False
    raise ValueError(f"{environment_name} 必须是 true 或 false")


def _database_url(data_dir: Path) -> str:
    environment_url = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL", "").strip()
    if environment_url:
        return environment_url
    secret_file = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL_FILE", "").strip()
    if secret_file:
        try:
            file_url = Path(secret_file).expanduser().read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            ).strip()
        except OSError as exc:
            raise ValueError("DATABASE_URL_FILE 无法读取") from exc
        if not file_url:
            raise ValueError("DATABASE_URL_FILE 不能为空")
        return file_url
    return f"sqlite:///{(data_dir / 'database' / 'app.db').as_posix()}"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    project_root: Path
    data_dir: Path
    database_path: Path
    upload_dir: Path
    model_media_cache_root: Path
    nas_maps_root: Path | None
    nas_history_fallback_root: Path | None
    log_dir: Path
    prompt_source: Path
    frontend_dist: Path
    host: str
    port: int
    session_days: int
    production_feedback_token: str | None
    content_ingress_token: str | None
    connector_ingress_token: str | None
    label_consumer_token: str | None
    operations_metrics_token: str | None
    prompt_lab_token: str | None
    backup_status_file: Path
    restore_status_file: Path
    database_url: str
    sqlite_worker_limit: int
    worker_process_count: int | None
    worker_cluster_expected_count: int | None
    database_pool_size: int
    database_max_overflow: int
    deploy_environment: str
    session_cookie_secure: bool
    allow_insecure_session_cookie: bool
    session_cookie_name: str
    allow_default_admin_password: bool
    login_throttle_enabled: bool


@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    data_dir = Path(os.getenv("DATA_DIR", str(_default_data_dir()))).expanduser().resolve()
    deploy_environment = (
        os.getenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", "local").strip().lower()
        or "local"
    )
    frontend_dist = Path(
        os.getenv("FRONTEND_DIST", str(PROJECT_ROOT / "frontend" / "dist"))
    ).expanduser().resolve()
    settings = Settings(
        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
        data_dir=data_dir,
        database_path=data_dir / "database" / "app.db",
        upload_dir=data_dir / "images",
        model_media_cache_root=Path(
            os.getenv(
                "MODEL_MEDIA_CACHE_ROOT",
                str(data_dir / "cache" / "model-media"),
            )
        ).expanduser().resolve(),
        nas_maps_root=_optional_path("NAS_MAPS_ROOT"),
        nas_history_fallback_root=_optional_path("NAS_HISTORY_FALLBACK_ROOT"),
        log_dir=data_dir / "logs",
        prompt_source=PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "3d66-aesthetic-v2.1.md",
        frontend_dist=frontend_dist,
        host=os.getenv("APP_HOST", "0.0.0.0"),
        port=int(os.getenv("APP_PORT", "8080")),
        session_days=max(1, int(os.getenv("SESSION_DAYS", "7"))),
        production_feedback_token=_production_feedback_token(data_dir),
        content_ingress_token=_integration_token(
            data_dir,
            environment_name="CONTENT_INGRESS_TOKEN",
            file_name="content-ingress.token",
        ),
        connector_ingress_token=_integration_token(
            data_dir,
            environment_name="CONNECTOR_INGRESS_TOKEN",
            file_name="connector-ingress.token",
        ),
        label_consumer_token=_integration_token(
            data_dir,
            environment_name="LABEL_CONSUMER_TOKEN",
            file_name="label-consumer.token",
        ),
        operations_metrics_token=_integration_token(
            data_dir,
            environment_name="OPERATIONS_METRICS_TOKEN",
            file_name="operations-metrics.token",
        ),
        prompt_lab_token=_integration_token(
            data_dir,
            environment_name="PROMPT_LAB_TOKEN",
            file_name="prompt-lab.token",
        ),
        backup_status_file=(
            data_dir / "operations" / "last-successful-backup.json"
        ),
        restore_status_file=(
            data_dir / "operations" / "last-successful-restore.json"
        ),
        database_url=_database_url(data_dir),
        sqlite_worker_limit=max(1, int(os.getenv("SQLITE_WORKER_LIMIT", "2"))),
        worker_process_count=_optional_bounded_int(
            "WORKER_PROCESS_COUNT",
            maximum=32,
        ),
        worker_cluster_expected_count=_strict_optional_bounded_int(
            "WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT",
            maximum=10_000,
        ),
        database_pool_size=_bounded_int(
            "DATABASE_POOL_SIZE",
            default=2,
            minimum=1,
            maximum=20,
        ),
        database_max_overflow=_bounded_int(
            "DATABASE_MAX_OVERFLOW",
            default=0,
            minimum=0,
            maximum=20,
        ),
        deploy_environment=deploy_environment,
        # 同一主机上并行部署（正式 443/80 与测试 8082/8443）共用 Cookie 名会互相覆盖，
        # 且浏览器拒绝用 HTTP 响应改写已有的 Secure 同名 Cookie；测试服应配独立名称。
        session_cookie_name=(
            re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_-]", "", os.getenv("SESSION_COOKIE_NAME", "3d66_session").strip())
            or "3d66_session"
        ),
        allow_insecure_session_cookie=_strict_bool(
            "ALLOW_INSECURE_SESSION_COOKIE", default=False
        ),
        session_cookie_secure=_strict_bool(
            "SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE",
            default=False,
        ),
        allow_default_admin_password=_strict_bool(
            "ALLOW_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD",
            default=deploy_environment != "production",
        ),
        # 2026-09-26 老大裁决：先去除登录限流。容器内只能看到 docker 网关地址，
        # 按来源限流等于全站共用一个桶；按账号限流又能被任何人锁住管理员。
        # 需要时设 LOGIN_THROTTLE_ENABLED=true 恢复。
        login_throttle_enabled=_strict_bool("LOGIN_THROTTLE_ENABLED", default=False),
    )
    settings.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings.upload_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings.model_media_cache_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings.log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings.backup_status_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings.restore_status_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return settings

===== FILE backend/app/connector_adapter_registry.py =====
"""Which connector adapters the platform can actually execute, and what each needs.

Registering a connector used to require someone who knew that
``schema_fingerprint`` is a 64-hex digest, which adapter key the delivery code
dispatches on, and where the digest comes from for that adapter - so every new
category went through an engineer. This registry is the missing half of a
visual configuration surface: the operator picks an adapter, and the platform
supplies the parts only it can know.

``executable`` is the honest bit. A contract can be registered with any adapter
key the regex allows, but only these keys are dispatched by
``connector_delivery.resolve_configured_downstream_adapter`` and the upstream
poll path. Anything else registers fine and then never delivers, which is
exactly the failure that is worth surfacing in the UI rather than at 3am.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Adapter:
    key: str
    direction: str
    label: str
    summary: str
    #: 'constant' - fixed by the adapter and verified at delivery
    #: 'live_schema' - read from the real table behind the locator
    #: 'convention' - a fixed value the platform records but does not verify
    fingerprint_source: str
    locator_hint: str
    executable: bool
    constant_fingerprint: str | None = None
    #: Deployment-side environment the locator resolves through.
    environment_hint: str = ""


#: The factory list/confirm upstream has no live schema to read; the digest is a
#: registration convention, kept here so the two places that mint it agree.
FACTORY_LIST_CONFIRM_FINGERPRINT = hashlib.sha256(b'factory-list-confirm-material-v1').hexdigest()
#: Computed rather than imported: pulling it from factory_submit_delivery drags
#: the whole delivery chain into anything that just wants to list adapters. A
#: test asserts the two stay equal.
FACTORY_SUBMIT_FINGERPRINT = hashlib.sha256(b'factory-submit-result-v1').hexdigest()


ADAPTERS: tuple[Adapter, ...] = (
    Adapter(
        key='sql-envelope-v1',
        direction='downstream',
        label='写入数据库表',
        summary='把结果逐条写进一张投递信封表，按业务幂等键去重，投递后回读校验。',
        fingerprint_source='live_schema',
        locator_hint='logical.<名称>，部署侧配置 DSN 与物理表名',
        environment_hint='LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_<定位符>[_TABLE|_SECRET_REFERENCE]',
        executable=True,
    ),
    Adapter(
        key='factory-submit-v1',
        direction='downstream',
        label='提交到工厂接口',
        summary='把打标结果 POST 到工厂 submit 接口，与 claim/confirmClaim 同一签名方案；服务端幂等。',
        fingerprint_source='constant',
        locator_hint='logical.<名称>，部署侧配置端点 URL，且必须是允许的工厂域名',
        environment_hint='LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_<定位符>',
        executable=True,
        constant_fingerprint=FACTORY_SUBMIT_FINGERPRINT,
    ),
    Adapter(
        key='factory-test-list-confirm-v1',
        direction='upstream',
        label='工厂任务列表与认领',
        summary='从工厂接口列出待办任务并确认认领；claim 只读，confirmClaim 会转移归属。',
        fingerprint_source='convention',
        locator_hint='logical.<名称>，部署侧配置端点 URL',
        environment_hint='由进料配置指定上游域名',
        executable=True,
        constant_fingerprint=FACTORY_LIST_CONFIRM_FINGERPRINT,
    ),
    Adapter(
        key='manual-push-v1',
        direction='upstream',
        label='占位：外部推送',
        summary='占位合同，用于在真实上游接入前冻结工作流形状；平台不会真正读取任何数据。',
        fingerprint_source='convention',
        locator_hint='logical.placeholder.<名称>',
        executable=False,
    ),
    Adapter(
        key='local-sql-envelope-v1',
        direction='downstream',
        label='占位：本地模拟投递',
        summary='占位合同，写本地虚构表用于演练；不会写入公司业务库。',
        fingerprint_source='convention',
        locator_hint='logical.placeholder.<名称>',
        executable=False,
    ),
)

BY_KEY = {item.key: item for item in ADAPTERS}


def adapter(key: str) -> Adapter | None:
    return BY_KEY.get((key or '').strip())


def derive_fingerprint(key: str, logical_locator: str) -> str | None:
    """The digest the platform can supply itself, or None if it cannot.

    'live_schema' deliberately returns None: reading the real table needs the
    deployment-side DSN and table name, and a registration form is not the place
    to open that connection. The existing probe endpoint is.
    """
    item = adapter(key)
    if item is None:
        return None
    if item.fingerprint_source == 'constant':
        return item.constant_fingerprint
    if item.fingerprint_source == 'convention':
        return item.constant_fingerprint
    return None


def as_payload() -> list[dict[str, object]]:
    return [
        {
            'adapter_key': item.key,
            'direction': item.direction,
            'label': item.label,
            'summary': item.summary,
            'fingerprint_source': item.fingerprint_source,
            'fingerprint_supplied_by_platform': derive_fingerprint(item.key, '') is not None,
            'locator_hint': item.locator_hint,
            'environment_hint': item.environment_hint,
            'executable': item.executable,
        }
        for item in ADAPTERS
    ]

===== FILE backend/app/connector_contracts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import re
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import ConnectorContractVersion


# Frozen workflows use dotted logical namespaces; each segment keeps the
# original identifier alphabet, with no empty segments or path separators.
_KEY = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*(?:\.[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*)*$")
_CATEGORY_KEY = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{0,39}$")
_ADAPTER_KEY = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{0,79}$")
_LOGICAL_LOCATOR = re.compile(
    r"^logical\.[a-z0-9]+(?:[._-][a-z0-9]+)*$"
)
_SHA256 = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
_DIRECTIONS = {"upstream", "downstream"}
_STATUSES = {"draft", "active", "retired"}
_FORBIDDEN_KEYS = {
    "access_token",
    "api_key",
    "connection_string",
    "database_url",
    "dsn",
    "password",
    "physical_table",
    "query",
    "secret",
    "sql",
    "table",
    "token",
}
_FORBIDDEN_VALUE_PARTS = (
    "://",
    "password=",
    "token=",
    "secret=",
    "select ",
    "insert ",
    "update ",
    "delete ",
    "drop ",
    "alter ",
)


class ConnectorContractError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _required_text(value: str, *, label: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip()
    if not normalized:
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            f"{label}不能为空",
        )
    return normalized


def _safe_mapping(value: Mapping[str, Any], *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            f"{label}必须是对象",
        )

    def validate(item: Any, *, path: str) -> Any:
        if isinstance(item, Mapping):
            normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
            for raw_key, raw_value in item.items():
                key = str(raw_key).strip()
                if not key:
                    raise ConnectorContractError(
                        "connector_contract_invalid",
                        f"{label}包含空字段名",
                    )
                if key.lower() in _FORBIDDEN_KEYS:
                    raise ConnectorContractError(
                        "connector_contract_sensitive_configuration",
                        f"{label}不得包含敏感配置：{path}{key}",
                    )
                normalized[key] = validate(
                    raw_value,
                    path=f"{path}{key}.",
                )
            return normalized
        if isinstance(item, Sequence) and not isinstance(
            item,
            (str, bytes, bytearray),
        ):
            return [
                validate(child, path=f"{path}{index}.")
                for index, child in enumerate(item)
            ]
        if isinstance(item, str):
            lowered = item.strip().lower()
            if any(part in lowered for part in _FORBIDDEN_VALUE_PARTS):
                raise ConnectorContractError(
                    "connector_contract_sensitive_configuration",
                    f"{label}不得包含敏感配置：{path.rstrip('.')}",
                )
            return item.strip()
        if item is None or isinstance(item, (bool, int, float)):
            return item
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            f"{label}包含不支持的值：{path.rstrip('.')}",
        )

    return validate(value, path="")


def create_connector_contract_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract_key: str,
    direction: str,
    category_key: str,
    logical_locator: str,
    adapter_key: str,
    schema_fingerprint: str,
    field_mapping: Mapping[str, Any],
    cursor_contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    idempotency_contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    batch_size: int,
    timeout_seconds: int,
    owner: str,
    status: str,
    created_by: str,
) -> ConnectorContractVersion:
    normalized_key = _required_text(contract_key, label="合同标识")
    normalized_direction = _required_text(direction, label="方向").lower()
    normalized_category = _required_text(category_key, label="类目标识")
    normalized_locator = _required_text(logical_locator, label="逻辑引用")
    normalized_adapter = _required_text(adapter_key, label="适配器标识")
    normalized_status = _required_text(status, label="状态").lower()
    normalized_fingerprint = schema_fingerprint.strip().lower()
    normalized_owner = _required_text(owner, label="负责人")
    normalized_creator = _required_text(created_by, label="创建人")

    if len(normalized_key) > 120 or not _KEY.fullmatch(normalized_key):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "合同标识格式无效",
        )
    if normalized_direction not in _DIRECTIONS:
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "方向只能是上游或下游",
        )
    if not _CATEGORY_KEY.fullmatch(normalized_category):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "类目标识格式无效",
        )
    if not _LOGICAL_LOCATOR.fullmatch(normalized_locator):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_physical_locator",
            "连接位置只能填写以 logical. 开头的逻辑引用",
        )
    if not _ADAPTER_KEY.fullmatch(normalized_adapter):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "适配器标识格式无效",
        )
    if not _SHA256.fullmatch(normalized_fingerprint):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "结构指纹必须是 SHA-256",
        )
    if normalized_status not in _STATUSES:
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "合同状态无效",
        )
    if isinstance(batch_size, bool) or not 1 <= batch_size <= 1000:
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "单批处理量必须在 1 到 1000 之间",
        )
    if isinstance(timeout_seconds, bool) or not 1 <= timeout_seconds <= 600:
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "超时时间必须在 1 到 600 秒之间",
        )

    normalized_mapping = _safe_mapping(field_mapping, label="字段映射")
    normalized_cursor = _safe_mapping(cursor_contract, label="游标规则")
    normalized_idempotency = _safe_mapping(
        idempotency_contract,
        label="去重规则",
    )
    definition = {
        "adapter_key": normalized_adapter,
        "batch_size": batch_size,
        "category_key": normalized_category,
        "contract_key": normalized_key,
        "cursor_contract": normalized_cursor,
        "direction": normalized_direction,
        "field_mapping": normalized_mapping,
        "idempotency_contract": normalized_idempotency,
        "logical_locator": normalized_locator,
        "owner": normalized_owner,
        "schema_fingerprint": normalized_fingerprint,
        "status": normalized_status,
        "timeout_seconds": timeout_seconds,
    }
    contract_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(definition).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(
            ConnectorContractVersion.contract_key == normalized_key,
            ConnectorContractVersion.contract_hash == contract_hash,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing

    latest_version = db.scalar(
        select(func.max(ConnectorContractVersion.version)).where(
            ConnectorContractVersion.contract_key == normalized_key
        )
    )
    contract = ConnectorContractVersion(
        contract_key=normalized_key,
        version=int(latest_version or 0) + 1,
        direction=normalized_direction,
        category_key=normalized_category,
        logical_locator=normalized_locator,
        adapter_key=normalized_adapter,
        schema_fingerprint=normalized_fingerprint,
        field_mapping_json=canonical_json(normalized_mapping),
        cursor_contract_json=canonical_json(normalized_cursor),
        idempotency_contract_json=canonical_json(normalized_idempotency),
        batch_size=batch_size,
        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
        owner=normalized_owner,
        status=normalized_status,
        contract_hash=contract_hash,
        created_by=normalized_creator,
    )
    db.add(contract)
    db.flush()
    return contract

===== FILE backend/app/connector_delivery.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re

    connector_is_paused,
    paused_workflow_version_ids,
)

    compute_live_schema_fingerprint,
    resolve_locator_secret_reference,
    resolve_locator_table,

    writable_connection_factory,
)

    ConnectorContractVersion,
    RuntimeAuditEvent,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
)

)

class ConnectorDeliveryError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DownstreamSafetyEvidence:
    least_privileged: bool
    schema_fingerprint: str
    verifier: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryKey:
    business_idempotency_key: str
    mechanism_version_id: int
    payload_hash: str
    # ADR-0064：同一业务身份的第几版；review_state=reviewed 表示人工纠偏后重发
    revision: int = 1
    review_state: str = "auto"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryItem:
    event_id: int
    key: DeliveryKey
    payload: Mapping[str, Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryBatch:
    target_contract_key: str
    contract_version: int
    items: tuple[DeliveryItem, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryWriteResult:
    key: DeliveryKey
    succeeded: bool
    external_record_key: str = ""
    error_code: str = ""
    retryable: bool = False
    # 上游自己的一句话说明（已清洗截断），只进审计详情：错误码要可枚举，这句不是。
    diagnostic: str = ""
    # 只读确认类重试要等下游落定，不能按秒级技术退避把次数用完；None 用投递默认退避。
    retry_after_seconds: float | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryAttempt:
    results: tuple[DeliveryWriteResult, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveredRow:
    key: DeliveryKey
    external_record_key: str


def overwrite_verdict(
    stored: DeliveryKey, new: DeliveryKey, *, source_advanced: bool = False,
) -> str | None:
    """ADR-0064 覆盖护栏：返回拒绝码；None 表示允许原地覆盖。

    - 旧版本迟到（revision 更小）不能覆盖新版本；
    - 同一 revision 内容不同视为身份冲突（fail-closed）；
    - 自动结果只能在源内容已进入新版时覆盖旧版人工已审行。
    """

    if new.revision < stored.revision:
        return "downstream_stale_revision"
    if new.revision == stored.revision:
        return "downstream_identity_conflict"
    if (
        stored.review_state == "reviewed"
        and new.review_state != "reviewed"
        and not source_advanced
    ):
        return "downstream_review_guard"
    return None


OVERWRITE_REJECTION_CODES = frozenset(
    {"downstream_stale_revision", "downstream_review_guard"}
)


class DownstreamAdapter(Protocol):
    def verify_target(self) -> DownstreamSafetyEvidence: ...

    def deliver(self, batch: DeliveryBatch) -> DeliveryAttempt: ...

    def read_back(self, keys: Sequence[DeliveryKey]) -> tuple[DeliveredRow, ...]: ...


class FixtureDownstreamAdapter:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        schema_fingerprint: str,
        least_privileged: bool = True,
        fail_once_keys: set[str] | None = None,
        corrupt_readback_keys: set[str] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._evidence = DownstreamSafetyEvidence(
            least_privileged=least_privileged,
            schema_fingerprint=schema_fingerprint,
            verifier="fixture-explicit-least-privilege",
        )
        self._fail_once_keys = set(fail_once_keys or ())
        self._corrupt_readback_keys = set(corrupt_readback_keys or ())
        self._rows: dict[str, DeliveredRow] = {}
        self._payloads: dict[str, Mapping[str, Any]] = {}
        self.attempted_batches: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    @property
    def rows(self) -> tuple[DeliveredRow, ...]:
        return tuple(self._rows[key] for key in sorted(self._rows))

    def verify_target(self) -> DownstreamSafetyEvidence:
        return self._evidence

    def deliver(self, batch: DeliveryBatch) -> DeliveryAttempt:
        self.attempted_batches.append(
            tuple(item.key.business_idempotency_key for item in batch.items)
        )
        results: list[DeliveryWriteResult] = []
        for item in batch.items:
            business_key = item.key.business_idempotency_key
            if business_key in self._fail_once_keys:
                self._fail_once_keys.remove(business_key)
                results.append(
                    DeliveryWriteResult(
                        key=item.key,
                        succeeded=False,
                        error_code="downstream_transient_failure",
                        retryable=True,
                    )
                )
                continue
            existing = self._rows.get(business_key)
            if existing is not None and existing.key != item.key:
                previous_payload = self._payloads.get(business_key, {})
                verdict = (
                    "downstream_stale_revision"
                    if source_revision_is_stale(previous_payload, item.payload)
                    else overwrite_verdict(
                        existing.key, item.key,
                        source_advanced=source_revision_advances(previous_payload, item.payload),
                    )
                )
                if verdict is not None:
                    results.append(
                        DeliveryWriteResult(
                            key=item.key,
                            succeeded=False,
                            error_code=verdict,
                            retryable=False,
                        )
                    )
                    continue
            external_record_key = f"fixture:{business_key}"
            self._rows[business_key] = DeliveredRow(
                key=item.key,
                external_record_key=external_record_key,
            )
            self._payloads[business_key] = json.loads(canonical_json(dict(item.payload)))
            results.append(
                DeliveryWriteResult(
                    key=item.key,
                    succeeded=True,
                    external_record_key=external_record_key,
                )
            )
        return DeliveryAttempt(results=tuple(results))

    def read_back(self, keys: Sequence[DeliveryKey]) -> tuple[DeliveredRow, ...]:
        rows: list[DeliveredRow] = []
        for key in keys:
            row = self._rows.get(key.business_idempotency_key)
            if row is None:
                continue
            if key.business_idempotency_key in self._corrupt_readback_keys:
                row = DeliveredRow(
                    key=DeliveryKey(
                        business_idempotency_key=row.key.business_idempotency_key,
                        mechanism_version_id=row.key.mechanism_version_id,
                        payload_hash="0" * 64,
                    ),
                    external_record_key=row.external_record_key,
                )
            rows.append(row)
        return tuple(rows)


_IDENTIFIER = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,62}$")


class SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter:
    """Insert immutable delivery envelopes and reconcile them by business key."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        connection_factory: Callable[[], AbstractContextManager[Connection]],
        table_name: str,
        evidence: DownstreamSafetyEvidence,
    ) -> None:
        if not _IDENTIFIER.fullmatch(table_name):
            raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                "downstream_target_invalid",
                "下游物理目标未通过标识符检查",
            )
        self._connection_factory = connection_factory
        self._table_name = table_name
        self._evidence = evidence

    def verify_target(self) -> DownstreamSafetyEvidence:
        return self._evidence

    @staticmethod
    def _stored_key(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> DeliveryKey:
        return DeliveryKey(
            business_idempotency_key=str(row["business_idempotency_key"]),
            mechanism_version_id=int(row["mechanism_version_id"]),
            payload_hash=str(row["payload_hash"]),
            revision=int(row["revision"]),
            review_state=str(row["review_state"]),
        )

    def _select_one(
        self,
        connection: Connection,
        business_key: str,
    ) -> Mapping[str, Any] | None:
        quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
        return connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT business_idempotency_key, mechanism_version_id, "
                "payload_hash, payload_json, external_record_key, revision, review_state "
                f"FROM {quote(self._table_name)} "
                "WHERE business_idempotency_key = :business_key"
            ),
            {"business_key": business_key},
        ).mappings().one_or_none()

    def deliver(self, batch: DeliveryBatch) -> DeliveryAttempt:
        results: list[DeliveryWriteResult] = []
        with self._connection_factory() as connection:
            quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
            for item in batch.items:
                business_key = item.key.business_idempotency_key
                existing = self._select_one(connection, business_key)
                published_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
                if existing is not None:
                    stored_key = self._stored_key(existing)
                    if stored_key == item.key:
                        results.append(
                            DeliveryWriteResult(
                                key=item.key,
                                succeeded=True,
                                external_record_key=str(
                                    existing["external_record_key"]
                                ),
                            )
                        )
                        continue
                    try:
                        previous_payload = json.loads(existing["payload_json"])
                    except (TypeError, ValueError):
                        previous_payload = {}
                    if not isinstance(previous_payload, dict):
                        previous_payload = {}
                    verdict = (
                        "downstream_stale_revision"
                        if source_revision_is_stale(previous_payload, item.payload)
                        else overwrite_verdict(
                            stored_key, item.key,
                            source_advanced=source_revision_advances(previous_payload, item.payload),
                        )
                    )
                    if verdict is not None:
                        results.append(
                            DeliveryWriteResult(
                                key=item.key,
                                succeeded=False,
                                error_code=verdict,
                                retryable=False,
                            )
                        )
                        continue
                    # Compare the exact row used by the source/review precheck.
                    # A concurrent review must force readback reconciliation.
                    connection.execute(
                        text(
                            f"UPDATE {quote(self._table_name)} SET "
                            "mechanism_version_id = :mechanism_version_id, "
                            "payload_hash = :payload_hash, "
                            "payload_json = :payload_json, "
                            "revision = :revision, "
                            "review_state = :review_state, "
                            "published_at = :published_at "
                            "WHERE business_idempotency_key = :business_key "
                            "AND revision = :stored_revision "
                            "AND payload_hash = :stored_payload_hash "
                            "AND review_state = :stored_review_state "
                            "AND mechanism_version_id = :stored_mechanism_version_id"
                        ),
                        {
                            "business_key": business_key,
                            "mechanism_version_id": item.key.mechanism_version_id,
                            "payload_hash": item.key.payload_hash,
                            "payload_json": canonical_json(dict(item.payload)),
                            "revision": item.key.revision,
                            "review_state": item.key.review_state,
                            "published_at": published_at,
                            "stored_revision": stored_key.revision,
                            "stored_payload_hash": stored_key.payload_hash,
                            "stored_review_state": stored_key.review_state,
                            "stored_mechanism_version_id": stored_key.mechanism_version_id,
                        },
                    )
                else:
                    values = {
                        "business_key": business_key,
                        "mechanism_version_id": item.key.mechanism_version_id,
                        "payload_hash": item.key.payload_hash,
                        "payload_json": canonical_json(dict(item.payload)),
                        "external_record_key": business_key,
                        "revision": item.key.revision,
                        "review_state": item.key.review_state,
                        "published_at": published_at,
                    }
                    columns = (
                        "business_idempotency_key, mechanism_version_id, "
                        "payload_hash, payload_json, external_record_key, "
                        "revision, review_state, published_at"
                    )
                    parameters = (
                        ":business_key, :mechanism_version_id, :payload_hash, "
                        ":payload_json, :external_record_key, "
                        ":revision, :review_state, :published_at"
                    )
                    dialect = connection.dialect.name
                    if dialect in {"mysql", "mariadb"}:
                        statement = text(
                            f"INSERT IGNORE INTO {quote(self._table_name)} "
                            f"({columns}) VALUES ({parameters})"
                        )
                    elif dialect in {"sqlite", "postgresql"}:
                        statement = text(
                            f"INSERT INTO {quote(self._table_name)} ({columns}) "
                            f"VALUES ({parameters}) "
                            "ON CONFLICT(business_idempotency_key) DO NOTHING"
                        )
                    else:
                        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                            "downstream_adapter_unavailable",
                            "下游数据库类型尚无安全插入实现",
                        )
                    connection.execute(statement, values)
                stored = self._select_one(connection, business_key)
                if stored is None or self._stored_key(stored) != item.key:
                    results.append(
                        DeliveryWriteResult(
                            key=item.key,
                            succeeded=False,
                            error_code="downstream_identity_conflict",
                            retryable=False,
                        )
                    )
                    continue
                results.append(
                    DeliveryWriteResult(
                        key=item.key,
                        succeeded=True,
                        external_record_key=str(stored["external_record_key"]),
                    )
                )
        return DeliveryAttempt(results=tuple(results))

    def read_back(self, keys: Sequence[DeliveryKey]) -> tuple[DeliveredRow, ...]:
        rows: list[DeliveredRow] = []
        with self._connection_factory() as connection:
            for key in keys:
                stored = self._select_one(
                    connection,
                    key.business_idempotency_key,
                )
                if stored is None:
                    continue
                rows.append(
                    DeliveredRow(
                        key=self._stored_key(stored),
                        external_record_key=str(stored["external_record_key"]),
                    )
                )
        return tuple(rows)


def resolve_configured_downstream_adapter(
    target: ConnectorContractVersion,
) -> DownstreamAdapter:
    if target.direction == "downstream" and target.adapter_key == "inspiration-factory-submit-v1":
        from .inspiration_factory_delivery import resolve_inspiration_factory_adapter
        return resolve_inspiration_factory_adapter(target)
    if target.direction == "downstream" and target.adapter_key == "factory-submit-v1":
        # HTTPS submit to the factory; endpoint is deployment-side and allowlisted.
        from .factory_submit_delivery import resolve_factory_submit_adapter
        return resolve_factory_submit_adapter(target)
    if target.direction != "downstream" or target.adapter_key != "sql-envelope-v1":
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_adapter_unavailable",
            "该下游合同尚未配置可执行适配器",
        )
    try:
        secret_reference = resolve_locator_secret_reference(
            target.logical_locator
        )
        table_name = resolve_locator_table(target.logical_locator)
        least_privileged, verifier = verify_least_privilege(
            target.logical_locator,
            secret_reference,
            table_name=table_name,
        )
        deploy_env = (
            os.getenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV") or "production"
        ).strip().lower()
        if not least_privileged and deploy_env in {"local", "dev", "test", "ci"}:
            least_privileged = True
            verifier = f"{verifier}；仅非生产环境豁免"
        live_fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
            target.logical_locator,
            secret_reference,
            table_name=table_name,
        )
        connection_factory = writable_connection_factory(
            target.logical_locator,
            secret_reference,
        )
    except ExternalDatasourceError as exc:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_adapter_unavailable",
            "下游连接尚未配置或无法通过安全检查",
        ) from exc
    return SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(
        connection_factory=connection_factory,
        table_name=table_name,
        evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(
            least_privileged=least_privileged,
            schema_fingerprint=live_fingerprint,
            verifier=verifier,
        ),
    )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryBatchResult:
    target_contract_id: int
    claimed_event_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    delivered_event_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    retry_event_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    blocked_event_ids: tuple[int, ...]


def _validate_target(target: ConnectorContractVersion) -> None:
    if target.direction != "downstream":
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_contract_direction_invalid",
            "所选合同不是下游写入合同",
        )
    if target.status != "active":
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_contract_inactive",
            "下游写入合同未启用",
        )


def _key(event: RuntimeOutboxEvent) -> DeliveryKey:
    return DeliveryKey(
        business_idempotency_key=event.business_idempotency_key,
        mechanism_version_id=event.mechanism_version_id,
        payload_hash=event.payload_hash,
        revision=int(event.revision or 1),
        review_state=str(event.review_state or "auto"),
    )


def _audit_attempt(
    db: Session,
    event: RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    target: ConnectorContractVersion,
    *,
    worker_id: str,
    action: str,
    details: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> None:
    db.add(
        RuntimeAuditEvent(
            event_key=(
                f"outbox:{event.id}:attempt:{event.attempt_count}:{action}"
            ),
            entity_type="runtime_outbox_event",
            entity_key=str(event.id),
            action=action,
            actor=worker_id[:80],
            details_json=canonical_json(
                {
                    "target_contract_id": target.id,
                    "target_contract_key": target.contract_key,
                    "target_contract_version": target.version,
                    "business_idempotency_key": event.business_idempotency_key,
                    "mechanism_version_id": event.mechanism_version_id,
                    "payload_hash": event.payload_hash,
                    **dict(details),
                }
            ),
        )
    )


# 写入成功但读回尚未确认时，隔这么久再只读确认一次（10-01 事件 71215、78663：工厂落定滞后数秒）。
READBACK_CONFIRM_DELAY_SECONDS = 30.0


def _failure(
    *,
    error_code: str,
    retryable: bool,
    retry_after_seconds: float | None = None,
) -> RuntimeFailure:
    return RuntimeFailure(
        error_code=error_code,
        error_message="下游交付未通过逐条确认",
        classification="transient" if retryable else "contract",
        retryable=retryable,
        # None lets fail_outbox_event apply its backoff; 0 made a retryable
        # failure due again immediately, so a few seconds of downstream jitter
        # used up every attempt and dead-lettered the event.
        retry_after_seconds=retry_after_seconds,
    )


# A claimed batch holds its leases across adapter.deliver (up to batch_size
# requests, each allowed the full timeout) plus read_back. A lease of one
# request timeout expired mid-batch; completion then raised outbox_lease_stale
# and rolled back the whole transaction, re-submitting events the downstream
# had already accepted. Size the lease for the worst case, bounded so a crashed
# worker's claim still becomes recoverable within the hour.
DELIVERY_LEASE_MARGIN_SECONDS = 60
MAX_DELIVERY_LEASE_SECONDS = 3600


def _delivery_lease_seconds(target: ConnectorContractVersion) -> int:
    worst = target.timeout_seconds * (target.batch_size + 1) + DELIVERY_LEASE_MARGIN_SECONDS
    return max(target.timeout_seconds, min(worst, MAX_DELIVERY_LEASE_SECONDS))


def deliver_outbox_batch(
    db: Session,
    target: ConnectorContractVersion,
    *,
    worker_id: str,
    adapter: DownstreamAdapter,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> DeliveryBatchResult:
    _validate_target(target)
    if not worker_id.strip():
        raise ValueError("worker_id 不能为空")
    if connector_is_paused(
        db,
        category_key=target.category_key,
        mechanism_key="",
        contract_key=target.contract_key,
    ):
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_connector_paused",
            "该下游接口已暂停交付",
        )
    from .incremental_routing import fence_outbox_payload
    from .workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
    excluded_versions = set(paused_workflow_version_ids(db,
        category_key=target.category_key, contract_key=target.contract_key))
    blocked_ids: list[int] = []

    def quarantine(event, error):
        fail_outbox_event(db, event.id, event.lease_token,
            _failure(error_code=error.code, retryable=False), now=now)
        mark_downstream_publish_outcome(db, outbox_event_id=event.id,
            delivery_status="rejected", rejection_code=error.code, now=now)
        _audit_attempt(db, event, target, worker_id=worker_id,
            action="delivery_fenced", details={"error_code": error.code,
                "resulting_status": "dead_lettered", "formal_write_performed": False})
        blocked_ids.append(event.id)
        db.flush()

    candidates = eligible_outbox_events(db, now=now, limit=target.batch_size,
        target_contract_key=target.contract_key, target_contract_version_id=target.id,
        excluded_mechanism_version_ids=excluded_versions)
    safe_ids: set[int] = set()
    for candidate in candidates:
        try:
            fence_outbox_payload(db, load_snapshot_blob(candidate.payload_blob))
        except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
            event = claim_outbox_event(db, worker_id, now=now,
                lease_seconds=_delivery_lease_seconds(target), only_event_ids={candidate.id},
                excluded_mechanism_version_ids=excluded_versions)
            if event is not None:
                quarantine(event, exc)
        else:
            safe_ids.add(candidate.id)
    if not safe_ids:
        return DeliveryBatchResult(target.id, (), (), (), tuple(blocked_ids))
    try:
        evidence = adapter.verify_target()
        if not evidence.least_privileged:
            raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                "downstream_permission_overbroad",
                "下游账号权限超出合同范围",
            )
        if evidence.schema_fingerprint != target.schema_fingerprint:
            raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                "downstream_schema_drift",
                "下游结构与冻结合同不一致",
            )
    except ConnectorDeliveryError as exc:
        if not blocked_ids:
            raise
        # Preserve the quarantine in the caller's commit. Healthy rows remain
        # unclaimed; the next tick reports the target error independently.
        for candidate in candidates:
            if candidate.id in blocked_ids:
                _audit_attempt(db, candidate, target, worker_id=worker_id,
                    action="delivery_target_blocked", details={"error_code": exc.code})
        db.flush()
        return DeliveryBatchResult(target.id, (), (), (), tuple(blocked_ids))

    excluded_versions = set(
        paused_workflow_version_ids(
            db,
            category_key=target.category_key,
            contract_key=target.contract_key,
        )
    )
    claimed: list[RuntimeOutboxEvent] = []
    for _ in range(target.batch_size):
        event = claim_outbox_event(
            db,
            worker_id,
            now=now,
            lease_seconds=_delivery_lease_seconds(target),
            target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
            target_contract_version_id=target.id,
            excluded_mechanism_version_ids=excluded_versions,
            only_event_ids=safe_ids,
        )
        if event is None:
            break
        mechanism_key = f"workflow-version:{event.mechanism_version_id}"
        if connector_is_paused(
            db,
            category_key=target.category_key,
            mechanism_key=mechanism_key,
            contract_key=target.contract_key,
        ):
            lease_token = event.lease_token
            if lease_token is None:
                raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                    "downstream_lease_lost",
                    "下游交付租约丢失",
                )
            release_outbox_claim(db, event.id, lease_token, now=now)
            excluded_versions.add(event.mechanism_version_id)
            continue
        claimed.append(event)
    if not claimed:
        return DeliveryBatchResult(target.id, (), (), (), tuple(blocked_ids))

    deliverable: list[RuntimeOutboxEvent] = []
    for event in claimed:
        if connector_is_paused(
            db,
            category_key=target.category_key,
            mechanism_key=f"workflow-version:{event.mechanism_version_id}",
            contract_key=target.contract_key,
        ):
            lease_token = event.lease_token
            if lease_token is None:
                raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                    "downstream_lease_lost",
                    "下游交付租约丢失",
                )
            release_outbox_claim(db, event.id, lease_token, now=now)
            continue
        deliverable.append(event)
    claimed = deliverable
    if not claimed:
        return DeliveryBatchResult(target.id, (), (), (), tuple(blocked_ids))

    items = tuple(
        DeliveryItem(
            event_id=event.id,
            key=_key(event),
            payload=load_snapshot_blob(event.payload_blob),
        )
        for event in claimed
    )
    safe_items = []
    for event, item in zip(claimed, items):
        try:
            fence_outbox_payload(db, item.payload)
        except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
            quarantine(event, exc)
        else:
            safe_items.append(item)
    items = tuple(safe_items)
    safe_claimed_ids = {item.event_id for item in items}
    claimed = [event for event in claimed if event.id in safe_claimed_ids]
    if not claimed:
        return DeliveryBatchResult(target.id, (), (), (), tuple(blocked_ids))
    batch = DeliveryBatch(
        target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
        contract_version=target.version,
        items=items,
    )
    try:
        attempt = adapter.deliver(batch)
    except Exception:
        attempt = DeliveryAttempt(
            results=tuple(
                DeliveryWriteResult(
                    key=item.key,
                    succeeded=False,
                    error_code="downstream_adapter_unavailable",
                    retryable=True,
                )
                for item in items
            )
        )

    results_by_key: dict[str, DeliveryWriteResult] = {}
    for result in attempt.results:
        business_key = result.key.business_idempotency_key
        if business_key in results_by_key:
            raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                "downstream_adapter_contract_invalid",
                "下游适配器返回了重复业务键",
            )
        results_by_key[business_key] = result
    successful_keys = [
        item.key
        for item in items
        if (
            results_by_key.get(item.key.business_idempotency_key) is not None
            and results_by_key[item.key.business_idempotency_key].succeeded
        )
    ]
    readback_error: str | None = None
    try:
        readback = adapter.read_back(successful_keys)
    except Exception as exc:
        readback = ()
        readback_error = type(exc).__name__
    readback_by_key = {
        row.key.business_idempotency_key: row for row in readback
    }

    delivered_ids: list[int] = []
    retry_ids: list[int] = []
    for event in claimed:
        lease_token = event.lease_token
        if lease_token is None:
            raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                "downstream_lease_lost",
                "下游交付租约丢失",
            )
        key = _key(event)
        result = results_by_key.get(key.business_idempotency_key)
        row = readback_by_key.get(key.business_idempotency_key)
        matched = row is not None and row.key == key and bool(
            row.external_record_key.strip()
        )
        if result is not None and result.succeeded and matched:
            complete_outbox_event(
                db,
                event.id,
                lease_token,
                DeliveryReceipt(
                    business_idempotency_key=key.business_idempotency_key,
                    mechanism_version_id=key.mechanism_version_id,
                    payload_hash=key.payload_hash,
                    external_record_key=row.external_record_key,
                    revision=key.revision,
                    review_state=key.review_state,
                ),
                now=now,
            )
            delivered_ids.append(event.id)
            mark_downstream_publish_outcome(
                db,
                outbox_event_id=event.id,
                delivery_status="delivered",
                external_record_key=row.external_record_key,
                now=now,
            )
            _audit_attempt(
                db,
                event,
                target,
                worker_id=worker_id,
                action="delivery_succeeded",
                details={"read_back_matched": True},
            )
            continue

        retry_after = None
        if result is None:
            error_code = "downstream_adapter_contract_invalid"
            retryable = False
        elif result.succeeded:
            # 写入成功但读回没确认：下游刚写入、读回还看不到，或读回调用本身失败。
            # 10-01 事件 71215：工厂同一秒写入，读回没看到就进了死信，重放后立刻确认。
            # 按投递重试，次数受 max_attempts 约束；重试由适配器的幂等保证：灵感图先读后写，
            # 日志已有同一提交时只会确认、尚未确认（工厂未落定，只读再等）或报 already_sent，
            # 不会再提交；3DSU 由服务端去重。
            error_code = "downstream_readback_mismatch"
            retryable = True
            retry_after = READBACK_CONFIRM_DELAY_SECONDS
        else:
            error_code = result.error_code or "downstream_delivery_failed"
            retryable = result.retryable
            retry_after = result.retry_after_seconds
        failed = fail_outbox_event(
            db,
            event.id,
            lease_token,
            _failure(error_code=error_code, retryable=retryable, retry_after_seconds=retry_after),
            now=now,
        )
        if failed.status == "queued":
            retry_ids.append(event.id)
            mark_downstream_publish_outcome(
                db,
                outbox_event_id=event.id,
                delivery_status="retrying",
                rejection_code=error_code,
                now=now,
            )
        else:
            blocked_ids.append(event.id)
            mark_downstream_publish_outcome(
                db,
                outbox_event_id=event.id,
                delivery_status=(
                    "rejected"
                    if error_code in OVERWRITE_REJECTION_CODES
                    or error_code == "downstream_identity_conflict"
                    else "dead_lettered"
                ),
                rejection_code=error_code,
                now=now,
            )
        _audit_attempt(
            db,
            event,
            target,
            worker_id=worker_id,
            action="delivery_failed",
            details={
                "error_code": error_code,
                "retryable": retryable,
                "resulting_status": failed.status,
                # 2026-09-20：工厂连着拒了几十条，我们只记下「未通过」，连它自己
                # 写的那句原因都没留，只能靠猜。带上它，下次点开审计就知道为什么。
                **({"upstream_message": result.diagnostic}
                   if result is not None and result.diagnostic else {}),
                **({"read_back_error": readback_error}
                   if readback_error and error_code == "downstream_readback_mismatch" else {}),
            },
        )
    db.flush()
    return DeliveryBatchResult(
        target_contract_id=target.id,
        claimed_event_ids=tuple(event.id for event in claimed),
        delivered_event_ids=tuple(delivered_ids),
        retry_event_ids=tuple(retry_ids),
        blocked_event_ids=tuple(blocked_ids),
    )


def discard_dead_letter_delivery(
    db: Session,
    event_id: int,
    *,
    actor: str,
    reason: str,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> RuntimeOutboxEvent:
    """人工放弃一条死信：不再投递，事件转 ``canceled``，台账记 ``rejected``。

    2026-09-16 老大裁决：任务 258–347 的 12 条死信（factory_submit_http_404 / transport_failed，
    2026-09-15 10:53–11:31）直接抛弃。此前没有"放弃"路径，只有重放；这 12 条卡着
    ``verify-operations`` 的 ``operations_critical``，让每一轮发布都在装 runtime.env 前停下。
    放弃是状态推进，不改历史：attempt_count、last_error 的来龙去脉都留着，只把最终态定为
    canceled 并写审计。
    """
    normalized_actor = actor.strip()
    normalized_reason = reason.strip()
    if not normalized_actor or not normalized_reason:
        raise ValueError("放弃死信必须填写操作者和原因")
    event = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
        .where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id == event_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if event is None:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_event_unknown",
            "下游交付事件不存在",
        )
    if event.status != "dead_lettered":
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_discard_state_invalid",
            "只有死信事件可以人工放弃",
        )
    current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    previous_error = event.last_error_code
    event.status = "canceled"
    event.last_error_code = "operator_discarded"
    event.last_error_message = normalized_reason[:500]
    event.updated_at = current
    mark_downstream_publish_outcome(
        db,
        outbox_event_id=event.id,
        delivery_status="rejected",
        rejection_code="operator_discarded",
        now=current,
    )
    details = {
        "reason": normalized_reason,
        "previous_error_code": previous_error,
        "attempt_count": event.attempt_count,
        "dead_lettered_at": platform_isoformat(event.dead_lettered_at) if event.dead_lettered_at else None,
    }
    target = (
        db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, event.target_contract_version_id)
        if event.target_contract_version_id is not None
        else None
    )
    if target is not None:
        _audit_attempt(
            db, event, target,
            worker_id=normalized_actor, action="dead_letter_discarded", details=details,
        )
    else:
        db.add(
            RuntimeAuditEvent(
                event_key=f"outbox:{event.id}:attempt:{event.attempt_count}:dead_letter_discarded",
                entity_type="runtime_outbox_event",
                entity_key=str(event.id),
                action="dead_letter_discarded",
                actor=normalized_actor[:80],
                details_json=canonical_json({
                    "business_idempotency_key": event.business_idempotency_key,
                    "mechanism_version_id": event.mechanism_version_id,
                    "payload_hash": event.payload_hash,
                    **details,
                }),
            )
        )
    db.flush()
    return event


def replay_dead_letter_delivery(
    db: Session,
    event_id: int,
    *,
    actor: str,
    reason: str,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> RuntimeOutboxEvent:
    normalized_actor = actor.strip()
    normalized_reason = reason.strip()
    if not normalized_actor or not normalized_reason:
        raise ValueError("死信重放必须填写操作者和原因")
    event = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
        .where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id == event_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if event is None:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_event_unknown",
            "下游交付事件不存在",
        )
    if event.status != "dead_lettered":
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_replay_state_invalid",
            "只有死信事件可以人工重放",
        )
    if event.attempt_count >= 100:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_replay_limit_reached",
            "下游交付已达到绝对尝试上限",
        )
    target = (
        db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, event.target_contract_version_id)
        if event.target_contract_version_id is not None
        else None
    )
    if target is None:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_target_contract_missing",
            "死信事件缺少冻结的下游合同版本",
        )
    previous_error = event.last_error_code
    previous_max_attempts = event.max_attempts
    event.max_attempts = max(event.max_attempts, event.attempt_count + 1)
    event.status = "queued"
    event.available_at = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    event.dead_lettered_at = None
    _audit_attempt(
        db,
        event,
        target,
        worker_id=normalized_actor,
        action="dead_letter_replayed",
        details={
            "reason": normalized_reason,
            "previous_error_code": previous_error,
            "previous_max_attempts": previous_max_attempts,
            "next_max_attempts": event.max_attempts,
        },
    )
    db.flush()
    return event

===== FILE backend/app/connector_governance.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import (
    ConnectorContractVersion,
    ConnectorPauseState,
    RuntimeAuditEvent,
    UpstreamIntakeEvent,
)


class ConnectorGovernanceError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        status_code: int = 409,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


def _required_text(value: str, *, label: str, maximum: int) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip()
    if not normalized:
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "connector_governance_request_invalid",
            f"{label}不能为空",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if len(normalized) > maximum:
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "connector_governance_request_invalid",
            f"{label}过长",
            status_code=422,
        )
    lowered = normalized.lower()
    if any(
        marker in lowered
        for marker in ("://", "password", "token=", "secret=", "api_key")
    ):
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "connector_governance_sensitive_text",
            f"{label}不得包含连接地址或敏感信息",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return normalized


def connector_pause_state(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    mechanism_key: str,
    contract_key: str,
) -> ConnectorPauseState | None:
    """Resolve an exact pause first and then the whole-contract pause."""

    candidates = db.scalars(
        select(ConnectorPauseState).where(
            ConnectorPauseState.category_key == category_key,
            ConnectorPauseState.target_contract_key.in_({"*", contract_key}),
            ConnectorPauseState.mechanism_key.in_({"", mechanism_key}),
        )
    ).all()
    return min(
        candidates,
        key=lambda row: (
            row.state != "paused",
            row.mechanism_key != mechanism_key,
            row.target_contract_key != contract_key,
            row.id,
        ),
        default=None,
    )


def connector_is_paused(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    mechanism_key: str,
    contract_key: str,
) -> bool:
    states = db.scalars(
        select(ConnectorPauseState).where(
            ConnectorPauseState.category_key == category_key,
            ConnectorPauseState.target_contract_key.in_({"*", contract_key}),
            ConnectorPauseState.mechanism_key.in_({"", mechanism_key}),
            ConnectorPauseState.state == "paused",
        )
    ).all()
    return bool(states)


def paused_workflow_version_ids(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    contract_key: str,
) -> frozenset[int]:
    """Return frozen workflow versions paused for this downstream target."""

    rows = db.scalars(
        select(ConnectorPauseState).where(
            ConnectorPauseState.category_key == category_key,
            ConnectorPauseState.target_contract_key.in_({"*", contract_key}),
            ConnectorPauseState.state == "paused",
            ConnectorPauseState.mechanism_key.like("workflow-version:%"),
        )
    ).all()
    values: set[int] = set()
    for row in rows:
        _, _, raw_version = row.mechanism_key.partition(":")
        try:
            version_id = int(raw_version)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if version_id > 0:
            values.add(version_id)
    return frozenset(values)


def set_connector_pause_state(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    mechanism_key: str,
    contract_key: str,
    state: str,
    reason: str,
    expected_revision: int | None,
    actor: str,
) -> ConnectorPauseState:
    normalized_category = _required_text(
        category_key,
        label="类目",
        maximum=40,
    )
    normalized_contract = _required_text(
        contract_key,
        label="接口",
        maximum=120,
    )
    normalized_mechanism = mechanism_key.strip()
    if len(normalized_mechanism) > 200:
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "connector_governance_request_invalid",
            "评测机制标识过长",
            status_code=422,
        )
    normalized_reason = _required_text(reason, label="操作原因", maximum=500)
    normalized_actor = _required_text(actor, label="操作人", maximum=80)
    if state not in {"active", "paused"}:
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "connector_pause_state_invalid",
            "接口状态无效",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if isinstance(expected_revision, bool) or (
        expected_revision is not None and expected_revision < 1
    ):
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "connector_pause_revision_invalid",
            "接口状态版本无效",
            status_code=422,
        )

    row = db.scalar(
        select(ConnectorPauseState)
        .where(
            ConnectorPauseState.category_key == normalized_category,
            ConnectorPauseState.mechanism_key == normalized_mechanism,
            ConnectorPauseState.target_contract_key == normalized_contract,
        )
        .with_for_update()
    )
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if row is None:
        if state != "paused" or expected_revision is not None:
            raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
                "connector_pause_revision_conflict",
                "接口状态已经变化，请刷新后重试",
            )
        row = ConnectorPauseState(
            category_key=normalized_category,
            mechanism_key=normalized_mechanism,
            target_contract_key=normalized_contract,
            state="paused",
            reason_code="manual_pause",
            paused_by=normalized_actor,
            paused_at=now,
            revision=1,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
    else:
        if expected_revision != row.revision:
            raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
                "connector_pause_revision_conflict",
                "接口状态已经变化，请刷新后重试",
            )
        if row.state == state:
            return row
        row.state = state
        row.revision += 1
        if state == "paused":
            row.reason_code = "manual_pause"
            row.paused_by = normalized_actor
            row.paused_at = now
        else:
            row.resumed_by = normalized_actor
            row.resume_reason = normalized_reason
            row.resumed_at = now
        db.flush()

    db.add(
        RuntimeAuditEvent(
            event_key=f"connector-pause:{row.id}:{row.revision}",
            entity_type="connector_contract",
            entity_key=normalized_contract,
            action=(
                "connector_paused" if state == "paused" else "connector_resumed"
            ),
            actor=normalized_actor,
            details_json=canonical_json(
                {
                    "category_key": normalized_category,
                    "mechanism_key": normalized_mechanism,
                    "reason": normalized_reason,
                    "revision": row.revision,
                }
            ),
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return row


def replay_upstream_intake_event(
    db: Session,
    *,
    event_id: int,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
) -> UpstreamIntakeEvent:
    normalized_reason = _required_text(reason, label="重放原因", maximum=500)
    normalized_actor = _required_text(actor, label="操作人", maximum=80)
    original = db.scalar(
        select(UpstreamIntakeEvent)
        .where(UpstreamIntakeEvent.id == event_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if original is None:
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "upstream_intake_event_missing",
            "上游接入记录不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if original.production_run_id is not None:
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "upstream_intake_event_already_bound",
            "该接入记录已有运行，请在运行中心重试失败节点",
        )
    contract = db.get(
        ConnectorContractVersion,
        original.contract_version_id,
    )
    if contract is None:
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "upstream_connector_contract_missing",
            "该接入记录对应的接口合同不存在",
        )
    if connector_is_paused(
        db,
        category_key=original.category_key,
        mechanism_key=original.mechanism_key,
        contract_key=contract.contract_key,
    ):
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "upstream_connector_paused",
            "该上游接口已暂停接收，暂不能重放",
        )
    try:
        payload = json.loads(original.payload_json)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "upstream_intake_payload_invalid",
            "上游接入记录内容损坏",
        ) from exc
    payload_json = canonical_json(payload)
    payload_hash = hashlib.sha256(payload_json.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    if not isinstance(payload, dict) or payload_hash != original.payload_hash:
        raise ConnectorGovernanceError(
            "upstream_intake_payload_invalid",
            "上游接入记录内容校验失败",
        )

    replay = UpstreamIntakeEvent(
        contract_version_id=original.contract_version_id,
        source_event_id=f"replay:{original.id}:{uuid4().hex[:16]}",
        source_cursor=original.source_cursor,
        source_occurred_at=original.source_occurred_at,
        category_key=original.category_key,
        mechanism_key=original.mechanism_key,
        payload_hash=original.payload_hash,
        payload_json=original.payload_json,
        disposition="replayed",
        replay_of_id=original.id,
        created_by=normalized_actor,
    )
    db.add(replay)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        RuntimeAuditEvent(
            event_key=f"connector-intake-replay:{replay.id}",
            entity_type="upstream_intake_event",
            entity_key=str(replay.id),
            action="upstream_event_replayed",
            actor=normalized_actor,
            details_json=canonical_json(
                {
                    "contract_version_id": original.contract_version_id,
                    "original_event_id": original.id,
                    "reason": normalized_reason,
                    "replay_event_id": replay.id,
                }
            ),
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return replay

===== FILE backend/app/connector_governance_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Any, Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .authz import has_permission
from .connector_contracts import (
    ConnectorContractError,
    create_connector_contract_version,
)
from .connector_delivery import resolve_configured_downstream_adapter
from .connector_governance import (
    ConnectorGovernanceError,
    connector_pause_state,
    replay_upstream_intake_event,
    set_connector_pause_state,
)
from .database import get_db
from .models import ConnectorContractVersion, ConnectorPauseState, User
from .readonly_sources import resolve_configured_connector_poll_adapter


class ConnectorContractFields(BaseModel):
    direction: Literal["upstream", "downstream"]
    category_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    logical_locator: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    adapter_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    # Optional: for adapters whose digest the platform can supply (a constant,
    # or a registration convention) the operator must not be asked to type a
    # 64-hex value. Adapters that read a live schema still require it, and the
    # probe endpoint is where that digest is confirmed against the real table.
    schema_fingerprint: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=64, max_length=64)
    field_mapping: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    cursor_contract: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    idempotency_contract: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    batch_size: int = Field(default=100, ge=1, le=1000)
    timeout_seconds: int = Field(default=30, ge=1, le=600)
    owner: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    status: Literal["draft", "active", "retired"] = "draft"


class ConnectorContractCreate(ConnectorContractFields):
    contract_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)


class ConnectorPauseRequest(BaseModel):
    category_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    mechanism_key: str = Field(default="", max_length=200)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)
    expected_revision: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)


class ConnectorReplayRequest(BaseModel):
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)


def _resolved_fingerprint(payload: "ConnectorContractFields") -> str:
    from .connector_adapter_registry import adapter, derive_fingerprint
    if payload.schema_fingerprint:
        return payload.schema_fingerprint
    supplied = derive_fingerprint(payload.adapter_key, payload.logical_locator)
    if supplied:
        return supplied
    known = adapter(payload.adapter_key)
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=422,
        detail={
            "code": "connector_contract_fingerprint_required",
            "message": (
                "该适配器的结构指纹要从真实表结构读取，请由研发提供后再登记"
                if known is not None else "未知适配器，请从可选列表中选择"
            ),
        },
    )


def _actor(user: User) -> str:
    return str(user.username or user.display_name or "connector-operator")[:80]


def _require_admin(user: User) -> None:
    if not user.is_admin:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403,
            detail={
                "code": "connector_governance_admin_required",
                "message": "只有系统管理员可以新增或探测接口合同",
            },
        )


def _require_operator(user: User) -> None:
    if not has_permission(user, "workflows:write"):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403,
            detail={
                "code": "connector_governance_write_forbidden",
                "message": "当前账号不能暂停、恢复或重放接口任务",
            },
        )


def _json_object(raw: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(raw or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return {}
    return value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}


def _pause_payload(row: ConnectorPauseState | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if row is None:
        return None
    return {
        "category_key": row.category_key,
        "mechanism_key": row.mechanism_key,
        "contract_key": row.target_contract_key,
        "state": row.state,
        "revision": row.revision,
        "reason_code": row.reason_code,
        "paused_by": row.paused_by,
        "paused_at": row.paused_at,
        "resumed_by": row.resumed_by,
        "resumed_at": row.resumed_at,
    }


def _contract_payload(
    row: ConnectorContractVersion,
    *,
    pause: ConnectorPauseState | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "contract_key": row.contract_key,
        "version": row.version,
        "direction": row.direction,
        "category_key": row.category_key,
        "logical_locator": row.logical_locator,
        "adapter_key": row.adapter_key,
        "schema_fingerprint": row.schema_fingerprint,
        "field_mapping": _json_object(row.field_mapping_json),
        "cursor_contract": _json_object(row.cursor_contract_json),
        "idempotency_contract": _json_object(row.idempotency_contract_json),
        "batch_size": row.batch_size,
        "timeout_seconds": row.timeout_seconds,
        "owner": row.owner,
        "status": row.status,
        "contract_hash": row.contract_hash,
        "created_by": row.created_by,
        "created_at": row.created_at,
        "structure_check": "passed",
        "pause": _pause_payload(pause),
    }


def _governance_error(exc: ConnectorGovernanceError) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(
        status_code=exc.status_code,
        detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)},
    )


def _create_contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract_key: str,
    payload: ConnectorContractFields,
    actor: str,
) -> ConnectorContractVersion:
    try:
        return create_connector_contract_version(
            db,
            contract_key=contract_key,
            direction=payload.direction,
            category_key=payload.category_key,
            logical_locator=payload.logical_locator,
            adapter_key=payload.adapter_key,
            schema_fingerprint=_resolved_fingerprint(payload),
            field_mapping=payload.field_mapping,
            cursor_contract=payload.cursor_contract,
            idempotency_contract=payload.idempotency_contract,
            batch_size=payload.batch_size,
            timeout_seconds=payload.timeout_seconds,
            owner=payload.owner,
            status=payload.status,
            created_by=actor,
        )
