    except ConnectorContractError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=422,
            detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)},
        ) from exc


def build_connector_governance_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/connector-governance",
        tags=["connector-governance"],
    )

    @router.get("/adapters")
    def list_adapters(_user: User = Depends(require_user)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        from .connector_adapter_registry import as_payload
        return {"items": as_payload()}

    @router.get("/contracts")
    def list_contracts(
        category_key: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=40),
        direction: Literal["upstream", "downstream"] | None = None,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        statement = select(ConnectorContractVersion)
        if category_key:
            statement = statement.where(
                ConnectorContractVersion.category_key == category_key
            )
        if direction:
            statement = statement.where(
                ConnectorContractVersion.direction == direction
            )
        rows = db.scalars(
            statement.order_by(
                ConnectorContractVersion.contract_key.asc(),
                ConnectorContractVersion.version.desc(),
            )
        ).all()
        return {
            "items": [
                _contract_payload(
                    row,
                    pause=connector_pause_state(
                        db,
                        category_key=row.category_key,
                        mechanism_key="",
                        contract_key=row.contract_key,
                    ),
                )
                for row in rows
            ]
        }

    @router.post(
        "/contracts",
        status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    )
    def create_contract(
        payload: ConnectorContractCreate,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_admin(user)
        row = _create_contract(
            db,
            contract_key=payload.contract_key,
            payload=payload,
            actor=_actor(user),
        )
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(row)
        return _contract_payload(row)

    @router.post(
        "/contracts/{contract_key}/versions",
        status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    )
    def create_contract_version(
        contract_key: str,
        payload: ConnectorContractFields,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_admin(user)
        latest = db.scalar(
            select(ConnectorContractVersion)
            .where(ConnectorContractVersion.contract_key == contract_key)
            .order_by(ConnectorContractVersion.version.desc())
            .limit(1)
        )
        if latest is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="接口合同不存在")
        if (
            latest.category_key != payload.category_key
            or latest.direction != payload.direction
        ):
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail={
                    "code": "connector_contract_identity_mismatch",
                    "message": "同一接口的类目和方向不能在新版本中改变",
                },
            )
        row = _create_contract(
            db,
            contract_key=contract_key,
            payload=payload,
            actor=_actor(user),
        )
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(row)
        return _contract_payload(row)

    @router.post("/contracts/{contract_id}/probe")
    def probe_contract(
        contract_id: int,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_admin(user)
        contract = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, contract_id)
        if contract is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="接口合同不存在")
        try:
            if contract.direction == "upstream":
                evidence = resolve_configured_connector_poll_adapter(
                    contract
                ).verify_read_only()
                passed = (
                    evidence.read_only
                    and evidence.schema_fingerprint == contract.schema_fingerprint
                )
            else:
                evidence = resolve_configured_downstream_adapter(
                    contract
                ).verify_target()
                passed = (
                    evidence.least_privileged
                    and evidence.schema_fingerprint == contract.schema_fingerprint
                )
            if not passed:
                raise RuntimeError("connector safety evidence mismatch")
        except Exception as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail={
                    "code": "connector_probe_failed",
                    "message": "接口尚未通过权限与结构检查，请联系研发处理",
                },
            ) from exc
        return {
            "contract_id": contract.id,
            "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
            "contract_version": contract.version,
            "ready": True,
            "structure_check": "passed",
            "permission_check": "passed",
        }

    def change_pause_state(
        *,
        contract_key: str,
        payload: ConnectorPauseRequest,
        state: str,
        user: User,
        db: Session,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_operator(user)
        contract_exists = db.scalar(
            select(ConnectorContractVersion.id).where(
                ConnectorContractVersion.contract_key == contract_key,
                ConnectorContractVersion.category_key == payload.category_key,
            )
        )
        if contract_exists is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="接口合同不存在")
        try:
            row = set_connector_pause_state(
                db,
                category_key=payload.category_key,
                mechanism_key=payload.mechanism_key,
                contract_key=contract_key,
                state=state,
                reason=payload.reason,
                expected_revision=payload.expected_revision,
                actor=_actor(user),
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
        except ConnectorGovernanceError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _governance_error(exc) from exc
        result = _pause_payload(row)
        assert result is not None
        return result

    @router.put("/contracts/{contract_key}/pause")
    def pause_contract(
        contract_key: str,
        payload: ConnectorPauseRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return change_pause_state(
            contract_key=contract_key,
            payload=payload,
            state="paused",
            user=user,
            db=db,
        )

    @router.put("/contracts/{contract_key}/resume")
    def resume_contract(
        contract_key: str,
        payload: ConnectorPauseRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return change_pause_state(
            contract_key=contract_key,
            payload=payload,
            state="active",
            user=user,
            db=db,
        )

    @router.post(
        "/intake-events/{event_id}/replay",
        status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    )
    def replay_event(
        event_id: int,
        payload: ConnectorReplayRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_operator(user)
        try:
            row = replay_upstream_intake_event(
                db,
                event_id=event_id,
                reason=payload.reason,
                actor=_actor(user),
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
        except ConnectorGovernanceError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _governance_error(exc) from exc
        return {
            "id": row.id,
            "contract_version_id": row.contract_version_id,
            "source_event_id": row.source_event_id,
            "category_key": row.category_key,
            "mechanism_key": row.mechanism_key,
            "disposition": row.disposition,
            "replay_of_id": row.replay_of_id,
            "received_at": row.received_at,
        }

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/connector_intake.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Protocol

from sqlalchemy import BigInteger, cast, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .connector_governance import connector_is_paused
from .models import ConnectorContractVersion, UpstreamIntakeEvent


class ConnectorIntakeError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        status_code: int = 400,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class IntakeRequest:
    source_event_id: str
    source_cursor: str
    source_occurred_at: datetime | None
    category_key: str
    mechanism_key: str
    payload: Mapping[str, Any]
    actor: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class IntakeDecision:
    event: UpstreamIntakeEvent
    disposition: str
    idempotent_replay: bool
    out_of_order: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PollRecord:
    source_event_id: str
    source_cursor: str
    source_occurred_at: datetime | None
    payload: Mapping[str, Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PollSafetyEvidence:
    read_only: bool
    schema_fingerprint: str
    verifier: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PollPage:
    records: tuple[PollRecord, ...]
    schema_fingerprint: str


class ConnectorPollAdapter(Protocol):
    def verify_read_only(self) -> PollSafetyEvidence: ...

    def fetch_page(self, *, after_cursor: str, limit: int) -> PollPage: ...


class FixtureConnectorPollAdapter:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        read_only: bool,
        schema_fingerprint: str,
        records: Sequence[PollRecord],
    ) -> None:
        self._read_only = read_only
        self._schema_fingerprint = schema_fingerprint
        self._records = tuple(records)
        self.verify_count = 0
        self.fetch_count = 0

    def verify_read_only(self) -> PollSafetyEvidence:
        self.verify_count += 1
        return PollSafetyEvidence(
            read_only=self._read_only,
            schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint,
            verifier="fixture-explicit-read-only",
        )

    def fetch_page(self, *, after_cursor: str, limit: int) -> PollPage:
        self.fetch_count += 1
        return PollPage(
            records=self._records[:limit],
            schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint,
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PollResult:
    contract_id: int
    requested_after_cursor: str
    event_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    accepted_count: int
    delayed_count: int
    rejected_count: int
    duplicate_count: int


def _required(value: str, *, label: str, maximum: int) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip()
    if not normalized:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_request_invalid",
            f"{label}不能为空",
        )
    if len(normalized) > maximum:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_request_invalid",
            f"{label}过长",
        )
    return normalized


def _payload(value: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, str]:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_payload_invalid",
            "事件内容必须是对象",
        )
    try:
        serialized = canonical_json(dict(value))
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_payload_invalid",
            "事件内容无法转换为标准 JSON",
        ) from exc
    return serialized, hashlib.sha256(serialized.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _cursor_contract(contract: ConnectorContractVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(contract.cursor_contract_json)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_cursor_contract_invalid",
            "上游合同的游标规则损坏",
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_cursor_contract_invalid",
            "上游合同的游标规则不是对象",
            status_code=409,
        )
    kind = value.get("kind", "text")
    ordering = value.get("ordering", "ascending")
    policy = value.get("out_of_order", "delay")
    if kind not in {"integer", "text"}:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_cursor_contract_invalid",
            "上游合同的游标类型不受支持",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if ordering not in {"ascending", "descending"}:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_cursor_contract_invalid",
            "上游合同的游标排序不受支持",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if policy not in {"accept", "delay", "reject"}:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_cursor_contract_invalid",
            "上游合同的乱序策略不受支持",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return value


def _cursor_value(raw: str, *, kind: str) -> int | str:
    normalized = raw.strip()
    if not normalized:
        return ""
    if kind == "integer":
        try:
            return int(normalized)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise ConnectorIntakeError(
                "intake_cursor_invalid",
                "事件游标必须是整数",
            ) from exc
    return normalized


def _watermark(
    db: Session,
    contract: ConnectorContractVersion,
    *,
    kind: str,
    ordering: str,
) -> int | str | None:
    cursor_column = UpstreamIntakeEvent.source_cursor
    aggregate = func.max if ordering == "ascending" else func.min
    value_expression = (
        cast(cursor_column, BigInteger) if kind == "integer" else cursor_column
    )
    value = db.scalar(
        select(aggregate(value_expression)).where(
            UpstreamIntakeEvent.contract_version_id == contract.id,
            UpstreamIntakeEvent.disposition.in_({"accepted", "replayed"}),
            UpstreamIntakeEvent.source_cursor != "",
        )
    )
    if value is None:
        return None
    return int(value) if kind == "integer" else str(value)


def _is_out_of_order(
    cursor: int | str,
    watermark: int | str | None,
    *,
    ordering: str,
) -> bool:
    if cursor == "" or watermark is None:
        return False
    if ordering == "ascending":
        return cursor <= watermark
    return cursor >= watermark


def _active_upstream_contract(
    contract: ConnectorContractVersion | None,
) -> ConnectorContractVersion:
    if contract is None:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "upstream_contract_unknown",
            "上游接入合同不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if contract.direction != "upstream":
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "upstream_contract_direction_invalid",
            "所选合同不是上游接入合同",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if contract.status != "active":
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "upstream_contract_inactive",
            "上游接入合同未启用",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return contract


def ingest_upstream_event(
    db: Session,
    contract: ConnectorContractVersion,
    request: IntakeRequest,
) -> IntakeDecision:
    contract = _active_upstream_contract(contract)
    event_id = _required(request.source_event_id, label="事件编号", maximum=200)
    actor = _required(request.actor, label="接入方", maximum=80)
    category_key = _required(request.category_key, label="类目", maximum=40)
    mechanism_key = request.mechanism_key.strip()
    if len(mechanism_key) > 200:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_request_invalid",
            "评测机制标识过长",
        )
    if category_key != contract.category_key:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "intake_category_mismatch",
            "事件类目与上游合同不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if connector_is_paused(
        db,
        category_key=category_key,
        mechanism_key=mechanism_key,
        contract_key=contract.contract_key,
    ):
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "upstream_connector_paused",
            "该上游接口已暂停接收新事件",
            status_code=409,
        )
    payload_json, payload_hash = _payload(request.payload)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(UpstreamIntakeEvent).where(
            UpstreamIntakeEvent.contract_version_id == contract.id,
            UpstreamIntakeEvent.source_event_id == event_id,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        if existing.payload_hash != payload_hash:
            raise ConnectorIntakeError(
                "intake_idempotency_conflict",
                "事件编号已存在但内容不同",
                status_code=409,
            )
        return IntakeDecision(
            event=existing,
            disposition=existing.disposition,
            idempotent_replay=True,
            out_of_order=False,
        )

    cursor_rules = _cursor_contract(contract)
    cursor = _cursor_value(
        request.source_cursor,
        kind=str(cursor_rules.get("kind", "text")),
    )
    watermark = _watermark(
        db,
        contract,
        kind=str(cursor_rules.get("kind", "text")),
        ordering=str(cursor_rules.get("ordering", "ascending")),
    )
    out_of_order = _is_out_of_order(
        cursor,
        watermark,
        ordering=str(cursor_rules.get("ordering", "ascending")),
    )
    disposition = "accepted"
    if out_of_order:
        disposition = {
            "accept": "accepted",
            "delay": "delayed",
            "reject": "rejected",
        }[str(cursor_rules.get("out_of_order", "delay"))]

    occurred_at = request.source_occurred_at
    if occurred_at is not None and occurred_at.utcoffset() is None:
        occurred_at = occurred_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    event = UpstreamIntakeEvent(
        contract_version_id=contract.id,
        source_event_id=event_id,
        source_cursor=request.source_cursor.strip(),
        source_occurred_at=occurred_at,
        category_key=category_key,
        mechanism_key=mechanism_key,
        payload_hash=payload_hash,
        payload_json=payload_json,
        disposition=disposition,
        created_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(event)
    db.flush()
    return IntakeDecision(
        event=event,
        disposition=disposition,
        idempotent_replay=False,
        out_of_order=out_of_order,
    )


def poll_connector_contract(
    db: Session,
    contract_id: int,
    *,
    actor: str,
    adapter: ConnectorPollAdapter,
) -> PollResult:
    contract = _active_upstream_contract(
        db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, contract_id)
    )
    evidence = adapter.verify_read_only()
    if not evidence.read_only:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "upstream_not_read_only",
            "上游连接无法证明为只读",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if evidence.schema_fingerprint != contract.schema_fingerprint:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "upstream_schema_drift",
            "上游结构与冻结合同不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    cursor_rules = _cursor_contract(contract)
    watermark = _watermark(
        db,
        contract,
        kind=str(cursor_rules.get("kind", "text")),
        ordering=str(cursor_rules.get("ordering", "ascending")),
    )
    after_cursor = "" if watermark is None else str(watermark)
    page = adapter.fetch_page(
        after_cursor=after_cursor,
        limit=contract.batch_size,
    )
    if page.schema_fingerprint != contract.schema_fingerprint:
        raise ConnectorIntakeError(
            "upstream_schema_drift",
            "上游读取期间检测到结构变化",
            status_code=409,
        )

    decisions = [
        ingest_upstream_event(
            db,
            contract,
            IntakeRequest(
                source_event_id=record.source_event_id,
                source_cursor=record.source_cursor,
                source_occurred_at=record.source_occurred_at,
                category_key=contract.category_key,
                mechanism_key="",
                payload=record.payload,
                actor=actor,
            ),
        )
        for record in page.records
    ]
    return PollResult(
        contract_id=contract.id,
        requested_after_cursor=after_cursor,
        event_ids=tuple(decision.event.id for decision in decisions),
        accepted_count=sum(
            decision.disposition == "accepted" for decision in decisions
        ),
        delayed_count=sum(
            decision.disposition == "delayed" for decision in decisions
        ),
        rejected_count=sum(
            decision.disposition == "rejected" for decision in decisions
        ),
        duplicate_count=sum(
            decision.idempotent_replay for decision in decisions
        ),
    )

===== FILE backend/app/connector_intake_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import hmac
import re
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .connector_intake import (
    ConnectorIntakeError,
    IntakeRequest,
    ingest_upstream_event,
    poll_connector_contract,
)
from .database import get_db
from .models import ConnectorContractVersion
from .readonly_sources import (
    ReadOnlySourceError,
    resolve_configured_connector_poll_adapter,
)
from .workflow_external_wait import (
    ExternalResumeRequest,
    ExternalWaitError,
    resolve_external_wait_contract,
    resume_external_wait,
)


_SIGNATURE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
_MAX_TIMESTAMP_SKEW_SECONDS = 300


class ConnectorPushRequest(BaseModel):
    source_event_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    source_cursor: str = Field(default="", max_length=300)
    source_occurred_at: datetime | None = None
    category_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    mechanism_key: str = Field(default="", max_length=200)
    payload: dict[str, Any]


def connector_push_signature(
    *,
    secret: str,
    contract_key: str,
    timestamp: str,
    source_event_id: str,
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> str:
    payload_json = canonical_json(dict(payload))
    payload_hash = hashlib.sha256(payload_json.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    material = "\n".join(
        (
            "connector-push-v1",
            contract_key,
            timestamp,
            source_event_id,
            payload_hash,
        )
    )
    return hmac.new(
        secret.encode("utf-8"),
        material.encode("utf-8"),
        hashlib.sha256,
    ).hexdigest()


def connector_continuation_signature(
    *,
    secret: str,
    correlation_id: str,
    timestamp: str,
    source_event_id: str,
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> str:
    payload_json = canonical_json(dict(payload))
    payload_hash = hashlib.sha256(payload_json.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    material = "\n".join(
        (
            "connector-continuation-v1",
            correlation_id,
            timestamp,
            source_event_id,
            payload_hash,
        )
    )
    return hmac.new(
        secret.encode("utf-8"),
        material.encode("utf-8"),
        hashlib.sha256,
    ).hexdigest()


def _verify_signature(
    *,
    secret: str | None,
    contract_key: str,
    timestamp: str | None,
    signature: str | None,
    payload: ConnectorPushRequest,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> None:
    if secret is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="连接器推送认证未配置")
    try:
        supplied_timestamp = int(timestamp or "")
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="连接器推送认证失败") from exc
    current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if abs(int(current.timestamp()) - supplied_timestamp) > _MAX_TIMESTAMP_SKEW_SECONDS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="连接器推送认证失败")
    normalized_signature = (signature or "").strip().lower()
    if not _SIGNATURE.fullmatch(normalized_signature):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="连接器推送认证失败")
    expected = connector_push_signature(
        secret=secret,
        contract_key=contract_key,
        timestamp=str(supplied_timestamp),
        source_event_id=payload.source_event_id,
        payload=payload.payload,
    )
    if not hmac.compare_digest(normalized_signature, expected):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="连接器推送认证失败")


def _verify_continuation_signature(
    *,
    secret: str | None,
    correlation_id: str,
    timestamp: str | None,
    signature: str | None,
    payload: ConnectorPushRequest,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> None:
    if secret is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="连接器推送认证未配置")
    try:
        supplied_timestamp = int(timestamp or "")
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="连接器推送认证失败") from exc
    current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if abs(int(current.timestamp()) - supplied_timestamp) > _MAX_TIMESTAMP_SKEW_SECONDS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="连接器推送认证失败")
    normalized_signature = (signature or "").strip().lower()
    if not _SIGNATURE.fullmatch(normalized_signature):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="连接器推送认证失败")
    expected = connector_continuation_signature(
        secret=secret,
        correlation_id=correlation_id,
        timestamp=str(supplied_timestamp),
        source_event_id=payload.source_event_id,
        payload=payload.payload,
    )
    if not hmac.compare_digest(normalized_signature, expected):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="连接器推送认证失败")


def _active_contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract_key: str,
) -> ConnectorContractVersion:
    contract = db.scalar(
        select(ConnectorContractVersion)
        .where(
            ConnectorContractVersion.contract_key == contract_key,
            ConnectorContractVersion.direction == "upstream",
            ConnectorContractVersion.status == "active",
        )
        .order_by(ConnectorContractVersion.version.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    if contract is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="上游接入合同不存在或未启用")
    return contract


def _bind_incremental_run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    event_id: int,
    category_key: str,
    actor: str,
    disposition: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把已接收的上游事件绑定成增量运行；未配置机制时只标记待运行。"""
    if disposition not in {"accepted", "replayed"}:
        return {"production_run_id": None, "run_status": "not_runnable"}
    from .incremental_mechanisms import (
        IncrementalMechanismError,
        resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue,
    )
    from .workflow_connector_executor import (
        WorkflowConnectorError,
        create_incremental_run_from_intake,
    )

    try:
        mechanism = resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(db, category_key)
    except IncrementalMechanismError as exc:
        return {
            "production_run_id": None,
            "run_status": "mechanism_not_configured",
            "run_error": exc.code,
        }
    from .incremental_input import incremental_autorun_policy
    snapshot = getattr(mechanism, "workflow_snapshot", None)
    if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) and incremental_autorun_policy(snapshot).get("model_execution_mode") == "real":
        from .connector_native_dispatch import dispatch_real_connector_event
        from .incremental_autorun import IncrementalAutorunError
        from .workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
        try:
            result = dispatch_real_connector_event(db, event_id=event_id, mechanism=mechanism, actor=actor)
            db.commit()
            return result
        except (WorkflowConnectorError, IncrementalAutorunError, WorkflowRuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            return {"production_run_id": None, "run_status": "run_binding_failed",
                    "run_error": getattr(exc, "code", "connector_input_invalid")}
    try:
        run = create_incremental_run_from_intake(
            db,
            event_id=event_id,
            workflow_version_id=mechanism.workflow_version_id,
            actor=actor,
        )
        db.commit()
    except WorkflowConnectorError as exc:
        db.rollback()
        return {
            "production_run_id": None,
            "run_status": "run_binding_failed",
            "run_error": exc.code,
        }
    return {"production_run_id": run.id, "run_status": run.status}


def build_connector_intake_router(
    get_signing_secret: Callable[[], str | None],
    require_operator: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/connectors", tags=["connectors"])

    @router.post("/upstream/{contract_key}/events")
    def push_upstream_event(
        contract_key: str,
        payload: ConnectorPushRequest,
        timestamp: str | None = Header(
            default=None,
            alias="X-Connector-Timestamp",
        ),
        signature: str | None = Header(
            default=None,
            alias="X-Connector-Signature",
        ),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _verify_signature(
            secret=get_signing_secret(),
            contract_key=contract_key,
            timestamp=timestamp,
            signature=signature,
            payload=payload,
        )
        contract = _active_contract(db, contract_key=contract_key)
        try:
            decision = ingest_upstream_event(
                db,
                contract,
                IntakeRequest(
                    source_event_id=payload.source_event_id,
                    source_cursor=payload.source_cursor,
                    source_occurred_at=payload.source_occurred_at,
                    category_key=payload.category_key,
                    mechanism_key=payload.mechanism_key,
                    payload=payload.payload,
                    actor=f"connector:{contract_key}"[:80],
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
        except ConnectorIntakeError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=exc.status_code,
                detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)},
            ) from exc
        # 上游事实已落库；若该类目已启用增量机制，则在同一请求里把事件绑定成一次
        # 冻结工作流运行（此前只落库不建 run，自动链路在这里断掉）。类目未绑定时
        # 事件保留为待运行，不报错——启用机制后由运维重放。
        run_binding = _bind_incremental_run(
            db,
            event_id=decision.event.id,
            category_key=payload.category_key or contract.category_key,
            actor=f"connector:{contract_key}"[:80],
            disposition=decision.disposition,
        )
        return {
            "event_id": decision.event.id,
            "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
            "contract_version": contract.version,
            "disposition": decision.disposition,
            "idempotent_replay": decision.idempotent_replay,
            "out_of_order": decision.out_of_order,
            **run_binding,
            "received_at": decision.event.received_at,
        }

    @router.post("/continuations/{correlation_id}/events")
    def push_continuation_event(
        correlation_id: str,
        payload: ConnectorPushRequest,
        timestamp: str | None = Header(
            default=None,
            alias="X-Connector-Timestamp",
        ),
        signature: str | None = Header(
            default=None,
            alias="X-Connector-Signature",
        ),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _verify_continuation_signature(
            secret=get_signing_secret(),
            correlation_id=correlation_id,
            timestamp=timestamp,
            signature=signature,
            payload=payload,
        )
        try:
            contract = resolve_external_wait_contract(db, correlation_id)
            if contract is None:
                raise ExternalWaitError(
                    "external_wait_not_found",
                    "没有找到对应的外部等待事实",
                    status_code=404,
                )
            decision = ingest_upstream_event(
                db,
                contract,
                IntakeRequest(
                    source_event_id=payload.source_event_id,
                    source_cursor=payload.source_cursor,
                    source_occurred_at=payload.source_occurred_at,
                    category_key=payload.category_key,
                    mechanism_key=payload.mechanism_key,
                    payload=payload.payload,
                    actor=f"continuation:{correlation_id}"[:80],
                ),
            )
            run = resume_external_wait(
                db,
                ExternalResumeRequest(
                    correlation_id=correlation_id,
                    callback_intake_event_id=decision.event.id,
                    callback_payload_hash=decision.event.payload_hash,
                    actor=f"continuation:{correlation_id}"[:80],
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
        except (ConnectorIntakeError, ExternalWaitError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=getattr(exc, "status_code", 409),
                detail={
                    "code": getattr(exc, "code", "continuation_rejected"),
                    "message": "回调未通过合同、幂等或续跑校验",
                },
            ) from exc
        return {
            "schema_version": "connector-continuation-response-v1",
            "correlation_id": correlation_id,
            "event_id": decision.event.id,
            "contract_version_id": contract.id,
            "disposition": decision.disposition,
            "idempotent_replay": decision.idempotent_replay,
            "run_id": run.id,
            "run_status": run.status,
            "received_at": decision.event.received_at,
        }

    @router.post("/upstream/{contract_id}/poll")
    def poll_upstream_contract(
        contract_id: int,
        operator: Any = Depends(require_operator),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        contract = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, contract_id)
        if contract is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="上游接入合同不存在")
        actor = str(
            getattr(operator, "username", None)
            or getattr(operator, "display_name", None)
            or "connector-operator"
        )
        try:
            adapter = resolve_configured_connector_poll_adapter(contract)
            result = poll_connector_contract(
                db,
                contract.id,
                actor=actor,
                adapter=adapter,
            )
            db.commit()
        except (ConnectorIntakeError, ReadOnlySourceError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            status_code = getattr(exc, "status_code", 409)
            code = getattr(exc, "code", "upstream_poll_unavailable")
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status_code,
                detail={
                    "code": code,
                    "message": "上游读取未通过合同或安全检查",
                },
            ) from exc
        return {
            "contract_id": result.contract_id,
            "requested_after_cursor": result.requested_after_cursor,
            "event_count": len(result.event_ids),
            "accepted_count": result.accepted_count,
            "delayed_count": result.delayed_count,
            "rejected_count": result.rejected_count,
            "duplicate_count": result.duplicate_count,
        }

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/connector_native_dispatch.py =====
"""Map signed connector facts into the authoritative content-ingress pipeline."""
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from sqlalchemy import select
from .models import ConnectorContractVersion,UpstreamIntakeEvent,ProductionRun
from .audit import canonical_json
from .workflow_connector_executor import WorkflowConnectorError


def dispatch_real_connector_event(db, *, event_id, mechanism, actor):
    from .incremental_mechanisms import lock_incremental_category, resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue
    from .incremental_automation import automation_paused
    from .incremental_input import incremental_autorun_policy
    candidate = db.get(UpstreamIntakeEvent, event_id)
    if candidate is None:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError('upstream_intake_event_not_runnable', 'Intake event is missing')
    # Match native dispatch order: category, workflow control, then intake.
    lock_incremental_category(db, candidate.category_key)
    mechanism = resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(db, candidate.category_key, locked_refresh=True)
    if incremental_autorun_policy(mechanism.workflow_snapshot).get('model_execution_mode') != 'real':
        raise WorkflowConnectorError('upstream_intake_run_conflict', 'Real binding changed before dispatch')
    automation_paused(db, category_key=candidate.category_key, workflow_version_id=mechanism.workflow_version_id)
    event=db.scalar(select(UpstreamIntakeEvent).where(UpstreamIntakeEvent.id==event_id).with_for_update())
    if event is None or event.disposition not in {'accepted','replayed'}:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError('upstream_intake_event_not_runnable','接入事实尚不可运行')
    if event.production_run_id is not None:
        run=db.get(ProductionRun,event.production_run_id)
        if run is None or run.model_execution_mode!='real':
            raise WorkflowConnectorError('upstream_intake_run_conflict','既有运行不是已授权的真实运行')
        return {'production_run_id':run.id,'run_status':run.status}
    contract=db.get(ConnectorContractVersion,event.contract_version_id)
    snapshot=mechanism.workflow_snapshot
    manifest=snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    upstream=[n for n in manifest.get('nodes',[]) if n.get('kind')=='upstream_connector']
    ids={n.get('config',{}).get('connector_snapshot',{}).get('connector_contract_id') for n in upstream}
    if contract is None or contract.id not in ids or contract.category_key!=event.category_key:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError('upstream_intake_workflow_mismatch','接入事实与冻结上游合同不一致')
    raw=json.loads(event.payload_json)
    if hashlib.sha256(event.payload_json.encode()).hexdigest()!=event.payload_hash:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError('upstream_intake_payload_hash_mismatch','接入事实校验失败')
    mapping=json.loads(contract.field_mapping_json)
    fields=mapping.get('fields',mapping)
    if not isinstance(fields,Mapping):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError('connector_input_mapping_invalid','上游字段映射必须为对象')
    normalized={}
    for key,path in fields.items():
        if not isinstance(path,str) or not path:
            raise WorkflowConnectorError('connector_input_mapping_invalid','上游字段映射必须声明字段路径')
        value=raw
        for part in path.removeprefix('$.').split('.'):
            if not isinstance(value,Mapping) or part not in value:
                value=None;break
            value=value[part]
        if value is not None:normalized[key]=value
    for key in ('content_id','content_version'):
        if not isinstance(normalized.get(key),str) or not normalized[key].strip():
            raise WorkflowConnectorError('connector_input_mapping_incomplete','上游合同必须映射 content_id 和 content_version')
    if normalized.get('category_key',event.category_key)!=event.category_key:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError('intake_category_mismatch','映射类目与合同不一致')
    normalized['category_key']=event.category_key
    from .label_governance import ingest_content_event
    from .incremental_autorun import dispatch_content_event
    event_key='connector-native:'+hashlib.sha256(canonical_json([contract.id,event.source_event_id]).encode()).hexdigest()
    cursor = json.loads(contract.cursor_contract_json or '{}')
    change_order = None
    if cursor.get('kind') == 'integer':
        value = raw
        for part in str(cursor.get('field', '')).removeprefix('$.').split('.'):
            value = value.get(part) if isinstance(value, Mapping) else None
        if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (str, int)) or str(value) != event.source_cursor:
            raise WorkflowConnectorError('connector_signed_cursor_missing', 'Signed cursor must match the accepted cursor')
        sequence = int(value)
        if cursor.get('ordering', 'ascending') == 'descending':
            sequence = -sequence
        change_order = ('connector:' + contract.contract_key, sequence)
    canonical,record,duplicate=ingest_content_event(db,event_id=event_key,
        schema_version='content-ingress-v1',event_type=normalized.pop('event_type','content.created'),
        source_system=contract.logical_locator,occurred_at=event.source_occurred_at or event.received_at,
        payload=normalized,received_by=actor,source_change_order=change_order,connector_intake_reference={'intake_event_id': event.id, 'payload_hash': event.payload_hash})
    dispatch=dispatch_content_event(db,event=canonical,record=record,duplicate=duplicate,actor=actor)
    run=db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.run_key==dispatch.run_key)) if dispatch.run_key else None
    if run is not None:
        if run.model_execution_mode!='real' or run.workflow_version_id!=mechanism.workflow_version_id:
            raise WorkflowConnectorError('upstream_intake_run_conflict','增量运行授权或版本发生变化')
        event.production_run_id=run.id
    db.flush()
    return {'production_run_id':run.id if run else None,'run_status':run.status if run else dispatch.routing_status}


def link_connector_run(db, *, canonical, run):
    """Link every immediate or deferred native run to its signed intake fact."""
    reference = json.loads(canonical.identity_snapshot_json or '{}').get('connector_intake')
    if reference is None:
        return
    event = db.scalar(select(UpstreamIntakeEvent).where(
        UpstreamIntakeEvent.id == reference.get('intake_event_id')).with_for_update())
    if event is None or event.payload_hash != reference.get('payload_hash'):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError('upstream_intake_run_conflict', 'Connector identity mismatch')
    expected = 'connector-native:' + hashlib.sha256(canonical_json([
        event.contract_version_id, event.source_event_id]).encode()).hexdigest()
    if canonical.event_id != expected or run.model_execution_mode != 'real':
        raise WorkflowConnectorError('upstream_intake_run_conflict', 'Connector run identity mismatch')
    if event.production_run_id not in (None, run.id):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError('upstream_intake_run_conflict', 'Connector already has another run')
    event.production_run_id = run.id
    db.flush()

===== FILE backend/app/correction_contract.py =====
"""Canonical correction-contract metadata and value validation."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from copy import deepcopy
from numbers import Real
from typing import Any, Mapping

from .prompt_output_contract import (
    PromptOutputContract,
    parse_output_contract,
)


_HAN_PATTERN = re.compile(r"[\u3400-\u9fff]")
_LAYER_ORDER = {"A": 0, "B": 1, "V3": 2}
SUPPORTED_CORRECTION_NODE_TYPES = frozenset(
    {
        "enum",
        "enumeration",
        "integer",
        "int",
        "number",
        "float",
        "decimal",
        "boolean",
        "bool",
        "text",
        "string",
        "list",
        "array",
        "object",
        "json_object",
        "rule_hit",
        "rule_judgement",
        "rule_judgment",
    }
)
_NODE_FIELDS = {
    "node_key",
    "layer",
    "path",
    "order",
    "label",
    "description",
    "type",
    "semantic_version",
    "compatibility_key",
    "required",
    "evidence",
    "options",
    "allowed_values",
    "values",
    "min",
    "max",
    "minimum",
    "maximum",
    "minItems",
    "maxItems",
    "min_value",
    "max_value",
    "recompute_ref",
    "metadata",
}


class ContractValidationError(ValueError):
    """Stable, structured validation failure for correction contracts."""

    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, fields: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.fields = list(fields or [])


def _canonical_payload(contract: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload = deepcopy(dict(contract))
    payload.pop("contract_hash", None)
    return payload


def correction_contract_hash(contract: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Hash canonical JSON while excluding the hash field itself."""
    encoded = json.dumps(
        _canonical_payload(contract),
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    ).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()


def normalize_correction_contract(
    raw: Mapping[str, Any], *, category_key: str
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return a deterministic, detached contract representation."""
    if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
        raise ContractValidationError(
            "CORRECTION_CONTRACT_INVALID",
            "纠偏合同必须是对象",
            ["contract"],
        )
    normalized = deepcopy(dict(raw))
    normalized["category_key"] = category_key
    nodes = normalized.get("nodes", [])
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        raise ContractValidationError(
            "CORRECTION_CONTRACT_INVALID",
            "纠偏合同 nodes 必须是数组",
            ["nodes"],
        )

    canonical_nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item in nodes:
        if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
            canonical_nodes.append(deepcopy(item))
            continue
        node = deepcopy(dict(item))
        unknown = {
            key: value for key, value in node.items() if key not in _NODE_FIELDS
        }
        existing_metadata = node.get("metadata")
        if existing_metadata is not None and not isinstance(existing_metadata, Mapping):
            existing_metadata = {"value": deepcopy(existing_metadata)}
        metadata = dict(existing_metadata or {})
        metadata.update(unknown)
        if metadata:
            node["metadata"] = metadata
        for key in unknown:
            node.pop(key, None)
        canonical_nodes.append(node)

    def sort_key(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, int, str, str]:
        layer = node.get("layer")
        path = node.get("path")
        order = node.get("order", 0)
        return (
            _LAYER_ORDER.get(layer, len(_LAYER_ORDER)),
            order if isinstance(order, int) and not isinstance(order, bool) else 0,
            str(path or ""),
            str(node.get("node_key", "")),
        )

    normalized["nodes"] = sorted(canonical_nodes, key=sort_key)
    return normalized


def _nonempty_string(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, str) and bool(value.strip())


def _has_chinese(value: Any) -> bool:
    return _nonempty_string(value) and bool(_HAN_PATTERN.search(value))


def _bounds(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[Any, Any]:
    lower = next(
        (node[key] for key in ("minimum", "min", "min_value") if key in node),
        None,
    )
    upper = next(
        (node[key] for key in ("maximum", "max", "max_value") if key in node),
        None,
    )
    return lower, upper


def _is_numeric_type(node_type: str) -> bool:
    return (
        node_type in {"integer", "int", "number", "float", "decimal"}
        or node_type.startswith("integer_")
        or node_type.startswith("number_")
        or node_type.startswith("float_")
    )


def validate_correction_contract(contract: Mapping[str, Any], *, presentation_overlay: bool = False) -> list[str]:
    """Return human-readable field failures without mutating the contract."""
    errors: list[str] = []
    if not isinstance(contract, Mapping):
        return ["contract 必须是对象"]
    if not _nonempty_string(contract.get("contract_version")):
        errors.append("contract_version 必须是非空字符串")
    if not _nonempty_string(contract.get("category_key")):
        errors.append("category_key 必须是非空字符串")
    nodes = contract.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not nodes:
        return errors + ["nodes 必须是非空数组"]
    if presentation_overlay:
        # Validate the complete structure with repaired display labels, while
        # the original embedded hash is still checked against original bytes below.
        nodes = correction_display_contract(contract)['nodes']

    seen: set[str] = set()
    for index, node in enumerate(nodes):
        prefix = f"nodes[{index}]"
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            errors.append(f"{prefix} 必须是对象")
            continue
        node_key = node.get("node_key")
        field_prefix = f"{prefix}.{node_key}" if _nonempty_string(node_key) else prefix
        if not _nonempty_string(node_key):
            errors.append(f"{field_prefix}.node_key 缺失")
        elif node_key in seen:
            errors.append(f"{field_prefix}.node_key 重复")
        else:
            seen.add(node_key)
        if not _nonempty_string(node.get("layer")):
            errors.append(f"{field_prefix}.layer 必须是非空字符串")
        for field in ("label", "description"):
            if not _has_chinese(node.get(field)):
                errors.append(f"{field_prefix}.{field} 必须是非空中文描述")
        for field in ("type", "semantic_version", "compatibility_key"):
            if not _nonempty_string(node.get(field)):
                errors.append(f"{field_prefix}.{field} 缺失")
        if not isinstance(node.get("required"), bool):
            errors.append(f"{field_prefix}.required 必须显式为布尔值")
        evidence = node.get("evidence")
        if not isinstance(evidence, Mapping) or not _nonempty_string(
            evidence.get("description")
        ):
            errors.append(f"{field_prefix}.evidence.description 缺失")
        node_type = str(node.get("type", "")).lower()
        if (
            node_type
            and node_type not in SUPPORTED_CORRECTION_NODE_TYPES
            and not _is_numeric_type(node_type)
        ):
            errors.append(f"{field_prefix}.type 不受支持")
        options = node.get("options", node.get("allowed_values", node.get("values")))
        if node_type in {"enum", "enumeration"} and (
            not isinstance(options, list) or not options
        ):
            errors.append(f"{field_prefix}.options 必须是非空数组")
        if _is_numeric_type(node_type):
            lower, upper = _bounds(node)
            if lower is None or upper is None:
                errors.append(f"{field_prefix} 必须声明数值上下界")
            elif (
                not isinstance(lower, Real)
                or isinstance(lower, bool)
                or not isinstance(upper, Real)
                or isinstance(upper, bool)
            ):
                errors.append(f"{field_prefix} 数值上下界必须是数字")
            elif lower > upper:
                errors.append(f"{field_prefix} 数值上下界无效")
        if node.get("layer") == "V3" and not _nonempty_string(node.get("recompute_ref")):
            errors.append(f"{field_prefix}.recompute_ref 缺失")
    contract_hash = contract.get("contract_hash")
    if contract_hash is not None and contract_hash != correction_contract_hash(contract):
        errors.append("contract_hash 与规范化合同不一致")
    return errors


def assert_correction_contract_complete(contract: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    errors = validate_correction_contract(contract)
    if errors:
        fields: list[str] = []
        for error in errors:
            location = error.split(" ", 1)[0]
            parts = location.split(".")
            if len(parts) >= 3 and parts[0].startswith("nodes["):
                fields.append(".".join(parts[1:-1]))
            else:
                fields.append(location)
        raise ContractValidationError(
            "CORRECTION_CONTRACT_INCOMPLETE",
            "纠偏合同不完整：" + "；".join(errors),
            fields,
        )


# 路线字段：值本身由「国内/海外 × 整体/单体」派生，但纠偏页必须能改——
# 运营看图发现这条被判错了整体/单体，直接在 is_single 里填对的值就该生效，
# 平台负责把它翻译成一次路线改判，而不是拿合同把人挡回去（2026-09-03 老大定）。
_ROUTE_CONTROLLED_FIELDS = {
    "is_single": "整体/单体",
    "ll_id": "国内素材 ID",
    "res_id": "海外素材 ID",
}
_SITE_BY_NODE_KEY = (("overseas", "overseas"), ("domestic", "domestic"))


def route_site_from_node_key(node_key: str) -> str | None:
    """从 workflow.prompt_overseas_whole.is_single 这类键里认出站点。"""

    lowered = node_key.lower()
    for token, site in _SITE_BY_NODE_KEY:
        if token in lowered:
            return site
    return None


def allowed_is_single_values(node_key: str) -> list[int] | None:
    """该节点所在站点允许的整体/单体编码；认不出站点时返回 None。"""

    from .knowledge_graph_contracts import IS_SINGLE_CODES

    site = route_site_from_node_key(node_key)
    if site is None:
        return None
    return sorted(IS_SINGLE_CODES[site].values())


def _display_value(value: Any) -> str:
    text = "空值" if value is None else str(value)
    return text if len(text) <= 60 else text[:57] + "…"


def _node_value_error_message(
    node: Mapping[str, Any], value: Any, *, node_key: str, options: Any
) -> str:
    field = node_key.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
    label = str(node.get("label") or _ROUTE_CONTROLLED_FIELDS.get(field) or field)
    if field == "is_single":
        allowed = allowed_is_single_values(node_key)
        if allowed:
            site = "海外" if route_site_from_node_key(node_key) == "overseas" else "国内"
            pairs = "、".join(
                f"{code}（{'整体' if index == 0 else '单体'}）"
                for index, code in enumerate(allowed)
            )
            return (
                f"「{label}」在{site}链路只能填 {pairs}，当前填的是 {_display_value(value)}。"
                "填另一个值会把这条素材改判到对应路线，下一轮生效。"
            )
    if isinstance(options, list) and options:
        allowed = "、".join(_display_value(item) for item in options[:8])
        return f"「{label}」只能填 {allowed}，当前填的是 {_display_value(value)}"
    node_type = str(node.get("type", "")).lower()
    type_hints = {
        "integer": "整数", "int": "整数", "number": "数字", "float": "数字",
        "boolean": "是/否", "bool": "是/否", "text": "文本", "string": "文本",
        "list": "多个值", "array": "多个值",
    }
    hint = type_hints.get(node_type)
    if hint:
        return f"「{label}」必须是{hint}，当前填的是 {_display_value(value)}"
    return f"「{label}」的值不符合本轮冻结的合同要求（当前填的是 {_display_value(value)}）"


def allows_manual_text(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """Text labels may extend suggestions; route codes and operational enums stay closed."""
    options = node.get("options", node.get("allowed_values", node.get("values")))
    field = str(node.get("node_key", "")).rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
    return (str(node.get("type", "")).lower() in {"enum", "enumeration"}
            and isinstance(options, list) and bool(options)
            and all(isinstance(v, str) for v in options)
            and field not in {"grade", "level", "level_cap", "final_level", "is_single", "source", "confidence", "status"})


def validate_node_value(node: Mapping[str, Any], value: Any, *, manual_text: bool = False) -> None:
    node_key = str(node.get("node_key", "node"))
    if value is None and node.get("required") is False:
        return
    node_type = str(node.get("type", "")).lower()
    options = node.get("options", node.get("allowed_values", node.get("values")))
    valid = True
    if node_type in {"enum", "enumeration"}:
        valid = isinstance(options, list) and value in options
        if not valid and manual_text and allows_manual_text(node):
            valid = isinstance(value, str) and bool(value.strip()) and len(value) <= 2000
        # 整体/单体判错时，运营要能在字段里直接改对，由平台改判路线；
        # 因此放行同站点的另一个合法编码（海外 1/2、国内 0/1）。
        if not valid and node_key.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] == "is_single":
            allowed = allowed_is_single_values(node_key)
            valid = bool(allowed) and value in allowed
    elif node_type in {"integer", "int"} or node_type.startswith("integer_"):
        valid = isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)
    elif _is_numeric_type(node_type):
        valid = isinstance(value, Real) and not isinstance(value, bool)
    elif node_type in {"boolean", "bool"}:
        valid = isinstance(value, bool)
    elif node_type in {"text", "string"}:
        valid = isinstance(value, str)
    elif node_type in {"list", "array"}:
        valid = isinstance(value, list)
    elif node_type in {"object", "json_object"}:
        valid = isinstance(value, Mapping)
    elif node_type in {"rule_hit", "rule_judgement", "rule_judgment"}:
        valid = (
            isinstance(value, Mapping)
            and _nonempty_string(value.get("rule_id"))
            and value.get("confidence") in {"high", "medium", "low"}
            and _nonempty_string(value.get("evidence"))
        )
    if valid and _is_numeric_type(node_type):
        lower, upper = _bounds(node)
        valid = (lower is None or value >= lower) and (upper is None or value <= upper)
    if not valid:
        raise ContractValidationError(
            "CORRECTION_NODE_VALUE_INVALID",
            _node_value_error_message(
                node, value, node_key=node_key, options=options
            ),
            [node_key],
        )


def inherit_correction_node(
    previous: Mapping[str, Any] | None, current: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    result = deepcopy(dict(current))
    compatible = (
        isinstance(previous, Mapping)
        and previous.get("node_key") == current.get("node_key")
        and previous.get("type") == current.get("type")
        and previous.get("semantic_version") == current.get("semantic_version")
        and previous.get("compatibility_key") == current.get("compatibility_key")
    )
    if compatible:
        for field in ("human_value", "reason", "evidence"):
            if field in previous:
                result[field] = deepcopy(previous[field])
        result["inheritance"] = {"status": "inherited"}
    elif previous is None:
        result["inheritance"] = {"status": "new"}
    else:
        result["inheritance"] = {"status": "changed"}
    return result


def _fallback_label(prefix: str, key: Any) -> str:
    return f"{prefix}{str(key or '未命名')}"


def _source_nodes(
    source: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    layer: str,
    path_prefix: str,
    label_prefix: str,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if not isinstance(source, Mapping):
        return []
    raw_nodes = source.get("nodes")
    if isinstance(raw_nodes, list):
        result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for index, raw in enumerate(raw_nodes):
            if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
                continue
            node = deepcopy(dict(raw))
            key = str(node.get("node_key") or node.get("key") or f"node_{index}")
            node["node_key"] = key
            node.setdefault("layer", layer)
            node.setdefault("path", f"{path_prefix}.{key}")
            node.setdefault("order", index)
            node.setdefault("label", node.get("name") or _fallback_label(label_prefix, key))
            node.setdefault("description", f"冻结{node['label']}的纠偏判断")
            node.setdefault("type", node.get("value_type") or "text")
            node.setdefault("semantic_version", "1")
            node.setdefault("compatibility_key", key)
            node.setdefault("required", False)
            node.setdefault("evidence", {"description": f"请提供{node['label']}的图片证据"})
            result.append(node)
        return result
    raw_fields = source.get("fields")
    if not isinstance(raw_fields, list):
        return []
    result = []
    for index, raw in enumerate(raw_fields):
        if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
            continue
        key = str(raw.get("key") or raw.get("field_key") or f"field_{index}")
        label = str(raw.get("label") or raw.get("name") or _fallback_label(label_prefix, key))
        node = {
            **deepcopy(dict(raw)),
            "node_key": f"{path_prefix}.{key}",
            "layer": layer,
            "path": f"{path_prefix}.{key}",
            "order": index,
            "label": label,
            "description": str(raw.get("description") or f"冻结{label}的纠偏判断"),
            "type": str(raw.get("type") or raw.get("value_type") or "text"),
            "semantic_version": str(raw.get("semantic_version") or "1"),
            "compatibility_key": str(raw.get("compatibility_key") or key),
            "required": bool(raw.get("required", False)),
            "evidence": raw.get("evidence") if isinstance(raw.get("evidence"), Mapping) else {"description": f"请提供{label}的图片证据"},
        }
        result.append(node)
    return result


_PRODUCTION_FIELD_SPECS: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {

        "type": "enum",
        "options": ["AI图", "实景照片", "3D数字效果图", "其它"],
        "required": True,
    },
