def _chinese_text(value: Any, fallback: str) -> str:
    text = str(value or "").strip()
    return text if text and _has_chinese(text) else fallback


def _node_evidence(label: str, *, required: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "description": f"请提供{label}的图片或人工判断证据",
        "required": required,
    }


def _redline_reason_options(
    v3_bundle: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> list[str] | None:
    if not isinstance(v3_bundle, Mapping):
        return None
    contract = v3_bundle.get("contract")
    policy = contract.get("redline_policy") if isinstance(contract, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(policy, Mapping):
        return None
    options: list[str] = []
    for rule in policy.get("rules") or []:
        if not isinstance(rule, Mapping):
            continue
        for value in rule.get("match_any") or []:
            if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip() and value.strip() not in options:
                options.append(value.strip())
    return options


def _production_field_nodes(
    source: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    v3_bundle: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Build call-A nodes from an explicit or compatibility production contract."""

    raw_items: list[Any] = []
    if isinstance(source, Mapping):
        candidate = source.get("fields")
        if not isinstance(candidate, list):
            candidate = source.get("nodes")
        if isinstance(candidate, list):
            raw_items = candidate
    if not raw_items:
        raw_items = [
            {"key": key, **deepcopy(spec)}
            for key, spec in _PRODUCTION_FIELD_SPECS.items()
        ]

    nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    frozen_reason_options = _redline_reason_options(v3_bundle)
    for index, raw in enumerate(raw_items):
        if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
            continue
        key = str(raw.get("key") or raw.get("field_key") or "").strip()
        raw_node_key = str(raw.get("node_key") or "").strip()
        if raw_node_key.startswith("call_a."):
            node_key = raw_node_key
            field_key = raw_node_key.split(".", 1)[1]
        elif key:
            field_key = key.removeprefix("call_a.")
            node_key = f"call_a.{field_key}"
        else:
            continue
        fallback = _PRODUCTION_FIELD_SPECS.get(field_key, {})
        label = _chinese_text(
            raw.get("label") or raw.get("name") or fallback.get("label"),
            f"生产字段 {field_key}",
        )
        node: dict[str, Any] = {
            **deepcopy(dict(raw)),
            "node_key": node_key,
            "layer": "A",
            "path": str(raw.get("path") or node_key),
            "order": index,
            "label": label,
            "description": _chinese_text(
                raw.get("description") or fallback.get("description"),
                f"冻结{label}的人工判断",
            ),
            "type": str(
                raw.get("type")
                or raw.get("value_type")
                or fallback.get("type")
                or "text"
            ),
            "semantic_version": str(raw.get("semantic_version") or "1"),
            "compatibility_key": str(
                raw.get("compatibility_key") or f"production-field:{field_key}"
            ),
            "required": bool(raw.get("required", fallback.get("required", False))),
            "evidence": (
                deepcopy(raw["evidence"])
                if isinstance(raw.get("evidence"), Mapping)
                else _node_evidence(label)
            ),
            "metadata": {
                "node_type": "call_a_field",
                "field_key": field_key,
                **(
                    deepcopy(dict(raw.get("metadata")))
                    if isinstance(raw.get("metadata"), Mapping)
                    else {}
                ),
            },
        }
        for bound in ("options", "allowed_values", "values", "min", "max", "minimum", "maximum"):
            if bound not in node and bound in fallback:
                node[bound] = deepcopy(fallback[bound])
        if field_key == "reason" and frozen_reason_options is not None:
            node["options"] = list(frozen_reason_options)
        nodes.append(node)
    return nodes


def _dimension_rule_nodes(
    dimension_definition: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    v3_bundle: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    selected_keys: list[str] | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Flatten frozen dimension and subcategory rule definitions into B nodes."""

    definitions: list[tuple[Mapping[str, Any], bool]] = []

    def add_dimensions(value: Any, *, respect_selection: bool) -> None:
        if not isinstance(value, list):
            return
        for item in value:
            if isinstance(item, Mapping):
                definitions.append((item, respect_selection))

    if isinstance(dimension_definition, Mapping):
        add_dimensions(
            dimension_definition.get("dimensions"),
            respect_selection=True,
        )

    if isinstance(v3_bundle, Mapping):
        subcategories = v3_bundle.get("subcategory_dimensions")
        if isinstance(subcategories, Mapping):
            for config in subcategories.values():
                if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
                    continue
                for group_name in ("common_group", "specific_group"):
                    group = config.get(group_name)
                    schema = group.get("schema_definition") if isinstance(group, Mapping) else None
                    if isinstance(schema, Mapping):
                        add_dimensions(
                            schema.get("dimensions"),
                            respect_selection=False,
                        )

    selected = set(selected_keys or [])
    seen: set[tuple[str, str, str]] = set()
    nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for dimension, respect_selection in definitions:
        dimension_key = str(dimension.get("key") or "").strip()
        if (
            not dimension_key
            or (respect_selection and selected and dimension_key not in selected)
        ):
            continue
        dimension_label = _chinese_text(
            dimension.get("label") or dimension.get("name"),
            f"维度 {dimension_key}",
        )
        for rule_kind, source_key, path_key in (
            ("deduction", "deduction_rules", "hit_rules"),
            ("bonus", "bonus_rules", "hit_bonus_rules"),
        ):
            rules = dimension.get(source_key)
            if not isinstance(rules, list):
                continue
            for index, raw_rule in enumerate(rules):
                if not isinstance(raw_rule, Mapping):
                    continue
                rule_id = str(raw_rule.get("rule_id") or raw_rule.get("key") or "").strip()
                if not rule_id:
                    continue
                identity = (dimension_key, rule_kind, rule_id)
                if identity in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(identity)
                description = _chinese_text(
                    raw_rule.get("description"),
                    f"{dimension_label}的{rule_id}规则判断",
                )
                label = f"{dimension_label}：{description}"
                nodes.append(
                    {
                        "node_key": f"call_b.{dimension_key}.{rule_id}",
                        "layer": "B",
                        "path": f"dimension.{dimension_key}.{path_key}.{rule_id}",
                        "order": len(nodes),
                        "label": label,
                        "description": description,
                        "type": "rule_hit",
                        "semantic_version": str(
                            raw_rule.get("semantic_version")
                            or dimension.get("semantic_version")
                            or "1"
                        ),
                        "compatibility_key": f"dimension-rule:{dimension_key}:{rule_kind}:{rule_id}",
                        "required": False,
                        "evidence": _node_evidence(label, required=True),
                        "metadata": {
                            "node_type": "dimension_rule",
                            "dimension_key": dimension_key,
                            "rule_id": rule_id,
                            "rule_kind": rule_kind,
                            "editable": True,
                        },
                    }
                )
    return nodes


def _hard_defect_options(v3_bundle: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Read the frozen hard-defect rule list so options follow the mechanism.

    Options come from the frozen contract rather than a frontend constant, so
    adding or removing a defect in a new mechanism version needs no code change.
    """
    if not isinstance(v3_bundle, Mapping):
        return []
    contract = v3_bundle.get("contract")
    modifiers = contract.get("common_modifiers") if isinstance(contract, Mapping) else None
    veto = modifiers.get("high_score_veto") if isinstance(modifiers, Mapping) else None
    rules = veto.get("rules") if isinstance(veto, Mapping) else None
    options: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for rule in rules or []:
        if not isinstance(rule, Mapping) or rule.get("source") != "hard_defects":
            continue
        key = str(rule.get("key") or "").strip()
        if not key or any(item["value"] == key for item in options):
            continue
        options.append(
            {
                "value": key,
                "label": _chinese_text(rule.get("description"), key),
                "severity": str(rule.get("severity") or ""),
            }
        )
    return options


def _judgement_nodes(v3_bundle: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Model judgements that drive the score but had no correctable node.

    Both write through the deterministic replay path: hard defects cap or veto
    the score, and the Call-B aesthetic score is the matcher's starting score.
    Without these nodes an operator could see a judgement they disagree with and
    have no way to correct it.
    """
    if not isinstance(v3_bundle, Mapping) or not isinstance(
        v3_bundle.get("contract"), Mapping
    ):
        return []
    spec_version = str(v3_bundle["contract"].get("spec_version") or "1")
    defect_options = _hard_defect_options(v3_bundle)
    nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
        {
            "node_key": "call_a.hard_defects",
            "layer": "A",
            "path": "precheck.hard_defects",
            "order": 0,
            "label": "硬缺陷判定",
            "description": "调用A判定的硬缺陷；命中会压分或封顶，改动后服务端重算",
            "type": "list",
            "options": [option["value"] for option in defect_options],
            "semantic_version": spec_version,
            "compatibility_key": "precheck-hard-defects",
            "required": False,
            "evidence": _node_evidence("硬缺陷判定", required=True),
            "metadata": {
                "node_type": "precheck_field",
                "option_labels": {
                    option["value"]: option["label"] for option in defect_options
                },
                "option_severities": {
                    option["value"]: option["severity"] for option in defect_options
                },
            },
            "recompute_ref": "evaluation_v3_pipeline.recompute_qualified_v3",
        },
        {
            "node_key": "call_b.aesthetic_score",
            "layer": "B",
            "path": "aesthetic.aesthetic_score",
            "order": 0,
            "label": "调用B美感分",
            "description": "调用B给出的 0-100 美感分，是等级撮合器的初始分",
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 0,
            "maximum": 100,
            "semantic_version": spec_version,
            "compatibility_key": "call-b-aesthetic-score",
            "required": False,
            "evidence": _node_evidence("调用B美感分", required=True),
            "metadata": {"node_type": "aesthetic_score"},
            "recompute_ref": "evaluation_v3_pipeline.recompute_qualified_v3",
        },
    ]
    return nodes


def _v3_nodes(v3_bundle: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Expose readable V3 decision nodes while keeping rule execution server-side."""

    if not isinstance(v3_bundle, Mapping):
        return []
    contract = v3_bundle.get("contract")
    if not isinstance(contract, Mapping):
        return []
    nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    track_block = contract.get("track_classification")
    tracks = track_block.get("tracks") if isinstance(track_block, Mapping) else None
    track_options = [
        str(item.get("key"))
        for item in tracks or []
        if isinstance(item, Mapping) and item.get("key")
    ]
    track_labels = {
        str(item.get("key")): str(item.get("label"))
        for item in tracks or []
        if isinstance(item, Mapping) and item.get("key")
    }
    if track_options:
        nodes.append(
            {
                "node_key": "v3.track_key",
                "layer": "V3",
                "path": "scoring.track_key",
                "order": len(nodes),
                "label": "等级撮合赛道",
                "description": "根据冻结分类合同确定图片所属赛道",
                "type": "enum",
                "options": track_options,
                "semantic_version": str(contract.get("spec_version") or "1"),
                "compatibility_key": "v3-track-classification",
                "required": True,
                "evidence": _node_evidence("等级撮合赛道", required=True),
                "metadata": {"node_type": "track", "option_labels": track_labels},
                "recompute_ref": "evaluation_v3_pipeline.recompute_qualified_v3",
            }
        )

    # 等级阈值有两套字段：旧 level_thresholds 与新 level_scale（现行合同用后者，
    # 旧字段为 null）。此前只认旧字段，导致新合同下「最终等级」节点整个不生成，
    # 复核页那一行没有纠偏按钮——运营反馈「纠偏改不了等级」的根因。
    thresholds = contract.get("level_thresholds")
    if not (isinstance(thresholds, (list, Mapping)) and thresholds):
        scale = contract.get("level_scale")
        if isinstance(scale, Mapping):
            levels_raw = scale.get("levels")
            if isinstance(levels_raw, list) and levels_raw:
                thresholds = [
                    {
                        "level": item.get("level"),
                        "min_score": item.get("min_score"),
                    }
                    for item in levels_raw
                    if isinstance(item, Mapping)
                    and item.get("level")
                    and item.get("enabled", True)
                ]
    if isinstance(thresholds, (list, Mapping)) and thresholds:
        threshold_value = deepcopy(thresholds)
        threshold_type = "list" if isinstance(thresholds, list) else "object"
        nodes.append(
            {
                "node_key": "v3.level_thresholds",
                "layer": "V3",
                "path": "scoring.level_thresholds",
                "order": len(nodes),
                "label": "等级分数阈值",
                "description": "本轮冻结的分数到等级映射阈值，仅供查看",
                "type": threshold_type,
                "semantic_version": str(contract.get("spec_version") or "1"),
                "compatibility_key": "v3-level-thresholds",
                "required": True,
                "evidence": _node_evidence("等级分数阈值"),
                "metadata": {
                    "node_type": "v3_thresholds",
                    "editable": False,
                    "frozen_value": threshold_value,
                    "read_only_reason": "等级阈值属于冻结规则，只能通过候选机制版本修改",
                },
                "recompute_ref": "evaluation_v3_pipeline.recompute_qualified_v3",
            }
        )
        levels = [
            str(item.get("level"))
            for item in thresholds
            if isinstance(item, Mapping) and item.get("level")
        ] if isinstance(thresholds, list) else [
            str(key) for key in thresholds if str(key) in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"}
        ]
        if levels:
            nodes.append(
                {
                    "node_key": "v3.final_level",
                    "layer": "V3",
                    "path": "scoring.level",
                    "order": len(nodes),
                    "label": "最终等级",
                    "description": "由服务端权威评分引擎根据冻结规则计算最终等级",
                    "type": "enum",
                    "options": list(dict.fromkeys(levels)),
                    "semantic_version": str(contract.get("spec_version") or "1"),
                    "compatibility_key": "v3-final-level",
                    "required": True,
                    "evidence": _node_evidence("最终等级", required=True),
                    "metadata": {"node_type": "final_level"},
                    "recompute_ref": "evaluation_v3_pipeline.recompute_qualified_v3",
                }
            )
    return nodes


def freeze_correction_contract(
    *,
    category_key: str,
    prompt_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    dimension_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    production_field_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    v3_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Compose and hash an immutable contract from the selected run inputs."""
    prompt_nodes = _source_nodes(
        prompt_snapshot,
        layer=str(prompt_snapshot.get("layer") or prompt_snapshot.get("stage") or "A"),
        path_prefix="prompt",
        label_prefix="提示词节点 ",
    )
    dimension_nodes = _source_nodes(
        dimension_snapshot,
        layer="A",
        path_prefix="dimensions",
        label_prefix="维度 ",
    )
    field_nodes = _source_nodes(
        production_field_snapshot,
        layer="B",
        path_prefix="production_fields",
        label_prefix="生产字段 ",
    )
    v3_nodes = _source_nodes(
        v3_snapshot,
        layer="V3",
        path_prefix="v3",
        label_prefix="规则节点 ",
    )
    # V3 nodes must retain the server-side recompute reference; a missing
    # reference intentionally remains incomplete and is blocked at release.
    nodes = prompt_nodes + dimension_nodes + field_nodes + v3_nodes
    contract = normalize_correction_contract(
        {
            "contract_version": "correction-contract-v1",
            "category_key": category_key,
            "nodes": nodes,
            "sources": {
                "prompt": deepcopy(dict(prompt_snapshot)),
                "dimensions": deepcopy(dict(dimension_snapshot)),
                "production_fields": deepcopy(dict(production_field_snapshot)),
                "v3": deepcopy(dict(v3_snapshot)),
            },
        },
        category_key=category_key,
    )
    contract["contract_hash"] = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    return contract


def correction_contract_from_run_snapshot(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Extract a detached contract from a run snapshot without active fallback."""
    if not isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
        return None
    candidate = snapshot.get("correction_contract")
    if not isinstance(candidate, Mapping):
        if "contract_version" in snapshot and isinstance(snapshot.get("nodes"), list):
            candidate = snapshot
        else:
            return None
    return deepcopy(dict(candidate))

def _workflow_node_identity(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return str(node.get("node_instance_id") or node.get("key") or "").strip()


def _schema_type(schema: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    raw_type = schema.get("type")
    if isinstance(raw_type, list):
        raw_type = next(
            (item for item in raw_type if item != "null"),
            "object",
        )
    return str(raw_type or "object")

    "grade": "评级",
    "evidence": "证据",
    "shortcomings": "不足",
}


def _inspiration_field_labels() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """灵感图字段的中文名。红线项直接复用既有业务口径，不另起一套说法。"""

    from .schema_adapter import (
        _INSPIRATION_REDLINE_EVIDENCE_KEY_BY_REASON,
        _INSPIRATION_REDLINE_REASON_MAP,
    )

    labels: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {
        key: {"label": name} for key, name in _INSPIRATION_FIELD_LABELS.items()
    }
    # 红线子项：screenshot -> 「红线命中：是截图」。两张表合起来才覆盖全部取值，
    # 一张按 key 记、一张按业务原因反查，都是既有的权威口径。
    redline: dict[str, str] = dict(_INSPIRATION_REDLINE_REASON_MAP)
    for reason, key in _INSPIRATION_REDLINE_EVIDENCE_KEY_BY_REASON.items():
        redline.setdefault(str(key), str(reason))
    for key, reason in redline.items():
        labels[f"redline_triggered.{key}"] = {"label": f"红线命中：{reason}"}
    for dim, name in _INSPIRATION_DIMENSION_LABELS.items():
        for part, part_name in _DIMENSION_PART_LABELS.items():
            labels[f"dimensions.{dim}.{part}"] = {"label": f"{name} · {part_name}"}
    return labels


def _display_label_fallback(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    """任何仍无中文的 label 的兜底。

    映射表永远会落后于新字段：漏一个字段，validate_correction_contract 就判整份
    契约失败、纠偏页整页只读，运营一个字段都改不了。所以兜底不是锦上添花，而是
    保证「最坏情况下仍可纠偏」的下限。保留原字段名，运营仍能对上是哪一项。
    """

    key = str(node.get("node_key") or "")
    name = key.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] if key else "output"
    return f"提示词输出字段 {name}"


def correction_display_contract(contract: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Label-only copy: preserve frozen identity, field set, types and authored titles."""
    result = deepcopy(dict(contract))
    labels = {
        **_SCHEMA_FIELD_LABELS,
        **_model_3d_su_field_labels(),
        **_inspiration_field_labels(),
    }
    for node in result.get('nodes', []):
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            continue
        key = str(node.get('node_key') or '')
        metadata = node.get('metadata')
        schema = metadata.get('output_field_schema', {}) if isinstance(metadata, Mapping) else {}
        if isinstance(schema, Mapping) and schema.get('title'):
            continue
        for path, display in labels.items():
            if key.startswith('workflow.') and key.endswith('.'+path):
                if node.get('label') == path.rsplit('.', 1)[-1]:
                    node['label'] = display['label']
                    if not node.get('description') and display.get('description') and (
                            not display.get('options') or node.get('type') in {'text', 'string', 'enum'}):
                        node['description'] = display['description']
                break
        # 兜底：表里没有的字段也必须有中文 label，否则整份契约校验失败、
        # 纠偏页退化成只读。只补仍无中文的，已中文化的和作者写的 title 不动。
        if not _has_chinese(node.get('label')):
            node['label'] = _display_label_fallback(node)
        if not _has_chinese(node.get('description')):
            node['description'] = f"核对{node['label']}的人工判断"
    return result


def _model_3d_su_field_labels() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """3D/SU 专有风险字段：取值只有「空」或一句固定短语，做成可点选的枚举。

    短语直接复用 RISK_FIELD_PHRASES——它同时是调用 A 提示词里写死的文案和
    schema_adapter 的校验依据，从那里取才能保证三处口径一致。
    """

    from .model_3d_su_legacy_projection import RISK_FIELD_PHRASES

    names = {
        "watermark": "水印",
        "unrendered": "未渲染模型图",
        "brand_trademark": "品牌 logo/商标",
        "brand_name": "品牌名称",
        "chinese": "中文字样",
        "film_ip": "影视/综艺 IP",
        "celebrities": "名人/明星形象",
        "game_ip": "游戏/动漫 IP",
        "other_ip": "其它热门 IP",
        "qr_code": "二维码",
        "landmark": "地标建筑",
        "religion": "宗教元素",
    }
    labels: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for key, phrase in RISK_FIELD_PHRASES.items():
        name = names.get(key, key)
        labels[f"model_3d_su_fields.{key}"] = {
            "label": name,
            "description": f"图中是否可见{name}；命中填「{phrase}」，未命中留空",
            "options": ["", phrase],
            "type": "enum",
        }
    labels["model_3d_su_fields.high_risk"] = {
        "label": "高风险标记",
        "description": "需人工复核的高风险机器标记（如 cg_model），不自动定级 L5",
    }
    return labels


# 对 3D/SU 隐藏的纠偏字段。
# production_fields.image_defects 在 3D/SU 提示词里承担两件机器职责：CG 红线命中时
# 写入 CG_GAME_ANIMATION_REDLINE 标记，L4 严重呈现失败时记录事实描述（schema 允许
# 400 字自由文本）。它不是人工可判定的「有无水印」——3D/SU 的水印判定在
# model_3d_su_fields.watermark。放着让运营纠会把机器标记改坏，进而影响红线判定，
# 所以只在 3D/SU 从纠偏字段里摘掉；模型照常返回，红线链路不受影响。
_MODEL_3D_SU_HIDDEN_FIELDS = frozenset({"production_fields.image_defects"})


def _schema_field_overrides(path: str, *, model_3d_su: bool) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """返回该路径的中文覆盖；``None`` 表示这个字段不进纠偏。"""

    if model_3d_su and path in _MODEL_3D_SU_HIDDEN_FIELDS:
        return None
    if model_3d_su and path.startswith("model_3d_su_fields."):
        return _model_3d_su_field_labels().get(path, {})
    return _SCHEMA_FIELD_LABELS.get(path, {})


def _schema_path_value(root: Any, parts: tuple[str, ...]) -> Any:
    current = root
    for part in parts:
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping) or part not in current:
            return None
        current = current[part]
    return deepcopy(current)


def _correction_schema_leaves(
    schema: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    current: Any,
    prefix: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    inherited_required: bool = True,
    model_3d_su: bool = False,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if schema.get("x-correction-editable") is False or "const" in schema:
        return []
    schema_type = _schema_type(schema)
    properties = schema.get("properties")
    if schema_type == "object" and isinstance(properties, Mapping) and properties:
        required = {
            str(item)
            for item in schema.get("required", [])
            if isinstance(item, str)
        }
        leaves: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for raw_key, raw_child in properties.items():
            key = str(raw_key).strip()
            if not key or not isinstance(raw_child, Mapping):
                continue
            child_path = (*prefix, key)
            child_current = (
                current.get(key) if isinstance(current, Mapping) else None
            )
            leaves.extend(
                _correction_schema_leaves(
                    raw_child,
                    current=child_current,
                    prefix=child_path,
                    inherited_required=(
                        inherited_required and key in required
                    ),
                    model_3d_su=model_3d_su,
                )
            )
        return leaves

    path = ".".join(prefix)
    overrides = _schema_field_overrides(path, model_3d_su=model_3d_su)
    if overrides is None:
        return []
    node_type = {
        "string": "text",
        "integer": "integer",
        "number": "number",
        "boolean": "boolean",
        "array": "array",
        "object": "object",
    }.get(schema_type, "object")
    field: dict[str, Any] = {
        "key": path,
        "label": str(schema.get("title") or prefix[-1] if prefix else "output"),
        "description": str(
            schema.get("description")
            or f"核对提示词输出字段 {path or 'output'}"
        ),
        "type": node_type,
        "required": inherited_required,
        "value": deepcopy(current),
        "field_schema": deepcopy(dict(schema)),
    }
    enum = schema.get("enum")
    if isinstance(enum, list) and enum:
        field["type"] = "enum"
        field["options"] = deepcopy(enum)
    # 覆盖表只补 schema 没写的部分：提示词作者显式写了 title/description/enum 时，
    # 以冻结的 schema 为准，避免这里的静态表悄悄改掉某个版本的真实语义。
    if overrides:
        if not schema.get("title") and overrides.get("label"):
            field["label"] = overrides["label"]
        if (schema_type == "string" or not overrides.get("options")) and not schema.get("description") and overrides.get("description"):
            field["description"] = overrides["description"]
        if schema_type == "string" and "options" not in field and overrides.get("options"):
            field["options"] = deepcopy(overrides["options"])
            field["type"] = overrides.get("type", field["type"])
    for bound in ("minimum", "maximum", "minItems", "maxItems"):
        if bound in schema:
            field[bound] = deepcopy(schema[bound])
    if node_type in {"integer", "number"}:
        field.setdefault("minimum", -1_000_000)
        field.setdefault("maximum", 1_000_000)
    if schema_type == "array" and isinstance(schema.get("items"), Mapping):
        field["item_schema"] = deepcopy(dict(schema["items"]))
    return [field]


def correction_fields_from_output_contract(
    contract: Mapping[str, Any] | PromptOutputContract,
    result: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
    """Derive editable fields from one frozen strict prompt output contract."""

    parsed = parse_output_contract(contract)
    if parsed.mode != "json_schema":
        return (
            {
                "key": "output",
                "whole_output": True,
                "label": "提示词输出",
                "description": "核对该提示词节点生成的完整结构化结果",
                "type": "object",
                "required": False,
                "value": deepcopy(dict(result)) if isinstance(result, Mapping) else None,
            },
        )
    current = result if isinstance(result, Mapping) else {}
    # 3D/SU 由 schema 自身识别：只有它带 model_3d_su_fields 这一节。
    properties = parsed.schema.get("properties")
    model_3d_su = (
        isinstance(properties, Mapping) and "model_3d_su_fields" in properties
    )
    return tuple(
        _correction_schema_leaves(
            parsed.schema,
            current=current,
            model_3d_su=model_3d_su,
        )
    )


def prompt_output_schema_from_node(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    """Resolve the schema that actually governed a frozen prompt invocation."""

    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    raw_contract = config.get("output_contract")
    if isinstance(raw_contract, Mapping):
        parsed = parse_output_contract(raw_contract)
        if parsed.mode == "json_schema":
            return parsed.schema
    output_schema = node.get("output_schema")
    return output_schema if isinstance(output_schema, Mapping) else {}


def _prompt_output_correction_fields(
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    raw_contract = config.get("output_contract")
    if isinstance(raw_contract, Mapping):
        parsed = parse_output_contract(raw_contract)
        if parsed.mode == "json_schema":
            return tuple(
                {
                    key: deepcopy(value)
                    for key, value in field.items()
                    if key != "value"
                }
                for field in correction_fields_from_output_contract(parsed, {})
            )
    output_schema = prompt_output_schema_from_node(node)
    output_schema = output_schema if isinstance(output_schema, Mapping) else {}
    properties = output_schema.get("properties")
    if not isinstance(properties, Mapping) or not properties:
        return (
            {
                "key": "output",
                "whole_output": True,
                "label": "提示词输出",
                "description": "核对该提示词节点生成的完整结构化结果",
                "type": "object",
            },
        )
    result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for raw_key, raw_schema in properties.items():
        key = str(raw_key).strip()
        if not key:
            continue
        schema = raw_schema if isinstance(raw_schema, Mapping) else {}
        raw_type = str(schema.get("type") or "object")
        node_type = {
            "string": "text",
            "integer": "integer",
            "number": "number",
            "boolean": "boolean",
            "array": "array",
            "object": "object",
        }.get(raw_type, "object")
        field: dict[str, Any] = {
            "key": key,
            "label": str(schema.get("title") or f"提示词输出字段 {key}"),
            "description": str(
                schema.get("description") or f"核对提示词输出字段 {key}"
            ),
            "type": node_type,
            "required": key in set(output_schema.get("required") or []),
        }
        enum = schema.get("enum")
        if isinstance(enum, list) and enum:
            field["type"] = "enum"
            field["options"] = deepcopy(enum)
        if node_type in {"integer", "number"}:
            field["minimum"] = schema.get("minimum", -1_000_000)
            field["maximum"] = schema.get("maximum", 1_000_000)
        result.append(field)
    return tuple(result) or (
        {
            "key": "output",
            "whole_output": True,
            "label": "提示词输出",
            "description": "核对该提示词节点生成的完整结构化结果",
            "type": "object",
        },
    )


def _schema_output_correction_fields(
    schema: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    label: str,
    description: str,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
    """Derive editable leaves from any frozen node output schema."""

    if not isinstance(schema, Mapping):
        return (
            {
                "key": "output",
                "whole_output": True,
                "label": label,
                "description": description,
                "type": "object",
            },
        )
    fields = tuple(
        {
            key: deepcopy(value)
            for key, value in field.items()
            if key != "value"
        }
        for field in _correction_schema_leaves(schema, current={})
    )
    return fields or (
        {
            "key": "output",
            "whole_output": True,
            "label": label,
            "description": description,
            "type": "object",
        },
    )


def _frozen_contract_hash(value: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> str | None:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return None
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(
            value,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def _aggregate_case_correction_fields(
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
    """Expose deterministic aggregate facts from its frozen aggregation config."""

    fields: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for key, label in (
        ("source_total", "来源图片数"),
        ("included", "纳入聚合图片数"),
        ("succeeded", "成功图片数"),
        ("failed", "失败图片数"),
        ("skipped", "跳过图片数"),
    ):
        fields.append(
            {
                "key": f"counts.{key}",
                "label": label,
                "description": f"核对案例聚合的{label}",
                "type": "integer",
                "minimum": 0,
                "maximum": 1_000_000,
            }
        )
    for key, label in (
        ("count", "有效分数数量"),
        ("mean", "图片平均分"),
        ("minimum", "图片最低分"),
        ("maximum", "图片最高分"),
    ):
        fields.append(
            {
                "key": f"score.{key}",
                "label": label,
                "description": f"核对案例聚合的{label}",
                "type": "integer" if key == "count" else "number",
                "minimum": 0,
                "maximum": 1_000_000,
            }
        )
    feature_paths = config.get("feature_paths")
    if isinstance(feature_paths, Mapping):
        for raw_name in feature_paths:
            name = str(raw_name).strip()
            if not name:
                continue
            fields.append(
                {
                    "key": f"features.{name}.values",
                    "label": f"{name}聚合值",
                    "description": f"核对案例图片的{name}聚合值及出现次数",
                    "type": "array",
                }
            )
    fields.extend(
        (
            {
                "key": "results",
                "label": "逐图聚合明细",
                "description": "核对纳入案例聚合的逐图结果",
                "type": "array",
            },
            {
                "key": "failures",
                "label": "逐图失败明细",
                "description": "核对未成功纳入案例聚合的逐图失败结果",
                "type": "array",
            },
        )
    )
    return tuple(fields)


def _workflow_topological_order(
    nodes: list[Mapping[str, Any]], edges: list[Mapping[str, Any]]
) -> list[str]:
    keys = [_workflow_node_identity(node) for node in nodes]
    keys = [key for key in keys if key]
    incoming = {key: 0 for key in keys}
    outgoing = {key: [] for key in keys}
    for edge in edges:
        source = str(edge.get("from") or "")
        target = str(edge.get("to") or "")
        if source in outgoing and target in incoming:
            outgoing[source].append(target)
            incoming[target] += 1
    ready = [key for key in keys if incoming[key] == 0]
    order: list[str] = []
    while ready:
        current = ready.pop(0)
        order.append(current)
        for target in outgoing[current]:
            incoming[target] -= 1
            if incoming[target] == 0:
                ready.append(target)
    return order if len(order) == len(keys) else keys


def _workflow_module_content(config: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """取出规则模块冻结在工作流快照里的内容。

    组件快照把内容存成 content_json 字符串，直接读 content 会拿到空。
    """

    snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
    if not isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
        return {}
    raw = snapshot.get("content_json")
    if isinstance(raw, str) and raw.strip():
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(raw)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            return {}
        return parsed if isinstance(parsed, dict) else {}
    content = snapshot.get("content")
    return dict(content) if isinstance(content, Mapping) else {}


def _graded_bundle_from_workflow(
    nodes: list[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    spec_version: str,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """把等级评测类工作流的冻结规则整理成按字段呈现所需的合同形状。

    准入红线、赛道识别、质量上限、等级撮合四个模块冻结下来的内容，与历史合同
    里 redline_policy / track_classification / common_modifiers / level_scale
    四段是同构的，所以直接整理成同一形状，复用既有的节点构建器，不另写一套。
    没有等级刻度就不是按等级评测的工作流（例如知识图谱那种按字段抽取的），返回 None。
    """

    contents: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for node in nodes:
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
            continue
        module_type_key = config.get("module_type_key")
        if not isinstance(module_type_key, str) or not module_type_key:
            continue
        contents.setdefault(module_type_key, _workflow_module_content(config))

    matcher = contents.get("score_level_matcher") or {}
    levels = matcher.get("levels")
    if not isinstance(levels, list) or not levels:
        return None

    contract: dict[str, Any] = {
        "spec_version": spec_version,
        "level_scale": {"levels": deepcopy(levels)},
    }
    tracks = (contents.get("track_routing") or {}).get("tracks")
    if isinstance(tracks, list) and tracks:
        contract["track_classification"] = {"tracks": deepcopy(tracks)}
    veto = (contents.get("quality_cap_exception") or {}).get("high_score_veto")
    if isinstance(veto, Mapping):
        contract["common_modifiers"] = {"high_score_veto": deepcopy(dict(veto))}
    redline = contents.get("eligibility_redline") or {}
    if isinstance(redline.get("rules"), list):
        contract["redline_policy"] = deepcopy(redline)
    return {"contract": contract}


def _graded_field_panel_nodes(
    nodes: list[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    spec_version: str,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """为按字段呈现的复核页补齐可纠偏节点。

    原生工作流的合同以流程节点为单位（workflow.prompt_a.output 这类），而等级
    评测的复核页是按「调用A字段 / 调用B美感分 / 等级撮合」三段呈现的，两套键名
    对不上，页面就会整片显示「未纳入本轮纠偏合同」、一项都改不了。这里按同一份
    冻结规则补出三段各自的节点，键名沿用复核页既有的约定。
    """

    for node in nodes:
        kind = str(node.get("kind") or "")
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        component_config = (
            config.get("item_executor")
            if kind == "foreach_image"
            else config
        )
        component_config = (
            component_config
            if isinstance(component_config, Mapping)
            else {}
        )
        output_schema = (
            component_config.get("output_schema")
            if kind == "foreach_image"
            else node.get("output_schema")
        )
        properties = (
            output_schema.get("properties")
            if isinstance(output_schema, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        if kind in {"prompt", "foreach_image"} and (
            isinstance(component_config.get("output_contract"), Mapping)
            or isinstance(properties, Mapping) and bool(properties)
        ):
            # Current workflows are driven entirely by their frozen node schemas.
            # The fixed A/B/V3 panels remain read compatibility for old snapshots
            # only; adding them here would duplicate or invent fields.
            return []

    bundle = _graded_bundle_from_workflow(nodes, spec_version=spec_version)
    if bundle is None:
        return []
    return [
        *_production_field_nodes(None, v3_bundle=bundle),
        *_judgement_nodes(bundle),
        *_v3_nodes(bundle),
    ]


def freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(
    workflow_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Project a workflow-v2 run snapshot into an adaptive correction contract.

    The projection follows the frozen DAG and selected asset identities. Prompt,
    rule-contract, and level-matcher outputs accept append-only human overrides;
    connector and routing nodes remain evidence-only. No active registry lookup is
    used, so prompt C/D and future workflow nodes remain historically reproducible.
    """

    from .knowledge_graph_contracts import knowledge_graph_contract_summary

    workflow = workflow_snapshot.get("workflow")
    workflow = workflow if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else {}
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest")
    manifest = manifest if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else {}
    category_key = str(manifest.get("category_key") or "")
    raw_nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    raw_nodes = raw_nodes if isinstance(raw_nodes, list) else []
    nodes = [node for node in raw_nodes if isinstance(node, Mapping)]
    raw_edges = manifest.get("edges")
    edges = (
        [edge for edge in raw_edges if isinstance(edge, Mapping)]
        if isinstance(raw_edges, list)
        else []
    )
    order = _workflow_topological_order(nodes, edges)
    order_by_key = {key: index for index, key in enumerate(order)}
    prompts = {
        str(item.get("reference")): item
        for item in workflow_snapshot.get("prompts", [])
        if isinstance(item, Mapping) and item.get("reference")
    }
    models = {
        int(item["model_registry_id"]): item
        for item in workflow_snapshot.get("models", [])
        if isinstance(item, Mapping)
        and isinstance(item.get("model_registry_id"), int)
    }
    rules = {
        int(item["rule_revision_id"]): item
        for item in workflow_snapshot.get("rules", [])
        if isinstance(item, Mapping)
        and isinstance(item.get("rule_revision_id"), int)
    }
    labels = {
        "upstream_connector": "读取上游数据",
        "prompt": "AI 提示词调用",
        "foreach_image": "逐图片提示词调用",
        "aggregate_case": "聚合案例图片结果",
        "rule_contract": "规则与红线判断",
        "level_matcher": "等级撮合",
        "downstream_projection": "写入下游",
        "branch": "分成多条路线",
        "join": "汇合多条路线",
    }
    is_v3 = manifest.get("schema_version") == "workflow-v3"
    correctable_kinds = {
        "prompt",
        "foreach_image",
        "rule_contract",
        "level_matcher",
        "rule_module",
    }
    outgoing: dict[str, list[str]] = {
        _workflow_node_identity(node): [] for node in nodes
    }
    for edge in edges:
        source = edge.get("from")
        target = edge.get("to")
        if isinstance(source, str) and isinstance(target, str):
            outgoing.setdefault(source, []).append(target)
    prompt_node_ids = {
        _workflow_node_identity(node)
        for node in nodes
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt"
    }

    def has_paid_prompt_descendant(node_key: str) -> bool:
        pending = list(outgoing.get(node_key, ()))
        visited: set[str] = set()
        while pending:
            current = pending.pop()
            if current in visited:
                continue
            visited.add(current)
            if current in prompt_node_ids:
                return True
            pending.extend(outgoing.get(current, ()))
        return False
    contract_nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for fallback_order, raw_node in enumerate(nodes):
        key = _workflow_node_identity(raw_node)
        kind = str(raw_node.get("kind") or "").strip()
        if not key or not kind:
            continue
        config = raw_node.get("config")
        config = dict(config) if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        editable = kind in correctable_kinds
        metadata: dict[str, Any] = {
            "workflow_node_key": key,
            "node_instance_id": key,
            "workflow_kind": kind,
            "workflow_order": order_by_key.get(key, fallback_order),
            "workflow_display_name": str(
                raw_node.get("display_name")
                or labels.get(kind)
                or f"流程节点 {key}"
            ),
            "editable": editable,
            "selected_parameters": {
                field: deepcopy(config[field])
                for field in (
                    "prompt_ref",
                    "prompt_version_id",
                    "model_registry_id",
                    "max_tokens",
                    "thinking_level",
                    "thinking_enabled",
                    "temperature",
                    "structured_output",
                    "output_contract",
                    "contract_ref",
                    "matcher_ref",
                    "rule_revision_id",
                    "rule_module_version_id",
                    "input_bindings",
                    "scope",
                    "route_condition",
                    "connector_key",
                    "output_contract_key",
                    "field_contract_key",
                )
                if field in config
            },
        }
        if editable and has_paid_prompt_descendant(key):
            metadata["paid_rerun_required"] = True
        if kind == "aggregate_case":
            metadata["editable"] = False
            metadata["read_only_reason"] = (
                "案例聚合身份、计数、哈希和派生统计由运行时重算，只能查看"
            )
        if kind == "prompt":
            thinking_enabled = config.get("thinking_enabled") is True
            metadata["selected_parameters"]["thinking_enabled"] = (
                thinking_enabled
            )
            metadata["selected_parameters"]["thinking_level"] = (
                config.get("thinking_level") if thinking_enabled else "none"
            )
        prompt_ref = config.get("prompt_ref")
        if isinstance(prompt_ref, str):
            metadata["prompt_ref"] = prompt_ref
            if prompt_ref in prompts:
                metadata["prompt_version"] = _safe_workflow_asset(prompts[prompt_ref])
        model_id = config.get("model_registry_id")
        if isinstance(model_id, int):
            metadata["model_registry_id"] = model_id
            if model_id in models:
                metadata["model_version"] = _safe_workflow_asset(models[model_id])
        rule_id = config.get("rule_revision_id")
        if isinstance(rule_id, int):
            metadata["rule_revision_id"] = rule_id
            if rule_id in rules:
                metadata["rule_version"] = _safe_workflow_asset(rules[rule_id])
        component_snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
        if isinstance(component_snapshot, Mapping):
            component_version = {
                str(field): deepcopy(component_snapshot[field])
                for field in (
                    "kind",
                    "version_id",
                    "version",
                    "module_type_key",
                    "content_hash",
                )
                if field in component_snapshot
            }
            metadata["component_version"] = component_version
            if kind == "prompt":
                metadata["prompt_version"] = component_version
            elif kind == "rule_module":
                metadata["rule_version"] = component_version
        model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot")
        if isinstance(model_snapshot, Mapping):
            metadata["model_version"] = _safe_workflow_asset(model_snapshot)
        output_contract = knowledge_graph_contract_summary(
            config.get("output_contract_snapshot")
        )
        if output_contract is not None:
            metadata["output_contract"] = output_contract
        prompt_output_contract = None
        frozen_output_contract_hash = None
        if kind == "prompt" and isinstance(config.get("output_contract"), Mapping):
            frozen_output_contract_hash = _frozen_contract_hash(
                config.get("output_contract")
            )
            parsed_prompt_contract = parse_output_contract(
                config.get("output_contract")
            )
            prompt_output_contract = parsed_prompt_contract.model_dump(
                mode="json",
                by_alias=True,
            )
            metadata["prompt_output_contract"] = prompt_output_contract
        elif kind == "foreach_image":
            item_executor = config.get("item_executor")
            item_executor = (
                item_executor if isinstance(item_executor, Mapping) else {}
            )
            item_contract = item_executor.get("output_contract")
            frozen_output_contract_hash = _frozen_contract_hash(
                item_contract if isinstance(item_contract, Mapping) else None
            )
        elif kind == "aggregate_case":
            aggregate_contract = {
                "kind": "aggregate_case",
                "config": deepcopy(config),
                **(
                    {"output_schema": deepcopy(raw_node["output_schema"])}
                    if isinstance(raw_node.get("output_schema"), Mapping)
                    else {}
                ),
            }
            frozen_output_contract_hash = _frozen_contract_hash(
                aggregate_contract
            )
        elif isinstance(raw_node.get("output_schema"), Mapping):
            frozen_output_contract_hash = _frozen_contract_hash(
                raw_node.get("output_schema")
            )
        if frozen_output_contract_hash is None and output_contract is not None:
            raw_hash = output_contract.get("contract_hash")
            frozen_output_contract_hash = (
                str(raw_hash) if isinstance(raw_hash, str) and raw_hash else None
            )
        if frozen_output_contract_hash is not None:
            metadata["output_contract_hash"] = frozen_output_contract_hash
        module_type_key = config.get("module_type_key")
        if kind == "rule_module" and isinstance(module_type_key, str):
            metadata["module_type_key"] = module_type_key
        if not editable and kind != "aggregate_case":
            metadata["read_only_reason"] = "接口或流程控制节点只展示运行证据，不能改写"
        identity_payload = {
            "kind": kind,
            "prompt_ref": config.get("prompt_ref"),
            "prompt_version_id": config.get("prompt_version_id"),
            "model_registry_id": config.get("model_registry_id"),
            "rule_revision_id": config.get("rule_revision_id"),
            "rule_module_version_id": config.get("rule_module_version_id"),
            "output_contract_hash": (
                frozen_output_contract_hash
            ),
            "prompt_output_contract": prompt_output_contract,
            "component_hash": (
                component_snapshot.get("content_hash")
                if isinstance(component_snapshot, Mapping)
                else None
            ),
        }
        compatibility_hash = hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps(
                identity_payload,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                sort_keys=True,
                separators=(",", ":"),
            ).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()[:16]
        layer = (
            "V3"
            if kind in {"rule_contract", "level_matcher", "rule_module"}
            else "A"
        )
        if is_v3 and kind == "prompt":
            field_specs = _prompt_output_correction_fields(raw_node)
        elif is_v3 and kind == "foreach_image":
            item_executor = config.get("item_executor")
            item_executor = (
                item_executor if isinstance(item_executor, Mapping) else {}
            )
            field_specs = _prompt_output_correction_fields(
                {
                    "config": item_executor,
                    "output_schema": item_executor.get("output_schema", {}),
                }
            )
        elif is_v3 and kind == "aggregate_case":
            output_schema = raw_node.get("output_schema")
            output_properties = (
                output_schema.get("properties")
                if isinstance(output_schema, Mapping)
                else None
            )
            field_specs = (
                _schema_output_correction_fields(
                    output_schema,
                    label="案例聚合输出",
                    description="核对该案例聚合节点的完整冻结结果",
                )
                if isinstance(output_properties, Mapping) and output_properties
                else _aggregate_case_correction_fields(config)
            )
        elif is_v3 and kind == "rule_module":
            output_schema = raw_node.get("output_schema")
            output_properties = (
                output_schema.get("properties")
                if isinstance(output_schema, Mapping)
                else None
            )
            field_specs = (
                _schema_output_correction_fields(
                    output_schema,
                    label="规则输出",
                    description="核对该规则模块的完整判断结果",
                )
                if isinstance(output_properties, Mapping) and output_properties
                else _RULE_MODULE_CORRECTION_FIELDS.get(
                    str(module_type_key),
                    (
                        {
                            "key": "output",
                            "label": "规则输出",
                            "description": "核对该规则模块的完整判断结果",
                            "type": "object",
                        },
                    ),
                )
            )
        else:
            field_specs = (
                {
                    "key": None,
                    "label": str(
                        raw_node.get("display_name")
                        or labels.get(kind)
                        or f"流程节点 {key}"
                    ),
                    "description": (
                        "逐条核对该节点的冻结输出，并记录人工改正与证据"
                        if editable
                        else "查看该节点在本轮运行中的输入、输出与状态证据"
                    ),
                    "type": "object",
                },
            )
        for field_order, field_spec in enumerate(field_specs):
            field_key = field_spec.get("key")
            node_metadata = deepcopy(metadata)
            if is_v3 and kind == "prompt" and isinstance(field_key, str):
                node_metadata["output_field_key"] = field_key
                field_schema = field_spec.get("field_schema")
                if isinstance(field_schema, Mapping):
                    node_metadata["output_field_schema"] = deepcopy(
                        dict(field_schema)
                    )
                item_schema = field_spec.get("item_schema")
                if isinstance(item_schema, Mapping):
                    node_metadata["output_array_item_schema"] = deepcopy(
                        dict(item_schema)
                    )
            if is_v3 and kind == "foreach_image" and isinstance(field_key, str):
                node_metadata["foreach_field_template"] = True
                node_metadata["foreach_item_field_path"] = field_key
                field_schema = field_spec.get("field_schema")
                if isinstance(field_schema, Mapping):
                    node_metadata["output_field_schema"] = deepcopy(
                        dict(field_schema)
                    )
                item_schema = field_spec.get("item_schema")
                if isinstance(item_schema, Mapping):
                    node_metadata["output_array_item_schema"] = deepcopy(
                        dict(item_schema)
                    )
            if is_v3 and kind == "aggregate_case" and isinstance(field_key, str):
                node_metadata["output_field_key"] = field_key
            if is_v3 and kind == "rule_module" and isinstance(field_key, str):
                node_metadata["module_field_key"] = field_key
            field_identity_path = (
                field_key
                if isinstance(field_key, str)
                and field_spec.get("whole_output") is not True
                else "output"
            )
            if frozen_output_contract_hash is not None:
                node_metadata["field_identity"] = {
                    "node_instance_id": key,
                    "output_contract_hash": frozen_output_contract_hash,
                    "field_path": field_identity_path,
                    **({"item_key": "*"} if kind == "foreach_image" else {}),
                }
            if kind == "foreach_image" and isinstance(field_key, str):
                suffix = f".items[*].result.{field_key}"
                path_suffix = suffix
            else:
                suffix = f".{field_key}" if isinstance(field_key, str) else ""
                path_suffix = "" if field_spec.get("whole_output") is True else suffix
            contract_node = {
                "node_key": f"workflow.{key}{suffix}",
                "layer": layer,
                "path": (
                    f"workflow.{key}.output{path_suffix}"
                    if kind != "foreach_image"
                    else f"workflow.{key}.output.items[*].result.{field_key}"
                ),
                "order": order_by_key.get(key, fallback_order) * 100
                + field_order,
                "label": str(field_spec["label"]),
                "description": str(field_spec["description"]),
                "type": str(field_spec["type"]),
                "semantic_version": str(workflow.get("version") or "1"),
                "compatibility_key": (
                    f"workflow:{key}:{field_key or 'output'}:"
                    f"{compatibility_hash}"
                ),
                "required": bool(field_spec.get("required", False)),
                "evidence": {
                    "description": "请填写能定位到素材或规则判断的人工证据",
                    "required": editable,
                },
                "metadata": node_metadata,
            }
            for bound in (
                "options",
                "minimum",
                "maximum",
                "minItems",
                "maxItems",
            ):
                if bound in field_spec:
                    contract_node[bound] = deepcopy(field_spec[bound])
            if layer == "V3":
                contract_node["recompute_ref"] = (
                    f"workflow-human-override-v1:{key}:"
                    f"{field_key or 'output'}"
                )
            contract_nodes.append(contract_node)

    contract_nodes.extend(
        _graded_field_panel_nodes(
            nodes, spec_version=str(workflow.get("version") or "1")
        )
    )

    contract = normalize_correction_contract(
        {
            "contract_version": "workflow-correction-contract-v1",
            "category_key": category_key,
            "nodes": contract_nodes,
            "workflow": {
                "workflow_definition_id": workflow.get("workflow_definition_id"),
                "workflow_version_id": workflow.get("workflow_version_id"),
                "version": workflow.get("version"),
                "canonical_hash": workflow.get("canonical_hash"),
            },
            "sources": {
                "workflow_nodes": [
                    {
                        "key": _workflow_node_identity(node),
                        "kind": str(node.get("kind") or ""),
                        "display_name": node.get("display_name"),
                    }
                    for node in nodes
                ],
                "workflow_edges": deepcopy(edges),
            },
        },
        category_key=category_key,
    )
    contract["contract_hash"] = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    return contract


def _safe_workflow_asset(asset: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    secret_fields = {
        "system_prompt",
        "user_prompt",
        "encrypted_api_key",
        "api_key",
    }
    return {
        str(key): deepcopy(value)
        for key, value in asset.items()
        if str(key).lower() not in secret_fields
    }


def freeze_contract_from_execution_snapshot(
    *, category_key: str, execution_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build a contract from a frozen category execution snapshot.

    The execution snapshot is the only source of truth here.  An explicitly
    embedded contract wins; otherwise the adapter derives the form nodes from
    the frozen production-field, dimension, and V3 blocks.  No active database
    configuration is consulted as a compatibility shortcut.
    """

    explicit = execution_snapshot.get("correction_contract")
    if isinstance(explicit, Mapping) and isinstance(explicit.get("nodes"), list):
        normalized = normalize_correction_contract(explicit, category_key=category_key)
        normalized["contract_hash"] = correction_contract_hash(normalized)
        return normalized

    embedded_workflow = execution_snapshot.get("workflow_snapshot")
    if not isinstance(embedded_workflow, Mapping):
        embedded_workflow = (
            execution_snapshot
            if isinstance(execution_snapshot.get("workflow"), Mapping)
            else None
        )
    if isinstance(embedded_workflow, Mapping):
        return freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(embedded_workflow)

    pipeline = execution_snapshot.get("pipeline_config")
    if not isinstance(pipeline, Mapping):
        pipeline = {}
    dimension_contract = execution_snapshot.get("dimension_contract")
    if not isinstance(dimension_contract, Mapping):
        dimension_contract = {}
    dimension_definition = dimension_contract.get("definition")
    if not isinstance(dimension_definition, Mapping):
        dimension_definition = execution_snapshot.get("dimension_selection")
    if not isinstance(dimension_definition, Mapping):
        dimension_definition = {}
    production_fields = pipeline.get("production_fields")
    if not isinstance(production_fields, Mapping):
        production_fields = {}
    v3 = execution_snapshot.get("v3_authoritative_bundle")
    if not isinstance(v3, Mapping):
        v3 = {}
    selection = execution_snapshot.get("dimension_selection")
    selected_keys = None
    if isinstance(selection, Mapping) and selection.get("mode") != "none":
        effective_keys = selection.get("effective_keys")
        if isinstance(effective_keys, list):
            selected_keys = [str(item) for item in effective_keys if str(item)]

    production_nodes = _production_field_nodes(
        production_fields,
        v3_bundle=v3,
    )
    dimension_nodes = _dimension_rule_nodes(
        dimension_definition,
        v3,
        selected_keys=selected_keys,
    )
    v3_nodes = _v3_nodes(v3)
    # Model judgements that drive the score but had no correctable node before:
    # the hard-defect list (layer A) and the Call-B aesthetic score (layer B).
    # Each node carries its own layer, so appending them here keeps the frozen
    # snapshot grouping intact.
    for node in _judgement_nodes(v3):
        if node["layer"] == "B":
            dimension_nodes.append(node)
        else:
            production_nodes.append(node)
    prompt_snapshot = {
        "stage": "A",
        "version": execution_snapshot.get("rubric_version") or "frozen",
        "nodes": [],
    }
    contract = freeze_correction_contract(
        category_key=category_key,
        prompt_snapshot=prompt_snapshot,
        dimension_snapshot={"nodes": dimension_nodes},
        production_field_snapshot={"nodes": production_nodes},
        v3_snapshot={"nodes": v3_nodes},
    )
    # Keep the complete frozen source payload for audit/debugging while the
    # browser receives only normalized, executable-safe nodes.
    contract["sources"] = {
        "prompt": deepcopy(dict(prompt_snapshot)),
        "dimensions": deepcopy(dict(dimension_contract)),
        "production_fields": deepcopy(dict(production_fields)),
        "v3": deepcopy(dict(v3)),
    }
    contract["contract_hash"] = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    return contract

===== FILE backend/app/correction_deductions.py =====
"""Read-only score explanations and links to the actual frozen prompt inputs."""

import json
from collections.abc import Mapping


LABELS = {
    "model_detail": "模型细节", "material_rendering": "质感渲染",
    "lighting": "光感表现", "design_trend": "设计感及流行度",
    "visual_composition": "视觉构图",
}


def object_value(value):
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, str):
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(value)
            return parsed if isinstance(parsed, dict) else {}
        except ValueError:
            pass
    return {}


def frozen_dimensions(snapshot, track):
    root = object_value(snapshot)
    root = object_value(root.get("workflow_snapshot", root))
    root = object_value(root.get("workflow", root))
    manifest = object_value(root.get("manifest", root))
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        config = object_value(node.get("config"))
        if config.get("module_type_key") != "dimension_scoring":
            continue
        component = object_value(config.get("component_snapshot"))
        content = object_value(component.get("content_json", component.get("content")))
        tracks = object_value(content.get("subcategory_dimensions"))
        selected = object_value(tracks.get(track))
        definitions = {}
        for group_name in ("common_group", "specific_group"):
            group = object_value(selected.get(group_name))
            schema = object_value(group.get("schema_definition"))
            for dimension in schema.get("dimensions", []):
                if isinstance(dimension, dict) and dimension.get("key"):
                    definitions[dimension["key"]] = dimension
        return definitions
    return {}


def derived_deduction_node(node, scoring):
    """These module outputs are explanatory projections, not scoring inputs."""
    evidence = object_value(scoring.get("dimension_evidence"))
    metadata = object_value(node.get("metadata"))
    return (
        evidence.get("mode") == "rule_deduction"
        and bool(evidence.get("dimension_evidence"))
        and metadata.get("module_type_key") in {"dimension_scoring", "anchor_calibration"}
    )


def annotate_deductions(nodes, scoring, snapshot):
    evidence = object_value(scoring.get("dimension_evidence"))
    details = object_value(evidence.get("dimension_evidence"))
    if evidence.get("mode") != "rule_deduction" or not details:
        return
    definitions = frozen_dimensions(snapshot, scoring.get("track_key"))
    targets = {}
    for node in nodes:
        metadata = object_value(node.get("metadata"))
        path = str(metadata.get("output_field_key", ""))
        if metadata.get("workflow_kind") != "prompt":
            continue
        for key in details:
            if path == f"dimensions.{key}.hit_rules":
                targets[key] = node["node_key"]
                rules = definitions.get(key, {}).get("deduction_rules", [])
                if rules:
                    node.setdefault("metadata", {})["deduction_rule_options"] = [
                        {"rule_id": rule["rule_id"], "description": rule.get("description", ""),
                         "deduction": rule.get("deduction")}
                        for rule in rules if isinstance(rule, dict) and rule.get("rule_id")
                    ]
    rows = []
    for key, detail in details.items():
        if not isinstance(detail, Mapping):
            continue
        definition = definitions.get(key, {})
        rules = {rule.get("rule_id"): rule for rule in definition.get("deduction_rules", []) if isinstance(rule, dict)}
        rows.append({
            "key": key, "label": definition.get("label") or LABELS.get(key, key),
            "share": detail.get("share"), "raw_deduction": detail.get("raw_rule_deduction"),
            "applied_deduction": detail.get("applied_rule_deduction"),
            "point_deduction": detail.get("point_deduction"),
            "cap_applied": bool(detail.get("cap_applied")),
            "cap_reason": detail.get("cap_reason"),
            "hits": [{**dict(hit), "description": rules.get(hit.get("rule_id"), {}).get("description"),
                      "deduction": rules.get(hit.get("rule_id"), {}).get("deduction")}
                     for hit in detail.get("hit_rules", []) if isinstance(hit, Mapping)],
            "correction_node_key": targets.get(key),
        })
    for node in nodes:
        if not derived_deduction_node(node, scoring):
            continue
        node.update(editable=False, read_only=True,
                    read_only_reason="此项由冻结规则计算；请纠偏调用 B 的规则命中或美感初始分")
        metadata = node.setdefault("metadata", {})
        field = metadata.get("module_field_key") or metadata.get("output_field_key")
        if metadata.get("module_type_key") == "dimension_scoring" and field == "dimensions":
            metadata["deduction_breakdown"] = {
                "initial_score": evidence.get("initial_score"),
                "total_deduction": evidence.get("applied_deduction_total"), "rows": rows,
            }
        if metadata.get("module_type_key") == "anchor_calibration" and field == "calibrated_score":
            # The legacy presentation fell back to B's pre-deduction score and
            # called that an anchor calibration. Do not invent such a step.
            node["label"] = "美感初始分（扣分前）"
            node["description"] = "此值来自调用 B，不代表另行执行过锚点校准"
            node["current_value"] = evidence.get("initial_score")
        if metadata.get("module_type_key") == "anchor_calibration" and field == "reason":
            node["current_value"] = "美感初始分来自调用 B；维度扣分明细见上方，未记录独立锚点校准步骤"

===== FILE backend/app/correction_notes.py =====
"""Append-only operator notes, separate from value corrections and reruns."""
import hashlib
import json

from sqlalchemy import select

from .api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat
from .audit import append_audit_event
from .models import AuditEvent, EvaluationResult


def correction_notes(db, *, run_id, item_id):
    if db is None:
        return []
    rows = list(db.scalars(select(AuditEvent).where(
        AuditEvent.category == 'baseline_regression',
        AuditEvent.action == 'human_note_added',
        AuditEvent.subject_type == 'baseline_regression_item',
        AuditEvent.subject_id == str(item_id),
    ).order_by(AuditEvent.id.desc()).limit(50)))
    return [{'id': row.id, 'text': payload['text'], 'actor': row.actor,
             'created_at': platform_isoformat(row.created_at)}
            for row in reversed(rows)
            if (payload := json.loads(row.payload_json)).get('run_id') == run_id]


def append_correction_note(db, *, run, item, text, idempotency_key, actor):
    from .correction_view import CorrectionViewError

    text = text.strip()
    if not text or len(text) > 2000:
        raise CorrectionViewError(422, 'CORRECTION_NOTE_INVALID', '备注须为1至2000字')
    if not idempotency_key or len(idempotency_key) > 160:
        raise CorrectionViewError(422, 'CORRECTION_IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID', '备注幂等键无效')
    if item.run_id != run.id or not item.evaluation_id:
        raise CorrectionViewError(409, 'CORRECTION_RESULT_UNAVAILABLE', '该条目尚无评测结果')
    # Serialize duplicate retries without modifying the evaluation or its revision.
    db.scalar(select(EvaluationResult).where(
        EvaluationResult.id == item.evaluation_id).with_for_update())
    key = 'baseline-note:' + hashlib.sha256(
        f'{run.id}:{item.id}:{idempotency_key}'.encode()).hexdigest()
    payload = {'run_id': run.id, 'text': text}
    prior = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == key))
    if prior is not None and (json.loads(prior.payload_json) != payload or prior.actor != actor):
        raise CorrectionViewError(409, 'CORRECTION_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT', '该幂等键已用于其他备注')
    append_audit_event(db, category='baseline_regression', action='human_note_added',
                      subject_type='baseline_regression_item', subject_id=item.id,
                      actor=actor, payload=payload, event_key=key)
    return {'notes': correction_notes(db, run_id=run.id, item_id=item.id),
            'idempotent_replay': prior is not None}

===== FILE backend/app/correction_publication.py =====
"""Read-only publication evidence attached to an operator's correction view."""
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_text
import json
from sqlalchemy import select
from .models import ProductionRun, ContentIngressEvent, ContentRecord, RuntimeOutboxEvent


def _json(value, fallback):
    try:
        return json.loads(value or "null") or fallback
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return fallback


def correction_publication_state(db, evaluation):
    run_id = getattr(evaluation, "production_run_id", None)
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id) if db is not None and run_id else None
    if run is None or run.source_type != "content_ingress_event":
        return None
    snapshot = _json(read_run_snapshot_text(run), {})
    source_version = snapshot.get("runtime_context", {}).get("input_manifest", {}).get("source_version")
    event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, run.source_id)
    record = db.get(ContentRecord, event.content_record_id) if event else None
    current = bool(record and record.status != "deleted" and record.source_version == source_version)
    history = _json(evaluation.correction_history_json, [])
    last = next((item for item in reversed(history) if isinstance(item, dict) and
        (item.get("downstream_publish_status") or item.get("downstream_republish_event_ids"))), {})
    ids = last.get("downstream_republish_event_ids") or []
    ids = [value for value in ids if isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) and value > 0]
    rows = list(db.scalars(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id.in_(ids)))) if ids else []
    if not current or last.get("downstream_publish_status") == "superseded_source_not_published":
        status = "superseded_source_not_published"
    elif not ids:
        status = "not_published"
    elif len(rows) != len(ids):
        status = "unknown"
    elif any(row.status in {"dead_lettered", "canceled"} for row in rows):
        status = "failed"
    elif all(row.status == "delivered" for row in rows):
        status = "delivered"
    else:
        status = "queued"
    return {"scope": "source_version", "source_version": source_version,
        "current_source_version": record.source_version if record else None,
        "is_current_source": current, "status": status, "event_ids": ids,
        "read_back_verified": bool(rows) and all(row.status == "delivered" and
            _json(row.delivery_receipt_json, {}).get("read_back_matched") is True for row in rows)}

===== FILE backend/app/correction_view.py =====
"""Contract-driven correction views and append-only submissions."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .field_review_policy import resolve_field_review, review_summary
from .correction_contract import (
    ContractValidationError,
    SUPPORTED_CORRECTION_NODE_TYPES,
    allowed_is_single_values,
    correction_contract_hash,
    inherit_correction_node,
    validate_correction_contract,
    validate_node_value,
)
from .node_correction_api import CorrectNodeRequest, apply_node_correction
from .baseline_regression import persist_human_level_truth
from .baseline_truth import BaselineTruthError, resolve_effective_baseline_truth
from .models import ProductionRun
from .workflow_node_executors import redact_workflow_secrets
from .api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat
from .correction_deductions import annotate_deductions, derived_deduction_node


_EXECUTABLE_METADATA_KEYS = {
    "code",
    "expression",
    "executable",
    "javascript",
    "python",
    "rule_code",
    "threshold_override",
}

_TABLE_METADATA_KEYS = {
    "editable", "field_identity", "paid_rerun_required", "option_labels",
    "output_array_item_schema", "deduction_rule_options", "frozen_value",
    "node_instance_id", "output_field_key",
}
# Used while deriving values/read-only state; the final table projection removes
# these too. Full contract validation always precedes this presentation filter.
_TABLE_DERIVATION_KEYS = {
    "archived_value", "steps", "workflow_kind", "module_type_key", "module_field_key",
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _ViewContract:
    run: Any
    contract: dict[str, Any] | None
    expected_hash: str | None
    source: str
    actual_hash: str | None
    errors: tuple[str, ...]
    display: dict[str, Any] | None


def _prepare_view_contract(run: Any) -> _ViewContract:
    contract, expected, source = _run_contract(run)
    if contract is None:
        return _ViewContract(run, None, expected, source, None, (), None)
    from .correction_contract import correction_display_contract
    return _ViewContract(
        run, contract, expected, source, correction_contract_hash(contract),
        tuple(validate_correction_contract(contract, presentation_overlay=True)),
        correction_display_contract(contract),
    )


class CorrectionViewError(ValueError):
    """Stable error returned by every correction-view write boundary."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        status_code: int,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        fields: list[str] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status_code = status_code
        self.code = code
        self.fields = list(fields or [])

    def detail(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {"code": self.code, "message": str(self)}
        if self.fields:
            payload["fields"] = self.fields
        return payload


def _json_object(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return deepcopy(dict(value))
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        return {}
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "{}")
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        return {}
    return parsed if isinstance(parsed, dict) else {}


def _json_list(value: Any) -> list[Any]:
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return deepcopy(value)
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        return []
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "[]")
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        return []
    return parsed if isinstance(parsed, list) else []


def _canonical_json(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        value,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )
