def _run_contract(run: Any) -> tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, str | None, str]:
    contract = _json_object(getattr(run, "correction_contract_json", None))
    source = "frozen"
    if not contract:
        execution = _json_object(getattr(run, "execution_snapshot_json", None))
        embedded = execution.get("correction_contract")
        contract = deepcopy(dict(embedded)) if isinstance(embedded, Mapping) else {}
        source = "embedded_frozen" if contract else "legacy_missing"
    if not contract:
        return None, None, source
    expected_hash = getattr(run, "correction_contract_hash", None)
    if not isinstance(expected_hash, str) or not expected_hash:
        embedded_hash = contract.get("contract_hash")
        expected_hash = embedded_hash if isinstance(embedded_hash, str) else None
    return contract, expected_hash, source


def _safe_value(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {
            str(key): _safe_value(item)
            for key, item in value.items()
            if str(key).lower() not in _EXECUTABLE_METADATA_KEYS
        }
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_safe_value(item) for item in value]
    return deepcopy(value)


def _safe_metadata(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    safe = _safe_value(value)
    return safe if isinstance(safe, dict) else {}


def _node_steps(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    metadata = node.get("metadata")
    metadata = metadata if isinstance(metadata, Mapping) else {}
    raw = node.get("steps", metadata.get("steps"))
    if isinstance(raw, list):
        steps = [str(item).strip() for item in raw if str(item).strip()]
        if steps:
            return steps
    if node.get("layer") == "V3":
        return [
            "读取本轮冻结合同与当前节点证据",
            "由服务端权威评分引擎重新计算",
            "返回新的分数、等级和规则路径",
        ]
    return []


def _human_entry(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        result = deepcopy(dict(value))
        if "human_value" not in result and "new_value" in result:
            result["human_value"] = deepcopy(result["new_value"])
        return result
    return {"human_value": deepcopy(value)}


def build_correction_nodes(
    contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    model_values: Mapping[str, Any],
    human_values: Mapping[str, Any],
    previous_values: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    current_values: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    review_context: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
    _table: bool = False,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Materialize UI-safe nodes in the exact order frozen by the run.

    ``review_context`` 提供类目与抽检盐值时，每个字段节点会带上人工校验强度
    （必看 / 抽检命中 / 免检），供纠偏页分层展示——运营不必再逐字段确认。
    """

    previous_values = previous_values or {}
    current_values = current_values or {}
    result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    raw_nodes = contract.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(raw_nodes, list):
        return result
    for raw in raw_nodes:
        if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
            continue
        if _table:
            metadata = raw.get("metadata")
            metadata = metadata if isinstance(metadata, Mapping) else {}
            if metadata.get("editable") is False or raw.get("read_only"):
                continue
            raw = {
                **{key: value for key, value in raw.items() if key != "execution_evidence"},
                "metadata": {key: value for key, value in metadata.items()
                             if key in _TABLE_METADATA_KEYS | _TABLE_DERIVATION_KEYS},
            }
        node = _safe_value(raw)
        node_key = str(node.get("node_key") or "")
        if not node_key:
            continue
        # _safe_value already recursively sanitized and detached this mapping.
        node["metadata"] = node["metadata"] if isinstance(node.get("metadata"), dict) else {}
        for key in _EXECUTABLE_METADATA_KEYS:
            node.pop(key, None)
        metadata_editable = node["metadata"].get("editable", True) is not False
        frozen_value = node["metadata"].get("frozen_value")
        model_value = model_values.get(node_key)
        if model_value is None and "archived_value" in node["metadata"]:
            model_value = deepcopy(node["metadata"]["archived_value"])
        if model_value is None and not metadata_editable and "frozen_value" in node["metadata"]:
            model_value = frozen_value
        node["model_value"] = deepcopy(model_value)
        if review_context:
            review = resolve_field_review(
                category_key=review_context.get("category_key", ""),
                field=str(node.get("path") or node_key).rsplit(".", 1)[-1],
                salt=review_context.get("salt", ""),
                item_key=review_context.get("item_key", ""),
            )
            if review is not None:
                node["field_review"] = review
        current_value = current_values.get(node_key)
        if current_value is None and "archived_value" in node["metadata"]:
            current_value = deepcopy(node["metadata"]["archived_value"])
        node["current_value"] = deepcopy(current_value if current_value is not None else model_value)
        previous = previous_values.get(node_key)
        inherited = inherit_correction_node(
            previous if isinstance(previous, Mapping) else None,
            node,
        )
        current_human = human_values.get(node_key)
        if current_human is not None:
            inherited.update(_human_entry(current_human))
            inherited["inheritance"] = {"status": "current"}
        inherited["steps"] = _node_steps(node)
        inherited["editable"] = metadata_editable
        if metadata_editable and node_key.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] == "is_single":
            allowed = allowed_is_single_values(node_key)
            if allowed:
                # UI choices describe permitted human rerouting, not the one
                # value required from this frozen model branch. Never rewrite
                # the model schema or the original correction contract/hash.
                inherited["options"] = allowed
                inherited["metadata"]["option_labels"] = {
                    str(code): f"{code}（{'整体' if index == 0 else '单体'}）"
                    for index, code in enumerate(allowed)
                }
        if not metadata_editable:
            inherited["read_only"] = True
            inherited["read_only_reason"] = str(
                node["metadata"].get("read_only_reason")
                or "本节点属于冻结规则，只能查看"
            )
        result.append(inherited)
    return result


def _mapping_path(root: Any, path: str) -> Any:
    current = root
    for part in [item for item in path.split(".") if item]:
        if isinstance(current, Mapping):
            if part not in current:
                return None
            current = current[part]
            continue
        if isinstance(current, list):
            matched = next(
                (
                    item
                    for item in current
                    if isinstance(item, Mapping)
                    and str(
                        item.get("dimension_key")
                        or item.get("node_key")
                        or item.get("rule_id")
                        or item.get("key")
                    )
                    == part
                ),
                None,
            )
            if matched is None:
                return None
            current = matched
            continue
        return None
    return deepcopy(current)


def _value_for_node(roots: Mapping[str, Any], node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Any:
    metadata = node.get("metadata")
    metadata = metadata if isinstance(metadata, Mapping) else {}
    foreach_node_id = metadata.get("node_instance_id")
    foreach_item_key = metadata.get("foreach_item_key")
    foreach_field_path = metadata.get("foreach_item_field_path")
    if (
        metadata.get("foreach_field_template") is True
        and isinstance(foreach_node_id, str)
        and isinstance(foreach_item_key, str)
        and isinstance(foreach_field_path, str)
    ):
        workflow = roots.get("workflow")
        workflow = workflow if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else {}
        runtime = workflow.get(foreach_node_id)
        runtime = runtime if isinstance(runtime, Mapping) else {}
        output = runtime.get("output")
        output = output if isinstance(output, Mapping) else {}
        items = output.get("items")
        if isinstance(items, list):
            selected = next(
                (
                    item
                    for item in items
                    if isinstance(item, Mapping)
                    and str(item.get("item_key")) == foreach_item_key
                ),
                None,
            )
            if isinstance(selected, Mapping):
                result = selected.get("output")
                if isinstance(result, Mapping):
                    return _mapping_path(result, foreach_field_path)
    node_key = str(node.get("node_key") or "")
    if node_key in roots:
        return deepcopy(roots[node_key])
    path = str(node.get("path") or node_key)
    if path in roots:
        return deepcopy(roots[path])
    return _mapping_path(roots, path)


_AUTHORITATIVE_VALUE_MISSING = object()


def _scoring_step(
    scoring: Mapping[str, Any], *step_keys: str
) -> Mapping[str, Any] | None:
    raw_steps = scoring.get("steps")
    if not isinstance(raw_steps, list):
        return None
    wanted = set(step_keys)
    return next(
        (
            step
            for step in reversed(raw_steps)
            if isinstance(step, Mapping) and step.get("step") in wanted
        ),
        None,
    )


def _step_note(scoring: Mapping[str, Any], *step_keys: str) -> str:
    step = _scoring_step(scoring, *step_keys)
    return str(step.get("note") or "") if step is not None else ""


def _confidence_bucket(value: Any) -> str | None:
    if isinstance(value, str):
        normalized = value.strip().lower()
        if normalized in {"high", "medium", "low"}:
            return normalized
        try:
            value = float(normalized)
        except ValueError:
            return None
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return None
    if value >= 0.8:
        return "high"
    if value >= 0.5:
        return "medium"
    return "low"


def _prompt_a_facts(roots: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    """Return the most likely prompt-A business output in a frozen workflow.

    Historical native snapshots can wrap these facts in ``precheck`` while
    current runtime snapshots expose them directly.  Selecting by business
    keys keeps this read model independent from an operator-defined node name.
    """

    workflow = roots.get("workflow")
    if not isinstance(workflow, Mapping):
        return {}
    candidates: list[tuple[int, int, Mapping[str, Any]]] = []
    prompt_a_keys = {
        "classification",
        "track_classification",
        "image_quality",
        "production_fields",
        "track_confidence",
        "classification_confidence",
        "media_type",
        "trait",
    }
    for index, entry in enumerate(workflow.values()):
        if not isinstance(entry, Mapping):
            continue
        output = entry.get("output")
        output = output if isinstance(output, Mapping) else entry
        nested = output.get("precheck")
        facts = nested if isinstance(nested, Mapping) else output
        score = sum(1 for key in prompt_a_keys if key in facts)
        if score:
            candidates.append((score, -index, facts))
    if not candidates:
        return {}
    return deepcopy(max(candidates, key=lambda item: (item[0], item[1]))[2])


def _first_fact(*values: Any) -> Any:
    return next((value for value in values if value is not None), None)


def _numeric_fact(*values: Any) -> int | float | None:
    for value in values:
        if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool):
            return deepcopy(value)
    return None


def _quality_score_cap(scoring: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int | float | None:
    action = scoring.get("hard_defect_action")
    action = action if isinstance(action, Mapping) else {}
    cap_to = _numeric_fact(action.get("cap_to"))
    if cap_to is not None:
        return cap_to

    caps = scoring.get("caps")
    caps = caps if isinstance(caps, list) else []
    for cap in caps:
        if not isinstance(cap, Mapping):
            continue
        explicit = _numeric_fact(
            cap.get("cap_to"),
            cap.get("score_cap"),
            cap.get("max_score"),
            cap.get("value"),
        )
        if explicit is not None:
            return explicit

    soft_cap_step = _scoring_step(scoring, "quality_soft_cap")
    if soft_cap_step is not None:
        applied = _numeric_fact(soft_cap_step.get("score_after"))
        if applied is not None:
            return applied

    hard_cap_names = {"hard_defect_severity", "high_score_veto"}
    if any(
        isinstance(cap, Mapping) and cap.get("cap") in hard_cap_names
        for cap in caps
    ):
        veto_step = _scoring_step(scoring, "veto")
        if veto_step is not None:
            return _numeric_fact(veto_step.get("score_after"))
    return None


def _authoritative_rule_module_value(
    roots: Mapping[str, Any], node: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> Any:
    """Project finalized scoring facts back onto workflow rule nodes.

    Rule-module attempts keep immutable executor evidence, which can contain
    deterministic fixture values.  For native level workflows, the values an
    operator must review are the outputs of the authoritative finalizer stored
    in ``scoring_json``.  The frozen contract identifies each module and field;
    unknown or historical modules continue to use their runtime output.
    """

    metadata = node.get("metadata")
    if not isinstance(metadata, Mapping):
        return _AUTHORITATIVE_VALUE_MISSING
    module_type = metadata.get("module_type_key")
    field_key = metadata.get("module_field_key")
    if not isinstance(module_type, str) or not isinstance(field_key, str):
        return _AUTHORITATIVE_VALUE_MISSING
    scoring = roots.get("scoring")
    if not isinstance(scoring, Mapping) or not scoring:
        return _AUTHORITATIVE_VALUE_MISSING
    precheck = roots.get("precheck")
    precheck = precheck if isinstance(precheck, Mapping) else {}
    prompt_a = _prompt_a_facts(roots)
    classification = _first_fact(
        precheck.get("classification"),
        precheck.get("track_classification"),
        prompt_a.get("classification"),
        prompt_a.get("track_classification"),
    )
    classification = (
        classification if isinstance(classification, Mapping) else {}
    )
    image_quality = _first_fact(
        precheck.get("image_quality"), prompt_a.get("image_quality")
    )
    image_quality = image_quality if isinstance(image_quality, Mapping) else {}
    precheck_production = precheck.get("production_fields")
    precheck_production = (
        precheck_production
        if isinstance(precheck_production, Mapping)
        else {}
    )
    prompt_production = prompt_a.get("production_fields")
    prompt_production = (
        prompt_production if isinstance(prompt_production, Mapping) else {}
    )
    dimension_output = scoring.get("dimension_deduction_output")
    dimension_output = (
        dimension_output if isinstance(dimension_output, Mapping) else {}
    )

    if module_type == "eligibility_redline":
        if field_key == "hit" and "hard_reject" in scoring:
            return bool(scoring.get("hard_reject"))
        if field_key == "matched_rules" and "hit_rules" in scoring:
            raw_rules = scoring.get("hit_rules")
            return deepcopy(raw_rules) if isinstance(raw_rules, list) else []
        if field_key == "level_cap" and "hard_reject" in scoring:
            level = scoring.get("level")
            return (
                level
                if scoring.get("hard_reject") and isinstance(level, str)
                else None
            )
        if field_key == "reason":
            return _step_note(scoring, "redline")

    if module_type == "track_routing":
        if field_key == "track_key" and "track_key" in scoring:
            return deepcopy(scoring.get("track_key"))
        if field_key == "confidence":
            confidence = _first_fact(
                scoring.get("confidence"),
                classification.get("primary_confidence"),
                classification.get("confidence"),
                prompt_a.get("track_confidence"),
                prompt_a.get("classification_confidence"),
            )
            bucket = _confidence_bucket(confidence)
            if bucket is not None:
                return bucket
        if field_key == "reason":
            evidence = classification.get("evidence")
            if isinstance(evidence, list):
                readable = [
                    str(item).strip()
                    for item in evidence
                    if str(item).strip()
                ]
                if readable:
                    return "；".join(readable)
            prompt_evidence = _first_fact(
                prompt_a.get("track_evidence"), prompt_a.get("evidence")
            )
            if isinstance(prompt_evidence, list):
                readable = [
                    str(item).strip()
                    for item in prompt_evidence
                    if str(item).strip()
                ]
                if readable:
                    return "；".join(readable)
            if isinstance(prompt_evidence, str) and prompt_evidence.strip():
                return prompt_evidence.strip()
            return _step_note(scoring, "track")

    if module_type == "media_adjustment":
        if field_key == "media_type" and "media_key" in scoring:
            media_key = scoring.get("media_key")
            if media_key is not None:
                return deepcopy(media_key)
            return deepcopy(
                _first_fact(
                    precheck_production.get("trait"),
                    prompt_production.get("trait"),
                    prompt_a.get("media_type"),
                    prompt_a.get("trait"),
                )
            )
        if field_key == "score_adjustment":
            if "media_penalty" in scoring:
                return deepcopy(scoring.get("media_penalty"))
            evidence = scoring.get("common_modifier_evidence")
            evidence = evidence if isinstance(evidence, list) else []
            media_evidence = next(
                (
                    item
                    for item in evidence
                    if isinstance(item, Mapping)
                    and item.get("primitive") == "media_penalty"
                ),
                None,
            )
            if media_evidence is not None:
                penalty = _first_fact(
                    media_evidence.get("penalty"), media_evidence.get("delta")
                )
                if penalty is not None:
                    return deepcopy(penalty)
        if field_key == "reason":
            return _step_note(scoring, "media", "media_skipped")

    if module_type == "quality_cap_exception":
        if field_key == "quality_grade":
            for key in ("quality_severity", "severity", "quality_grade"):
                if image_quality.get(key) is not None:
                    return deepcopy(image_quality[key])
            return None
        if field_key == "score_cap":
            return _quality_score_cap(scoring)
        if field_key == "exception_applied":
            caps = scoring.get("caps")
            return any(
                isinstance(cap, Mapping)
                and cap.get("cap") == "hard_defect_exemption"
                for cap in (caps if isinstance(caps, list) else [])
            )
        if field_key == "reason":
            notes = [
                _step_note(scoring, "quality_soft_cap"),
                _step_note(scoring, "quality_exemption_skipped"),
                _step_note(scoring, "veto", "veto_skipped"),
            ]
            return "；".join(note for note in notes if note)

    if module_type == "dimension_scoring":
        if field_key == "dimensions":
            dimensions = _first_fact(
                dimension_output.get("dimensions"),
                scoring.get("aesthetic_dimensions"),
                _mapping_path(roots.get("aesthetic"), "dimensions"),
            )
            if dimensions is not None:
                return deepcopy(dimensions)
        if field_key == "total_score":
            step = _scoring_step(scoring, "dimension_rule_deduction", "dimensions")
            if step is not None and isinstance(
                step.get("score_after"), (int, float)
            ) and not isinstance(step.get("score_after"), bool):
                return deepcopy(step.get("score_after"))
            total_score = _numeric_fact(
                dimension_output.get("aesthetic_score"),
                scoring.get("inspiration_aesthetic_score"),
                scoring.get("foundation_after_rules"),
                scoring.get("foundation_before_rules"),
            )
            if total_score is not None:
                return total_score
        if field_key == "reason":
            return _step_note(scoring, "dimension_rule_deduction", "dimensions")

    if module_type == "anchor_calibration":
        if field_key == "anchor_id" and "foundation_sha256" in scoring:
            return deepcopy(scoring.get("foundation_sha256") or None)
        if field_key == "calibrated_score":
            foundation_step = _scoring_step(scoring, "b_aesthetic_foundation")
            calibrated = _numeric_fact(
                scoring.get("foundation_after_rules"),
                scoring.get("inspiration_aesthetic_score"),
                dimension_output.get("aesthetic_score"),
                scoring.get("foundation_before_rules"),
                foundation_step.get("score_after")
                if foundation_step is not None
                else None,
                scoring.get("score"),
            )
            if calibrated is not None:
                return calibrated
        if field_key == "reason":
            return _step_note(scoring, "b_aesthetic_foundation", "track_cap")

    if module_type == "score_level_matcher":
        if field_key == "score" and "score" in scoring:
            return deepcopy(scoring.get("score"))
        if field_key == "level" and "level" in scoring:
            return deepcopy(scoring.get("level"))
        if field_key == "reason":
            return _step_note(scoring, "level")

    return _AUTHORITATIVE_VALUE_MISSING


def _correction_workflow_outputs(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Expose the business output while retaining V3 runtime evidence.

    Workflow V3 attempts wrap the fixture result with immutable component and
    execution evidence.  Correction contracts intentionally address the
    operator-facing result as ``workflow.<instance>.output.<field>``; flatten
    only that wrapper so V2 snapshots keep their historical shape.
    """

    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {}
    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for raw_key, raw_entry in value.items():
        key = str(raw_key)
        if not isinstance(raw_entry, Mapping):
            normalized[key] = deepcopy(raw_entry)
            continue
        entry = deepcopy(dict(raw_entry))
        execution_output = entry.get("output")
        if (
            isinstance(execution_output, Mapping)
            and execution_output.get("node_instance_id") == key
            and "output" in execution_output
        ):
            entry["execution_evidence"] = deepcopy(dict(execution_output))
            entry["output"] = deepcopy(execution_output.get("output"))
        normalized[key] = entry
    return normalized


def _legacy_3d_su_root(stage_a: Any, score: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """3D/SU 调用 A 带独立风险字段时，附带旧链路 reason/hwreason 投影供运营比对。"""

    if not isinstance(stage_a, Mapping) or not isinstance(stage_a.get("model_3d_su_fields"), Mapping):
        return None
    from .model_3d_su_legacy_projection import legacy_3d_su_projection

    return legacy_3d_su_projection(stage_a, score=score)


def _model_roots(item: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    snapshot = _json_object(getattr(item, "result_snapshot_json", None))
    stage_a = snapshot.get("stage_a")
    stage_a = stage_a if isinstance(stage_a, Mapping) else {}
    production_fields = stage_a.get("production_fields")
    production_fields = (
        production_fields if isinstance(production_fields, Mapping) else {}
    )
    stage_b = snapshot.get("stage_b")
    stage_b = stage_b if isinstance(stage_b, Mapping) else {}
    dimensions = stage_b.get("dimensions")
    dimensions = dimensions if isinstance(dimensions, (Mapping, list)) else {}
    level = snapshot.get("predicted_level")
    score = snapshot.get("authoritative_score")
    workflow_outputs = _correction_workflow_outputs(
        snapshot.get("workflow_node_outputs")
    )
    return {
        **snapshot,
        "call_a": {**dict(production_fields), "grade": level, "score": score},
        "production_fields": dict(production_fields),
        "legacy_3d_su": _legacy_3d_su_root(stage_a, score),
        "precheck": dict(stage_a),
        "redline": dict(stage_a),
        "dimension": deepcopy(dimensions),
        "dimensions": deepcopy(dimensions),
        "aesthetic": dict(stage_b),
        "final_level": level,
        "level": level,
        "scoring": {
            "level": level,
            "score": score,
            **(
                dict(snapshot.get("scoring"))
                if isinstance(snapshot.get("scoring"), Mapping)
                else {}
            ),
        },
        "workflow": workflow_outputs,
    }


def _current_roots(item: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    evaluation = getattr(item, "evaluation", None)
    if evaluation is None:
        return _model_roots(item)
    roots = _model_roots(item)
    precheck = _json_object(getattr(evaluation, "precheck_json", None))
    production_fields = precheck.get("production_fields")
    production_fields = (
        production_fields if isinstance(production_fields, Mapping) else {}
    )
    scoring = _json_object(getattr(evaluation, "scoring_json", None))
    aesthetic = _json_object(getattr(evaluation, "aesthetic_json", None))
    level = getattr(evaluation, "level", None)
    score = getattr(evaluation, "score", None)
    workflow_outputs = _correction_workflow_outputs(
        _json_object(
            getattr(evaluation, "workflow_node_outputs_json", None)
        )
    )
    roots.update({
        "call_a": {**dict(production_fields), "grade": level, "score": score},
        "production_fields": dict(production_fields),
        "legacy_3d_su": _legacy_3d_su_root(precheck, score),
        "precheck": precheck,
        "redline": precheck,
        "dimension": aesthetic.get("dimensions", {}),
        "dimensions": aesthetic.get("dimensions", {}),
        "aesthetic": aesthetic,
        "scoring": scoring,
        "track": scoring.get("track_key"),
        "track_key": scoring.get("track_key"),
        "final_level": level,
        "level": level,
        **({"workflow": workflow_outputs} if workflow_outputs else {}),
    })
    return roots


def _workflow_runtime_outputs(item: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    evaluation = getattr(item, "evaluation", None)
    current = _json_object(
        getattr(evaluation, "workflow_node_outputs_json", None)
        if evaluation is not None
        else None
    )
    if current:
        return current
    snapshot = _json_object(getattr(item, "result_snapshot_json", None))
    frozen = snapshot.get("workflow_node_outputs")
    return deepcopy(dict(frozen)) if isinstance(frozen, Mapping) else {}


def _runtime_business_output(
    runtime: Mapping[str, Any], node_instance_id: str
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    output = runtime.get("output")
    if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
        return {}
    if output.get("node_instance_id") == node_instance_id and isinstance(
        output.get("output"), Mapping
    ):
        return output["output"]
    return output


def _materialize_runtime_contract(
    contract: Mapping[str, Any], item: Any, *, _display: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Expand frozen foreach wildcard fields without changing contract identity."""

    runtime_outputs = _workflow_runtime_outputs(item)
    from .correction_contract import correction_display_contract
    materialized = dict(_display) if _display is not None else correction_display_contract(contract)
    # Presentation only: old contracts keep their identity, field set and types.
    # In particular, preserve historical image_defects corrections for audit.
    expanded: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for raw_node in materialized.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(raw_node, Mapping):
            continue
        # Non-foreach nodes are only read before build_correction_nodes detaches
        # them. Foreach mutations below explicitly copy their nested metadata.
        node = dict(raw_node)
        metadata = node.get("metadata")
        metadata = metadata if isinstance(metadata, Mapping) else {}
        if metadata.get("foreach_field_template") is not True:
            expanded.append(node)
            continue
        node_id = metadata.get("node_instance_id")
        runtime = runtime_outputs.get(str(node_id)) if node_id else None
        business = (
            _runtime_business_output(runtime, str(node_id))
            if isinstance(runtime, Mapping) and isinstance(node_id, str)
            else {}
        )
        items = business.get("items")
        if not isinstance(items, list):
            node_metadata = deepcopy(dict(metadata))
            node_metadata["editable"] = False
            node_metadata["read_only_reason"] = "foreach_items_unavailable"
            node["metadata"] = node_metadata
            node["editable"] = False
            node["read_only"] = True
            node["read_only_reason"] = "foreach_items_unavailable"
            expanded.append(node)
            continue
        field_path = str(metadata.get("foreach_item_field_path") or "output")
        for item_order, raw_runtime_item in enumerate(items):
            if not isinstance(raw_runtime_item, Mapping):
                continue
            raw_item_key = raw_runtime_item.get("item_key")
            if not isinstance(raw_item_key, (str, int)) or isinstance(
                raw_item_key, bool
            ):
                continue
            item_key = str(raw_item_key)
            encoded_key = json.dumps(
                item_key,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                separators=(",", ":"),
            )
            concrete = deepcopy(node)
            concrete["node_key"] = (
                f'workflow.{node_id}.items[{encoded_key}].result.{field_path}'
            )
            concrete["path"] = (
                f'workflow.{node_id}.output.items[{encoded_key}]'
                f'.result.{field_path}'
            )
            workflow_order = int(metadata.get("workflow_order", 0) or 0)
            frozen_order = int(node.get("order", 0) or 0)
            field_order = max(frozen_order - workflow_order * 100, 0)
            concrete["order"] = (
                workflow_order * 1_000_000 + item_order * 1_000 + field_order
            )
            concrete_metadata = deepcopy(dict(metadata))
            concrete_metadata["foreach_item_key"] = item_key
            concrete_metadata["foreach_item_status"] = str(
                raw_runtime_item.get("status") or ""
            )
            identity = concrete_metadata.get("field_identity")
            if isinstance(identity, Mapping):
                concrete_metadata["field_identity"] = {
                    **deepcopy(dict(identity)),
                    "item_key": item_key,
                }
            item_status = str(raw_runtime_item.get("status") or "")
            if item_status != "succeeded":
                concrete_metadata["editable"] = False
                concrete_metadata["read_only_reason"] = (
                    f"foreach_item_{item_status or 'unavailable'}"
                )
                concrete["editable"] = False
                concrete["read_only"] = True
                concrete["read_only_reason"] = concrete_metadata[
                    "read_only_reason"
                ]
            concrete["metadata"] = concrete_metadata
            expanded.append(concrete)
    materialized["nodes"] = expanded
    return materialized


def _apply_workflow_runtime_state(
    nodes: list[dict[str, Any]], item: Any
) -> None:
    outputs = _workflow_runtime_outputs(item)
    for node in nodes:
        metadata = node.get("metadata")
        if not isinstance(metadata, dict):
            continue
        node_id = metadata.get("node_instance_id")
        runtime = outputs.get(str(node_id)) if node_id else None
        if not isinstance(runtime, Mapping):
            continue
        status = runtime.get("status")
        if isinstance(status, str):
            metadata["runtime_status"] = status
        model_invocation = runtime.get("model_invocation")
        if isinstance(model_invocation, Mapping):
            node["execution_evidence"] = {
                "schema_version": "correction-node-execution-evidence-v1",
                "node_instance_id": str(node_id),
                "runtime_status": status,
                "model_invocation": redact_workflow_secrets(
                    deepcopy(dict(model_invocation))
                ),
            }
        if status != "skipped":
            continue
        reason = str(runtime.get("skipped_reason") or "route_not_selected")
        metadata["editable"] = False
        metadata["read_only_reason"] = reason
        node["editable"] = False
        node["read_only"] = True
        node["read_only_reason"] = reason


def _human_values(
    item: Any,
    contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    frozen_contract_hash: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    evaluation = getattr(item, "evaluation", None)
    if evaluation is None:
        return {}
    nodes = [node for node in contract.get("nodes", []) if isinstance(node, Mapping)]
    key_by_path = {
        str(node.get("path") or node.get("node_key")): str(node.get("node_key"))
        for node in nodes
    }
    known_keys = {str(node.get("node_key")) for node in nodes}
    workflow_keys = {
        str(node.get("node_key"))
        for node in nodes
        if isinstance(node.get("metadata"), Mapping)
        and node["metadata"].get("workflow_node_key")
    }
    expected_contract_hash = frozen_contract_hash or correction_contract_hash(
        contract
    )
    expected_run_id = getattr(item, "run_id", None)
    if expected_run_id is None:
        expected_run_id = getattr(getattr(item, "run", None), "id", None)
    values: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for event in _json_list(getattr(evaluation, "correction_history_json", None)):
        if not isinstance(event, Mapping):
            continue
        if event.get("corrector_confidence") is not None or event.get(
            "corrector_policy"
        ):
            continue
        node_key = event.get("node_key")
        if node_key not in known_keys:
            node_key = key_by_path.get(str(event.get("node_path") or ""))
        if not node_key:
            continue
        if str(node_key) in workflow_keys or event.get("workflow_node_key"):
            if (
                event.get("contract_hash") != expected_contract_hash
                or event.get("run_id") != expected_run_id
            ):
                continue
        values[str(node_key)] = {
            "human_value": deepcopy(event.get("new_value")),
            "reason": str(event.get("reason") or ""),
            "evidence": deepcopy(
                event.get("contract_evidence", event.get("evidence", []))
            ),
            "source": {
                "type": "node_correction",
                "corrector": event.get("corrector"),
                "corrected_at": event.get("corrected_at"),
                "review_revision": event.get("resulting_review_revision"),
            },
        }

    reviews = list(getattr(evaluation, "reviews", []) or [])
    reviews.sort(
        key=lambda review: (
            str(getattr(review, "created_at", "") or ""),
            int(getattr(review, "id", 0) or 0),
        )
    )
    for review in reviews:
        for correction in _json_list(getattr(review, "corrections_json", None)):
            if not isinstance(correction, Mapping):
                continue
            raw_path = str(correction.get("field_key") or "")
            node_key = key_by_path.get(raw_path)
            if node_key is None and raw_path.startswith("production_fields."):
                node_key = key_by_path.get("call_a." + raw_path.split(".", 1)[1])
            if not node_key or node_key in values:
                continue
            values[node_key] = {
                "human_value": deepcopy(correction.get("human_value")),
                "reason": str(correction.get("note") or getattr(review, "note", "")),
                "evidence": [],
                "source": {
                    "type": "human_review",
                    "review_id": getattr(review, "id", None),
                    "reviewer": getattr(review, "reviewer_name", None),
                },
            }
    return values


def _values_for_contract(
    roots: Mapping[str, Any], contract: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    values: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for node in contract.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or not node.get("node_key"):
            continue
        authoritative = _authoritative_rule_module_value(roots, node)
        values[str(node.get("node_key"))] = (
            _value_for_node(roots, node)
            if authoritative is _AUTHORITATIVE_VALUE_MISSING
            else authoritative
        )
    return values


def _previous_human_values(
    previous_item: Any | None,
    *, _prepared: tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, str | None] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if previous_item is None:
        return {}
    previous_run = getattr(previous_item, "run", None)
    if previous_run is None:
        return {}
    if _prepared is None:
        previous_contract, _, _ = _run_contract(previous_run)
        actual_hash = None
    else:
        previous_contract, actual_hash = _prepared
    if previous_contract is None:
        return {}
    values = _human_values(previous_item, previous_contract, frozen_contract_hash=actual_hash)
    by_key = {
        str(node.get("node_key")): node
        for node in previous_contract.get("nodes", [])
        if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("node_key")
    }
    return {
        key: {**deepcopy(by_key[key]), **deepcopy(value)}
        for key, value in values.items()
        if key in by_key
    }


def _legacy_archive_payload(item: Any) -> Mapping[str, Any] | list[Any] | None:
    """Read only item-owned archive fields; never consult a live mechanism."""

    snapshot = _json_object(getattr(item, "result_snapshot_json", None))
    for key in (
        "archived_correction_fields",
        "archived_correction_contract",
        "archived_fields",
        "correction_fields",
        "frozen_correction_fields",
    ):
        value = snapshot.get(key)
        if isinstance(value, (Mapping, list)):
            return value
    for key in (
        "archived_correction_fields",
        "archived_correction_contract",
        "archived_fields",
        "correction_fields",
    ):
        value = getattr(item, key, None)
        if isinstance(value, (Mapping, list)):
            return value
    return None


def _legacy_archive_nodes(
    archive: Mapping[str, Any] | list[Any],
) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[str]]:
    """Normalize a historical field subset and report unavailable node keys."""

    if isinstance(archive, Mapping):
        raw_nodes = archive.get("nodes")
        if not isinstance(raw_nodes, list):
            raw_nodes = archive.get("fields")
        if isinstance(raw_nodes, Mapping):
            raw_nodes = [
                {"node_key": key, "value": value}
                for key, value in raw_nodes.items()
            ]
        if raw_nodes is None:
            reserved = {
                "contract_version",
                "category_key",
                "expected_nodes",
                "node_keys",
                "contract",
                "contract_hash",
                "nodes",
                "fields",
            }
            direct_fields = {
                key: value
                for key, value in archive.items()
                if key not in reserved
            }
            if direct_fields:
                raw_nodes = [
                    {"node_key": key, "value": value}
                    for key, value in direct_fields.items()
                ]
        expected = archive.get("expected_nodes")
        if not isinstance(expected, list):
            expected = archive.get("node_keys")
    else:
        raw_nodes = archive
        expected = None
    if not isinstance(raw_nodes, list):
        raw_nodes = []
    expected_keys = [str(value) for value in expected or [] if str(value).strip()]
    nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    unavailable: list[str] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    required_fields = (
        "node_key",
        "layer",
        "path",
        "label",
        "description",
        "type",
        "semantic_version",
        "compatibility_key",
        "required",
        "evidence",
    )
    for index, raw in enumerate(raw_nodes):
        if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
            unavailable.append(f"归档节点[{index}]")
            continue
        source = deepcopy(dict(raw))
        node_key = str(source.get("node_key") or source.get("key") or "").strip()
        path = str(source.get("path") or node_key).strip()
        if not node_key and path:
            node_key = path
        layer = str(source.get("layer") or "").strip()
        if not layer:
            layer = "V3" if path.startswith(("v3.", "scoring.")) else "B" if path.startswith(("call_b.", "dimension.", "dimensions.")) else "A"
        missing = [field for field in required_fields if field not in source]
        if layer == "V3" and not source.get("recompute_ref"):
            missing.append("recompute_ref")
        if not node_key:
            unavailable.append(f"归档节点[{index}]")
            continue
        if node_key in seen:
            unavailable.append(node_key)
            continue
        seen.add(node_key)
        raw_metadata = source.get("metadata")
        metadata = deepcopy(dict(raw_metadata)) if isinstance(raw_metadata, Mapping) else {}
        metadata.setdefault("node_type", source.get("node_type") or "call_a_field")
        metadata["archived_value"] = deepcopy(
            source.get("value", source.get("model_value"))
        )
        metadata["archive_complete"] = not missing
        node = {
            **source,
            "node_key": node_key,
            "layer": layer,
            "path": path or node_key,
            "order": int(source.get("order", index) or index),
            "metadata": metadata,
        }
        node.pop("value", None)
        node.pop("model_value", None)
        if missing:
            unavailable.append(f"{node_key}（缺少：{'、'.join(missing)}）")
        nodes.append(node)
    for expected_key in expected_keys:
        if expected_key not in seen:
            unavailable.append(expected_key)
    return nodes, unavailable


def legacy_correction_view_from_archived_fields(item: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build the narrowest possible view for a run predating contract snapshots.

    The archive is intentionally treated as untrusted metadata.  Complete
    archived nodes can be edited in place; any missing node description or
    server recompute reference makes the entire legacy view read-only.  No
    current category, prompt, or V3 configuration is queried here.
    """

    run = getattr(item, "run", None)
    evaluation = getattr(item, "evaluation", None)
    archive = _legacy_archive_payload(item)
    contract: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    unavailable: list[str] = []
    nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    if archive is not None:
        archive_mapping = archive if isinstance(archive, Mapping) else {}
        raw_contract = (
            archive_mapping.get("contract")
            if isinstance(archive_mapping, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        raw_nodes_source: Mapping[str, Any] | list[Any] = (
            raw_contract if isinstance(raw_contract, Mapping) else archive
        )
        nodes, unavailable = _legacy_archive_nodes(raw_nodes_source)
        category_key = str(
            archive_mapping.get("category_key")
            if isinstance(archive_mapping, Mapping)
            else ""
        ) or str(getattr(run, "category_key", "legacy"))
        contract = {
            "contract_version": str(
                archive_mapping.get("contract_version", "legacy-correction-contract-v1")
                if isinstance(archive_mapping, Mapping)
                else "legacy-correction-contract-v1"
            ),
            "category_key": category_key,
            "nodes": nodes,
            "sources": {"archive": deepcopy(dict(archive_mapping))}
            if isinstance(archive_mapping, Mapping)
            else {"archive": deepcopy(archive)},
        }
        normalized = deepcopy(contract)
        normalized["contract_hash"] = correction_contract_hash(normalized)
        contract = normalized
        validation_errors = validate_correction_contract(contract)
        if validation_errors:
            unavailable.extend(
                error.split(".", 1)[0]
                if error.startswith("nodes[") and "." not in error.split(" ", 1)[0][7:]
                else error
                for error in validation_errors
            )
    else:
        unavailable = ["全部合同节点"]

    review_revision = int(getattr(evaluation, "review_revision", 0) or 0)
    read_only = bool(unavailable or evaluation is None or contract is None)
    if contract is not None:
        model_values = _values_for_contract(_model_roots(item), contract)
        current_values = _values_for_contract(_current_roots(item), contract)
        materialized = build_correction_nodes(
            contract,
            model_values=model_values,
            current_values=current_values,
            human_values=_human_values(item, contract),
            previous_values={},
        )
        if read_only:
            for node in materialized:
                node["editable"] = False
                node["read_only"] = True
                node["read_only_reason"] = "历史归档字段不完整，只能查看"
        nodes = materialized
    lane = "baseline"
    if getattr(run, "__tablename__", "") == "evaluation_production_runs":
        lane = "incremental"
    elif getattr(run, "__tablename__", "") == "prompt_regression_runs":
        lane = "candidate"
    return {
        "schema_version": "correction-view-v1",
        "lane": lane,
        "run_id": getattr(run, "id", getattr(item, "run_id", None)),
        "item_id": getattr(item, "id", None),
        "evaluation_id": getattr(item, "evaluation_id", None),
        "category_key": contract.get("category_key") if contract else getattr(run, "category_key", None),
        "snapshot_status": "legacy_compatible" if contract is not None and not read_only else "legacy_read_only",
        "snapshot_source": "archived_item_fields",
        "read_only": read_only,
        "unavailable_reason": "；".join(unavailable) if unavailable else None,
        "unavailable_nodes": list(dict.fromkeys(unavailable)),
        "contract": (
            {
                "contract_version": contract.get("contract_version"),
                "contract_hash": contract["contract_hash"],
                "category_key": contract["category_key"],
            }
            if contract
            else None
        ),
        "review_revision": review_revision,
        "nodes": nodes,
        "idempotent_replay": False,
    }


def table_correction_view(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Presentation only: the full frozen contract still validates every write.

    Tables render editable fields, whereas the evidence drawer consumes the
    complete view. Keep separate client caches for these two presentations.
    """
    nodes = []
    for source in payload.get("nodes", []):
        metadata = source.get("metadata") or {}
        if source.get("editable") is False or metadata.get("editable") is False or source.get("read_only"):
            continue
        node = {key: value for key, value in source.items() if key not in {"metadata", "execution_evidence"}}
        node["metadata"] = {key: value for key, value in metadata.items() if key in _TABLE_METADATA_KEYS}
        nodes.append(node)
    return {**payload, "nodes": nodes}


def build_correction_views(
    db: Session | None, *, run: Any, items: list[Any],
    previous_by_asset: Mapping[Any, Any], table: bool = False,
) -> dict[int, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Request-local frozen contract reuse; no mutable evidence is cached."""
    prepared = _prepare_view_contract(run)
    previous_contracts: dict[int, tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, str | None]] = {}
    views = {}
    for item in items:
        previous = previous_by_asset.get(item.asset_id)
        previous_run = getattr(previous, "run", None)
        previous_contract = None
        if previous_run is not None:
            key = id(previous_run)
            if key not in previous_contracts:
                contract, _, _ = _run_contract(previous_run)
                previous_contracts[key] = (
                    contract, correction_contract_hash(contract) if contract is not None else None,
                )
            previous_contract = previous_contracts[key]
        views[item.id] = build_correction_view(
            db, run=run, item=item, previous_item=previous, _prepared=prepared,
            _previous_contract=previous_contract, _table=table,
        )
    return views


def build_correction_view(
    db: Session | None,
    *,
    run: Any,
    item: Any,
    previous_item: Any | None = None,
    _prepared: _ViewContract | None = None,
    _previous_contract: tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, str | None] | None = None,
    _table: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build one correction form exclusively from the run's frozen data."""

    prepared = _prepared or _prepare_view_contract(run)
    if prepared.run is not run:
        raise ValueError("Correction presentation context belongs to another run")
    contract, expected_hash, snapshot_source = prepared.contract, prepared.expected_hash, prepared.source
    evaluation = getattr(item, "evaluation", None)
    review_revision = int(getattr(evaluation, "review_revision", 0) or 0)
    if contract is None:
        payload = legacy_correction_view_from_archived_fields(item)
        from .correction_publication import correction_publication_state
        payload["publication"] = correction_publication_state(db, evaluation)
        return payload

    actual_hash = prepared.actual_hash
    contract_errors = prepared.errors
    hash_mismatch = bool(expected_hash and expected_hash != actual_hash)
    read_only = bool(contract_errors or hash_mismatch or evaluation is None)
    unavailable: list[str] = []
    if hash_mismatch:
        unavailable.append("合同哈希与冻结快照不一致")
    unavailable.extend(contract_errors)
    if evaluation is None:
        unavailable.append("评测结果尚不可用")
    runtime_contract = _materialize_runtime_contract(contract, item, _display=prepared.display)
    model_values = _values_for_contract(_model_roots(item), runtime_contract)
    current_roots = _current_roots(item)
    current_values = _values_for_contract(current_roots, runtime_contract)
    category_key = str(
        getattr(getattr(run, "baseline_set", None), "category_key", "")
        or getattr(run, "category_key", "")
        or ""
    )
    nodes = build_correction_nodes(
        runtime_contract,
        model_values=model_values,
        current_values=current_values,
        human_values=_human_values(
            item,
            runtime_contract,
            frozen_contract_hash=expected_hash or actual_hash,
        ),
        previous_values=_previous_human_values(previous_item, _prepared=_previous_contract),
        _table=_table,
        review_context={
            # 盐里带轮次：同一轮抽检结果稳定，换一轮自动换一批样本
            "category_key": category_key,
            "salt": f"run:{getattr(run, 'id', 0)}",
            "item_key": str(getattr(item, "baseline_set_item_id", "") or getattr(item, "id", "")),
        },
    )
    _apply_workflow_runtime_state(nodes, item)
    annotate_deductions(
        nodes, current_roots.get("scoring", {}),
        getattr(run, "execution_snapshot_json", None),
    )
    if read_only:
        for node in nodes:
            node["editable"] = False
    contract_hash = expected_hash or actual_hash
    payload = {
        "schema_version": "correction-view-v1",
        "lane": "baseline",
        "field_review_policy": review_summary(category_key),
        "run_id": getattr(run, "id", None),
        "item_id": getattr(item, "id", None),
        "evaluation_id": getattr(item, "evaluation_id", None),
        "category_key": contract.get("category_key")
        or getattr(run, "category_key", None),
        "snapshot_status": (
            "frozen"
            if not contract_errors and not hash_mismatch
            else "legacy_read_only"
        ),
        "snapshot_source": snapshot_source,
        "read_only": read_only,
        "unavailable_reason": "；".join(unavailable) if unavailable else None,
        "unavailable_nodes": unavailable,
        "contract": {
            "contract_version": contract.get("contract_version"),
            "contract_hash": contract_hash,
            "category_key": contract.get("category_key"),
        },
        "review_revision": review_revision,
        "nodes": nodes,
        "idempotent_replay": False,
    }
    if db is None:
        return payload
    from .correction_publication import correction_publication_state
    payload["publication"] = correction_publication_state(db, evaluation)
    from .correction_notes import correction_notes
    payload["notes"] = correction_notes(db, run_id=run.id, item_id=item.id)
    source_item = getattr(item, "baseline_set_item", None)
    if source_item is None:
        return payload
    try:
        effective = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, source_item)
    except BaselineTruthError:
        payload["truth_status"] = "unavailable"
        return payload
    fields: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def collect(value: Mapping[str, Any], prefix: str = "") -> None:
        for key, child in value.items():
            path = f"{prefix}.{key}" if prefix else str(key)
            if isinstance(child, Mapping):
                collect(child, path)
            else:
                fields[path] = child

    collect(effective.expected_result)
    known_fields = sorted(
        key
        for key, value in fields.items()
        if not (isinstance(value, str) and not value.strip())
    )
    pending_fields = sorted(set(fields) - set(known_fields))
    frozen_revision = 0
    execution_snapshot = _json_object(
        getattr(run, "execution_snapshot_json", None)
    )
    truth_snapshot = execution_snapshot.get("truth_snapshot")
    if isinstance(truth_snapshot, list):
        for frozen in truth_snapshot:
            if (
                isinstance(frozen, Mapping)
                and frozen.get("baseline_set_item_id")
                == getattr(source_item, "id", None)
            ):
                value = frozen.get("truth_revision")
                if isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool):
                    frozen_revision = value
                break
    payload.update(
        {
            "truth_status": "available",
            "truth_source": effective.source,
            "truth_revision": effective.revision,
            "truth_hash": effective.canonical_hash,
            "effective_truth": {
                "truth_kind": effective.truth_kind,
                "expected_level": effective.expected_level,
                "expected_result": effective.expected_result,
                "truth_contract_key": effective.truth_contract_key,
                "workflow_input": effective.workflow_input,
            },
            "known_truth_fields": known_fields,
            "pending_truth_fields": pending_fields,
            "effective_next_run": effective.revision > frozen_revision,
        }
    )
    from .knowledge_graph_contracts import get_knowledge_graph_contract, shared_route_truth_fields
    route_fields = shared_route_truth_fields(getattr(item, "truth_contract_key", None), effective.truth_contract_key)
    if route_fields:
        old_route = get_knowledge_graph_contract(item.truth_contract_key)
        old_node = f"prompt_{old_route.site_scope}_{old_route.asset_scope}"
        for node in payload["nodes"]:
            metadata = node.get("metadata") or {}
            field = metadata.get("output_field_key")
            if metadata.get("node_instance_id") == old_node and field in route_fields:
                # A value cleared during route migration must not reappear from
                # old human history, nor look saved when it is still pending.
                value = deepcopy(effective.expected_result[field])
                node["current_value"] = value
                node.pop("human_value", None)
                if field in known_fields:
                    node["human_value"] = value
        payload["route_field_confirmation"] = {
            "from_contract_key": item.truth_contract_key,
            "to_contract_key": effective.truth_contract_key,
            "fields": list(route_fields),
        }
    return payload


def _evidence_required(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    evidence = node.get("evidence")
    metadata = node.get("metadata")
    return bool(
        (isinstance(evidence, Mapping) and evidence.get("required"))
        or (isinstance(metadata, Mapping) and metadata.get("evidence_required"))
    )


def _node_runtime_type(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    metadata = node.get("metadata")
    metadata = metadata if isinstance(metadata, Mapping) else {}
    explicit = node.get("node_type", metadata.get("node_type"))
    if isinstance(explicit, str) and explicit:
        return explicit
    path = str(node.get("path") or node.get("node_key") or "")
    if path.startswith("call_a."):
        return "call_a_field"
    # 美感分要在维度前缀之前判定：它是调用B的整体基础分，不属于任何单个维度规则。
    if path in {
        "aesthetic.aesthetic_score",
        "aesthetic_score",
        "call_b.aesthetic_score",
    }:
        return "aesthetic_score"
    if path.startswith("dimension.") or path.startswith("dimensions."):
        return "dimension_rule"
    if path.startswith("redline."):
        return "redline"
    if path.startswith("precheck."):
        return "precheck_field"
    if path in {"track", "track_key", "scoring.track_key"}:
        return "track"
    if path in {"final_level", "level", "scoring.level"}:
        return "final_level"
    raise CorrectionViewError(
        422,
        "CORRECTION_NODE_RUNTIME_UNSUPPORTED",
        f"节点 {node.get('node_key')} 没有可用的服务端纠偏映射",
        fields=[str(node.get("node_key"))],
    )


def _normalized_evidence(
    node_key: str, evidence: list[Mapping[str, Any]]
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    normalized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for index, item in enumerate(evidence, start=1):
        if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422,
                "CORRECTION_EVIDENCE_INVALID",
                f"节点 {node_key} 的第 {index} 条证据必须是对象",
                fields=[node_key],
            )
        allowed = {
            key: deepcopy(item[key])
            for key in (
                "rule_id",
                "old_confidence",
                "new_confidence",
                "old_evidence",
                "new_evidence",
            )
            if key in item
        }
        allowed.setdefault(
            "rule_id",
            str(item.get("field") or item.get("evidence_key") or f"{node_key}:{index}"),
        )
        if "new_evidence" not in allowed:
            text = item.get("text", item.get("description", item.get("value", "")))
            allowed["new_evidence"] = (
                str(text)
                if isinstance(text, (str, int, float, bool))
                else _canonical_json(text)
            )
        normalized.append(allowed)
    return normalized


def _submission_payload_hash(
    *, contract_hash: str, nodes: list[Mapping[str, Any]]
) -> str:
    canonical_nodes = sorted(
        (deepcopy(dict(node)) for node in nodes),
        key=lambda node: str(node.get("node_key") or ""),
    )
    return hashlib.sha256(
        _canonical_json(
            {"contract_hash": contract_hash, "nodes": canonical_nodes}
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def _existing_idempotent_event(
    evaluation: Any, idempotency_key: str
) -> Mapping[str, Any] | None:
    return next(
        (
            event
            for event in _json_list(
                getattr(evaluation, "correction_history_json", None)
            )
            if isinstance(event, Mapping)
            and event.get("idempotency_key") == idempotency_key
        ),
        None,
    )


def submit_correction_nodes(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run: Any,
    item: Any,
    contract_hash: str,
    nodes: list[Mapping[str, Any]],
    review_revision: int,
    idempotency_key: str,
    actor: str,
    real_execution_confirmed: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Validate and append one atomic batch of human node corrections."""

    contract, expected_hash, _ = _run_contract(run)
    if contract is None:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            409,
            "CORRECTION_CONTRACT_UNAVAILABLE",
            "该历史运行没有完整纠偏合同，只能只读查看",
        )
    actual_hash = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    frozen_hash = expected_hash or actual_hash
    if contract_hash != frozen_hash or actual_hash != frozen_hash:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            409,
            "CORRECTION_CONTRACT_STALE",
            "纠偏合同已变化，请刷新后重试",
        )
    contract_errors = validate_correction_contract(contract, presentation_overlay=True)
    if contract_errors:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            409,
            "CORRECTION_CONTRACT_INCOMPLETE",
            "本轮冻结纠偏合同不完整，只能只读查看",
            fields=contract_errors,
        )
    if getattr(item, "run_id", None) != getattr(run, "id", None):
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            409,
            "CORRECTION_ITEM_RUN_MISMATCH",
            "纠偏条目不属于该运行",
        )
    evaluation = getattr(item, "evaluation", None)
    if evaluation is None:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            409,
            "CORRECTION_RESULT_UNAVAILABLE",
            "该条目尚无可纠偏的评测结果",
        )
    if not isinstance(idempotency_key, str) or not idempotency_key.strip():
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            422,
            "CORRECTION_IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID",
            "幂等键不能为空",
        )
    if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not nodes:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            422,
            "CORRECTION_NODES_EMPTY",
            "至少提交一个纠偏节点",
        )

    submission_hash = _submission_payload_hash(
        contract_hash=contract_hash,
        nodes=nodes,
    )
    existing = _existing_idempotent_event(evaluation, idempotency_key)
    if existing is not None:
        if existing.get("submission_hash") != submission_hash:
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                409,
                "CORRECTION_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
                "幂等键已用于不同的纠偏内容",
            )
        replay = build_correction_view(db, run=run, item=item)
        replay["idempotent_replay"] = True
        return replay

    runtime_contract = _materialize_runtime_contract(contract, item)
    by_key = {
        str(node.get("node_key")): node
        for node in runtime_contract.get("nodes", [])
        if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("node_key")
    }
    prepared: list[tuple[Mapping[str, Any], Mapping[str, Any]]] = []
    current_scoring = _json_object(getattr(evaluation, "scoring_json", None))
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for request_node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(request_node, Mapping):
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422,
                "CORRECTION_NODE_INVALID",
                "纠偏节点必须是对象",
            )
        node_key = str(request_node.get("node_key") or "")
        if node_key not in by_key:
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                409,
                "CORRECTION_NODE_UNKNOWN",
                f"节点 {node_key or '未命名'} 不属于本轮冻结合同",
                fields=[node_key] if node_key else [],
            )
        if node_key in seen:
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422,
                "CORRECTION_NODE_DUPLICATE",
                f"节点 {node_key} 不能重复提交",
                fields=[node_key],
            )
        seen.add(node_key)
        contract_node = by_key[node_key]
        if derived_deduction_node(contract_node, current_scoring):
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422, "CORRECTION_DERIVED_SCORE_READ_ONLY",
                "此项由冻结规则计算，请纠偏调用 B 的规则命中或美感初始分",
                fields=[node_key],
            )
        metadata = contract_node.get("metadata")
        if isinstance(metadata, Mapping) and metadata.get("editable") is False:
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422,
                "CORRECTION_NODE_READ_ONLY",
                f"节点 {node_key} 属于冻结规则，只能查看，不能直接修改",
                fields=[node_key],
            )
        runtime_node_id = (
            str(metadata.get("node_instance_id") or "")
            if isinstance(metadata, Mapping)
            else ""
        )
        runtime_output = _workflow_runtime_outputs(item).get(runtime_node_id)
        if (
            runtime_node_id
            and isinstance(runtime_output, Mapping)
            and runtime_output.get("status") == "skipped"
        ):
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422,
                "CORRECTION_NODE_READ_ONLY",
                f"节点 {node_key} 本次未执行，只能查看，不能修改",
                fields=[node_key],
            )
        value_type = str(contract_node.get("type") or "").lower()
        if (
            value_type not in SUPPORTED_CORRECTION_NODE_TYPES
            and not value_type.startswith(("integer_", "number_", "float_"))
        ):
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422,
                "CORRECTION_NODE_TYPE_UNSUPPORTED",
                f"节点 {node_key} 的类型不受支持",
                fields=[node_key],
            )
        try:
            validate_node_value(contract_node, request_node.get("human_value"), manual_text=True)
        except ContractValidationError as exc:
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422,
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                fields=exc.fields,
            ) from exc
        reason = request_node.get("reason")
        if not isinstance(reason, str) or not reason.strip():
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422,
                "CORRECTION_REASON_REQUIRED",
                f"节点 {node_key} 必须填写人工决策理由",
                fields=[node_key],
            )
        evidence = request_node.get("evidence", [])
        if not isinstance(evidence, list):
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422,
                "CORRECTION_EVIDENCE_INVALID",
                f"节点 {node_key} 的证据必须是数组",
                fields=[node_key],
            )
        if _evidence_required(contract_node) and not evidence:
            raise CorrectionViewError(
                422,
                "CORRECTION_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED",
                f"节点 {node_key} 必须提供人工判断证据",
                fields=[node_key],
            )
        prepared.append((contract_node, request_node))

    if int(getattr(evaluation, "review_revision", 0) or 0) != review_revision:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            409,
            "CORRECTION_REVIEW_STALE",
            "审核修订号已变化，请刷新后重试",
        )

    paid_rerun_required = False
    production_run_id = getattr(evaluation, "production_run_id", None)
    if isinstance(production_run_id, int):
        from .workflow_correction import correction_paid_prompt_descendants

        production_run = db.get(ProductionRun, production_run_id)
        if (
            production_run is not None
            and production_run.model_execution_mode == "real"
        ):
            for contract_node, _request_node in prepared:
                metadata = contract_node.get("metadata")
                node_instance_id = (
                    str(metadata.get("node_instance_id") or "")
                    if isinstance(metadata, Mapping)
                    else ""
                )
                if node_instance_id and correction_paid_prompt_descendants(
                    production_run, node_instance_id
                ):
                    paid_rerun_required = True
                    break
    if paid_rerun_required and not real_execution_confirmed:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            409,
            "CORRECTION_PAID_RERUN_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED",
            "本次修改会改变下游提示词输入；请明确确认真实模型费用后再提交",
        )
    if paid_rerun_required and len(prepared) != 1:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            422,
            "CORRECTION_PAID_RERUN_SINGLE_NODE_REQUIRED",
            "涉及真实模型重跑时请一次只提交一个节点，避免重复计费和中间状态歧义",
        )

    savepoint = None if paid_rerun_required else db.begin_nested()
    try:
        for contract_node, request_node in prepared:
            node_key = str(contract_node["node_key"])
            current_roots = _current_roots(item)
            authoritative_values = _values_for_contract(
                current_roots, {"nodes": [contract_node]}
            )
            prior_human = _human_values(
                item,
                runtime_contract,
                frozen_contract_hash=frozen_hash,
            ).get(node_key)
            current_value = (
                deepcopy(prior_human.get("human_value"))
                if isinstance(prior_human, Mapping)
                and "human_value" in prior_human
                else authoritative_values.get(node_key)
            )
            original_evidence = deepcopy(request_node.get("evidence", []))
            correction_key = "contract:" + hashlib.sha256(
                f"{idempotency_key}:{node_key}".encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()[:48]
            metadata = contract_node.get("metadata")
            workflow_node_key = (
                str(metadata.get("workflow_node_key") or "")
                if isinstance(metadata, Mapping)
                else ""
            )
            node_instance_id = (
                str(metadata.get("node_instance_id") or workflow_node_key)
                if isinstance(metadata, Mapping)
                else workflow_node_key
            )
            if workflow_node_key:
                production_run_id = getattr(
                    evaluation, "production_run_id", None
                )
                if isinstance(production_run_id, int):
                    from .workflow_correction import (
                        WorkflowCorrectionError,
                        append_node_correction_and_replay,
                    )

                    field_path = str(
                        metadata.get("output_field_key")
                        or metadata.get("module_field_key")
                        or "output"
                    )
                    try:
                        append_node_correction_and_replay(
                            db,
                            run_id=production_run_id,
                            node_instance_id=node_instance_id,
                            field_path=field_path,
                            value=deepcopy(request_node.get("human_value")),
                            reason=str(
                                request_node.get("reason") or ""
                            ).strip(),
                            actor=actor,
                            correction_key=correction_key,
                            evidence=_normalized_evidence(
                                node_key, original_evidence
                            ),
                            event_metadata={
                                "node_path": str(
                                    contract_node.get("path")
                                    or contract_node["node_key"]
                                ),
                                "run_id": getattr(run, "id", None),
                            },
                            foreach_item_key=(
                                str(metadata.get("foreach_item_key"))
                                if isinstance(
                                    metadata.get("foreach_item_key"), str
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                            foreach_item_field_path=(
                                str(
                                    metadata.get("foreach_item_field_path")
                                )
                                if isinstance(
                                    metadata.get("foreach_item_field_path"),
                                    str,
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                            real_execution_confirmed=(
                                real_execution_confirmed
                            ),
                        )
                    except WorkflowCorrectionError as exc:
                        raise CorrectionViewError(
                            exc.status_code,
                            exc.code,
                            str(exc),
                            fields=[node_key],
                        ) from exc
                else:
                    history = _json_list(evaluation.correction_history_json)
                    prior = _human_values(
                        item,
                        runtime_contract,
                        frozen_contract_hash=frozen_hash,
                    ).get(node_key)
                    old_value = (
                        deepcopy(prior.get("human_value"))
                        if isinstance(prior, Mapping)
                        and "human_value" in prior
                        else current_value
                    )
                    history.append(
                        {
                            "correction_key": correction_key,
                            "node_type": "workflow_node",
                            "node_path": str(
                                contract_node.get("path")
                                or contract_node["node_key"]
                            ),
                            "old_value": old_value,
                            "new_value": deepcopy(
                                request_node.get("human_value")
                            ),
                            "evidence": _normalized_evidence(
                                node_key, original_evidence
                            ),
                            "reason": str(
                                request_node.get("reason") or ""
                            ).strip(),
                            "reason_codes": [],
                            "corrector": actor,
                            "corrected_at": platform_isoformat(datetime.now(timezone.utc)),
                            "downstream_recomputed": False,
                            "workflow_node_key": workflow_node_key,
                            "node_instance_id": node_instance_id,
                            "workflow_kind": metadata.get("workflow_kind"),
                            "run_id": getattr(run, "id", None),
                        }
                    )
                    evaluation.correction_history_json = json.dumps(
                        history, ensure_ascii=False
                    )
                    db.flush()
            else:
                apply_node_correction(
                    db,
                    result=evaluation,
                    payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
                        correction_key=correction_key,
                        node_type=_node_runtime_type(contract_node),
                        node_path=str(
                            contract_node.get("path") or contract_node["node_key"]
                        ),
                        old_value=current_value,
                        new_value=deepcopy(request_node.get("human_value")),
                        evidence=_normalized_evidence(node_key, original_evidence),
                        reason=str(request_node.get("reason") or "").strip(),
                    ),
                    corrector=actor,
                )
            history = _json_list(evaluation.correction_history_json)
            event = next(
                (
                    entry
                    for entry in reversed(history)
                    if isinstance(entry, dict)
                    and entry.get("correction_key") == correction_key
                ),
                None,
            )
            if event is None:
                raise CorrectionViewError(
                    409,
                    "CORRECTION_HISTORY_APPEND_FAILED",
                    f"节点 {node_key} 未形成可审计的纠偏历史",
                    fields=[node_key],
                )
            event.update(
                {
                    "schema_version": "contract-node-correction-v1",
                    "node_key": node_key,
                    "old_value": deepcopy(current_value),
                    "contract_hash": contract_hash,
                    "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                    "submission_hash": submission_hash,
                    "submitted_review_revision": review_revision,
                    "resulting_review_revision": review_revision + 1,
                    "contract_evidence": original_evidence,
                    **(
                        {"workflow_node_key": workflow_node_key}
                        if workflow_node_key
                        else {}
                    ),
                    **(
                        {
                            "node_instance_id": node_instance_id,
                            "workflow_kind": metadata.get("workflow_kind"),
                            "module_type_key": metadata.get(
                                "module_type_key"
                            ),
                            "module_field_key": metadata.get(
                                "module_field_key"
                            ),
                            "output_field_key": metadata.get(
                                "output_field_key"
                            ),
                            "foreach_item_key": metadata.get(
                                "foreach_item_key"
                            ),
                            "foreach_item_field_path": metadata.get(
