                                "foreach_item_field_path"
                            ),
                            "field_identity": deepcopy(
                                metadata.get("field_identity")
                            ),
                            "run_id": getattr(run, "id", None),
                            "workflow_definition_id": (
                                contract.get("workflow", {}).get(
                                    "workflow_definition_id"
                                )
                                if isinstance(
                                    contract.get("workflow"), Mapping
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                            "workflow_version_id": (
                                contract.get("workflow", {}).get(
                                    "workflow_version_id"
                                )
                                if isinstance(
                                    contract.get("workflow"), Mapping
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                            "workflow_canonical_hash": (
                                contract.get("workflow", {}).get(
                                    "canonical_hash"
                                )
                                if isinstance(
                                    contract.get("workflow"), Mapping
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                            "frozen_asset_identity": {
                                key: deepcopy(metadata[key])
                                for key in (
                                    "component_version",
                                    "prompt_version",
                                    "model_version",
                                    "rule_version",
                                    "selected_parameters",
                                )
                                if key in metadata
                            },
                        }
                        if workflow_node_key
                        and isinstance(metadata, Mapping)
                        else {}
                    ),
                }
            )
            evaluation.correction_history_json = json.dumps(
                history, ensure_ascii=False
            )
            if (
                not workflow_node_key
                and _node_runtime_type(contract_node) == "final_level"
                and getattr(run, "baseline_set_id", None) is not None
            ):
                category_key = (
                    getattr(run, "category_key", None)
                    or
                    getattr(getattr(evaluation, "job", None), "category_key", None)
                    or getattr(getattr(evaluation, "asset", None), "category_key", None)
                )
                if isinstance(category_key, str) and category_key:
                    persist_human_level_truth(
                        db,
                        category_key=category_key,
                        asset_id=int(evaluation.asset_id),
                        source_result_id=int(evaluation.id),
                        level=str(request_node.get("human_value")),
                        actor=actor,
                        reason=str(request_node.get("reason") or "人工纠偏更新最终等级"),
                    )
        evaluation.review_revision = review_revision + 1
        db.flush()
        if savepoint is not None:
            savepoint.commit()
    except Exception:
        if savepoint is not None:
            savepoint.rollback()
        else:
            db.rollback()
        raise

    return build_correction_view(db, run=run, item=item)

===== FILE backend/app/cover_attempts.py =====
"""Durable generation attempts, independent from HTTP request lifetimes.

Workers must claim an attempt before invoking ``advance``. The submission intent
is committed before any gateway POST: recovery always queries that same ID.
This module does not activate workflows or send results to business downstreams.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import uuid

from sqlalchemy import Integer, String, Text, UniqueConstraint, select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, Session, mapped_column

from .database import Base
from .cover_gateway import CoverGateway, CoverGatewayError, CoverParameters, _image_info


class CoverGenerationAttempt(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cover_generation_attempts"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("sample_key", "sequence_no", name="uq_cover_sample_sequence"),)
    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), primary_key=True)
    sample_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), index=True)
    sequence_no: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), index=True)
    input_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    uploaded_image: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    prompt_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=True)
    result_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    error_code: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40))
    updated_at: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40))


def _now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def create_attempt(db: Session, *, sample_key: str, sequence_no: int, image: bytes,
                   prompt: str, parameters: CoverParameters, input_directory: Path) -> CoverGenerationAttempt:
    """Freeze source bytes before queueing. Caller commits its enclosing transaction."""
    if not sample_key or len(sample_key) > 160 or type(sequence_no) is not int or sequence_no < 1:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_sample_identity")
    parameters.validate()
    if not isinstance(prompt, str) or not prompt.strip() or len(prompt) > 8000:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_prompt")
    _image_info(image)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(image).hexdigest()
    input_directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    source = input_directory / digest
    try:
        with source.open("xb") as file:
            file.write(image)
    except FileExistsError:
        if hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
            raise CoverGatewayError("input_snapshot_corrupted")
    snapshot = {"image_sha256": digest, "prompt": prompt, "parameters": vars(parameters),
                "provider": "cover-image-lan-v1", "mode": "image_edit"}
    now = _now()
    attempt = CoverGenerationAttempt(id="cover-" + uuid.uuid4().hex, sample_key=sample_key,
        sequence_no=sequence_no, state="prepared", input_json=json.dumps(snapshot, ensure_ascii=False),
        created_at=now, updated_at=now)
    db.add(attempt)
    db.flush()
    return attempt


def _transition(db: Session, attempt_id: str, source: str, target: str, **values) -> bool:
    changed = db.execute(update(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(
        CoverGenerationAttempt.id == attempt_id, CoverGenerationAttempt.state == source
    ).values(state=target, updated_at=_now(), **values)).rowcount
    db.commit()
    return changed == 1


def advance(db: Session, attempt_id: str, *, gateway: CoverGateway,
            input_directory: Path, output_directory: Path, allow_submit: bool = True) -> str:
    """Perform bounded network work; paused rounds pass allow_submit=False.

    prepared -> uploading -> ready -> submitting -> submitted/queued/running
    -> succeeded (only after local durable download). Upload has no generation
    side effect; interrupted uploads can be explicitly recovered to prepared.
    A worker crash in submitting is ALWAYS query-only on the next invocation.
    """
    row = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
    if row is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("attempt_not_found")
    snapshot = json.loads(row.input_json)
    state = row.state
    if state in {"succeeded", "failed", "needs_review", "uploading", "downloading"}:
        return state
    if state in {"prepared", "ready"} and not allow_submit:
        return state
    if state == "prepared":
        if not _transition(db, attempt_id, "prepared", "uploading"):
            return "contended"
        try:
            data = (input_directory / snapshot["image_sha256"]).read_bytes()
            if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != snapshot["image_sha256"]:
                raise CoverGatewayError("input_snapshot_corrupted")
            uploaded = gateway.upload(data)
        except (CoverGatewayError, OSError) as exc:
            _transition(db, attempt_id, "uploading", "needs_review",
                        error_code=getattr(exc, "code", "input_unavailable"))
            return "needs_review"
        _transition(db, attempt_id, "uploading", "ready", uploaded_image=uploaded)
        return "ready"
    if state == "ready":
        uploaded_image = row.uploaded_image
        if not _transition(db, attempt_id, "ready", "submitting"):
            return "contended"
        try:
            job = gateway.submit(request_id=attempt_id, image=uploaded_image,
                                 prompt=snapshot["prompt"], parameters=CoverParameters(**snapshot["parameters"]))
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            target = "submitting" if exc.uncertain else ("ready" if exc.retryable else "needs_review")
            _transition(db, attempt_id, "submitting", target, error_code=exc.code)
            return target
        target = "needs_review" if job.state in {"submission_unknown", "history_unavailable"} else "submitted"
        _transition(db, attempt_id, "submitting", target, prompt_id=job.prompt_id,
                    error_code=job.state if target == "needs_review" else None)
        return target
    if state not in {"submitting", "submitted", "queued", "running"}:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_attempt_state")
    try:
        job = gateway.query(attempt_id)
    except CoverGatewayError as exc:
        # Even a 404 cannot prove a timed-out submission did not execute.
        if exc.uncertain:
            _transition(db, attempt_id, state, "needs_review", error_code=exc.code)
            return "needs_review"
        return state
    if job.state in {"submission_unknown", "history_unavailable"}:
        _transition(db, attempt_id, state, "needs_review", error_code=job.state)
        return "needs_review"
    if job.state == "succeeded":
        if not _transition(db, attempt_id, state, "downloading", prompt_id=job.prompt_id):
            return "contended"
        try:
            stored = gateway.download(attempt_id, directory=output_directory)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            _transition(db, attempt_id, "downloading", "submitted", error_code=exc.code)
            return "submitted"
        result = {"sha256": stored.sha256, "bytes": stored.size_bytes,
                  "width": stored.width, "height": stored.height}
        _transition(db, attempt_id, "downloading", "succeeded", result_json=json.dumps(result), error_code=None)
        return "succeeded"
    _transition(db, attempt_id, state, job.state, prompt_id=job.prompt_id)
    return job.state


def recover_local_stage(db: Session, attempt_id: str) -> bool:
    """Call only after proving the owning worker stopped (no time-based stealing).

    Upload/download can be safely repeated; submitting must remain query-only.
    """
    row = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
    if row and row.state in {"uploading", "downloading"}:
        return _transition(db, attempt_id, row.state, "prepared" if row.state == "uploading" else "submitted")
    return False


def latest_success(db: Session, sample_key: str) -> CoverGenerationAttempt | None:
    return db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(
        CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key == sample_key, CoverGenerationAttempt.state == "succeeded"
    ).order_by(CoverGenerationAttempt.sequence_no.desc()).limit(1))

===== FILE backend/app/cover_gateway.py =====
"""Short-request adapter for the fixed LAN cover gateway (not ComfyUI).

The caller owns the durable attempt ledger: persist request_id + uploaded image
before submit. This adapter never retries a POST or generates a replacement ID.
No browser Origin, bearer credentials, proxy environment or redirects are used.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import io
import math
from pathlib import Path
import re
import tempfile
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import httpx
from PIL import Image


REQUEST_ID = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,80}")
UPLOAD_NAME = re.compile(r"lan-[0-9a-f]{32}\.png")
STATES = frozenset({"submitted", "queued", "running", "succeeded", "failed",
                    "submission_unknown", "history_unavailable"})
MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024


class CoverGatewayError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, *, uncertain: bool = False, retryable: bool = False):
        # Never include response bodies, prompts or local paths in exceptions.
        super().__init__(code)
        self.code = code
        self.uncertain = uncertain
        self.retryable = retryable


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CoverParameters:
    width: int = 512
    height: int = 512
    steps: int = 20
    seed: int = 20261019
    cfg: float = 4.0

    def validate(self) -> None:
        if any(type(v) is not int for v in (self.width, self.height, self.steps, self.seed)):
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_integer_parameter")
        if any(v < 256 or v > 1024 or v % 64 for v in (self.width, self.height)):
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_dimensions")
        if not 1 <= self.steps <= 40 or not 0 <= self.seed < 2**63:
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_generation_parameter")
        if type(self.cfg) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(self.cfg) or not 1 <= self.cfg <= 8:
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_cfg")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CoverJob:
    request_id: str
    state: str
    prompt_id: str | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StoredCover:
    path: Path
    sha256: str
    size_bytes: int
    width: int
    height: int


def _request_id(value: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not REQUEST_ID.fullmatch(value):
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_request_id")
    return value


def _image_info(data: bytes, *, png_only: bool = False) -> tuple[int, int]:
    if not data or len(data) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_image_size")
    try:
        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(data)) as image:
            if image.format not in ({"PNG"} if png_only else {"PNG", "JPEG", "WEBP"}):
                raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_image_format")
            if image.width * image.height > 25_000_000:
                raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_image_dimensions")
            dimensions = image.size
            image.verify()
            return dimensions
    except CoverGatewayError:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_image") from exc


class CoverGateway:
    def __init__(self, base_url: str, *, transport: httpx.BaseTransport | None = None):
        url = urlsplit(base_url)
        if (url.scheme not in {"http", "https"} or not url.hostname or url.username
                or url.password or url.query or url.fragment or url.path not in {"", "/"}):
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_gateway_url")
        # Server-owned configuration only, never a caller-supplied image URL.
        self._client = httpx.Client(base_url=base_url.rstrip("/"), transport=transport,
                                   trust_env=False, follow_redirects=False,
                                   timeout=httpx.Timeout(30, connect=5))

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._client.close()

    def __enter__(self) -> CoverGateway:
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *_: Any) -> None:
        self.close()

    def _json(self, method: str, path: str, *, submitting: bool = False, **kwargs: Any) -> dict:
        try:
            response = self._client.request(method, path, **kwargs)
        except httpx.TransportError as exc:
            raise CoverGatewayError("submission_unknown" if submitting else "gateway_unreachable",
                                    uncertain=submitting, retryable=not submitting) from exc
        if response.status_code == 429:
            raise CoverGatewayError("queue_full", retryable=True)
        if response.status_code == 404:
            # A missing gateway record after submission is not permission to regenerate.
            raise CoverGatewayError("job_not_found", uncertain=path.startswith("/v1/jobs/"))
        if not 200 <= response.status_code < 300:
            uncertain = submitting and (response.status_code >= 500 or response.status_code == 409)
            raise CoverGatewayError("submission_unknown" if uncertain else f"gateway_http_{response.status_code}",
                                    uncertain=uncertain, retryable=not submitting and response.status_code >= 500)
        try:
            value = response.json()
            if not isinstance(value, dict):
                raise ValueError("not an object")
            return value
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise CoverGatewayError("gateway_invalid_response", uncertain=submitting) from exc

    def health(self) -> dict:
        return self._json("GET", "/health")

    def upload(self, data: bytes) -> str:
        _image_info(data)
        response = self._json("POST", "/v1/images",
                              files={"image": ("input", data, "application/octet-stream")})
        name = response.get("image")
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not UPLOAD_NAME.fullmatch(name):
            raise CoverGatewayError("gateway_invalid_upload_name")
        return name

    @staticmethod
    def _job(value: dict, request_id: str, *, submitting: bool) -> CoverJob:
        state, prompt_id = value.get("state"), value.get("prompt_id")
        if (value.get("request_id") != request_id or state not in STATES
                or (prompt_id is not None and (not isinstance(prompt_id, str) or not prompt_id))
                or (state not in {"submission_unknown", "history_unavailable"} and not prompt_id)):
            raise CoverGatewayError("gateway_invalid_job", uncertain=submitting)
        return CoverJob(request_id, state, prompt_id)

    def submit(self, *, request_id: str, image: str | None, prompt: str,
               parameters: CoverParameters) -> CoverJob:
        _request_id(request_id)
        parameters.validate()
        if image is None:
            # Never pretend a blank reference image is a verified text-to-image model.
            raise CoverGatewayError("text_to_image_not_supported")
        if not isinstance(image, str) or not UPLOAD_NAME.fullmatch(image):
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_uploaded_image")
        if not isinstance(prompt, str) or not prompt.strip() or len(prompt) > 8000:
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_prompt")
        body = {"request_id": request_id, "image": image, "prompt": prompt,
                "width": parameters.width, "height": parameters.height,
                "steps": parameters.steps, "seed": parameters.seed, "cfg": parameters.cfg}
        return self._job(self._json("POST", "/v1/jobs", submitting=True, json=body),
                         request_id, submitting=True)

    def query(self, request_id: str) -> CoverJob:
        _request_id(request_id)
        return self._job(self._json("GET", f"/v1/jobs/{request_id}"), request_id, submitting=False)

    def download(self, request_id: str, *, directory: Path) -> StoredCover:
        """Download to a content-addressed owned store; never overwrite another result."""
        _request_id(request_id)
        data = bytearray()
        try:
            with self._client.stream("GET", f"/v1/jobs/{request_id}/image") as response:
                if response.status_code != 200:
                    raise CoverGatewayError(f"download_http_{response.status_code}", retryable=True)
                for chunk in response.iter_bytes(64 * 1024):
                    data.extend(chunk)
                    if len(data) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
                        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_image_size")
        except httpx.TransportError as exc:
            raise CoverGatewayError("download_interrupted", retryable=True) from exc
        width, height = _image_info(bytes(data), png_only=True)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        destination = directory / f"{digest}.png"
        if destination.exists():
            if hashlib.sha256(destination.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
                raise CoverGatewayError("stored_image_corrupted")
        else:
            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=directory, delete=False, suffix=".part") as file:
                temporary = Path(file.name)
                file.write(data)
            try:
                # Content address makes concurrent same-file replacement equivalent.
                temporary.replace(destination)
            finally:
                temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        return StoredCover(destination, digest, len(data), width, height)

===== FILE backend/app/cover_regression.py =====
"""Image-generation stock rounds using existing baseline sets as sample sources."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
from pathlib import Path
import uuid

from sqlalchemy import ForeignKey, Integer, String, Text, UniqueConstraint, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, Session, mapped_column

from .database import Base, reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction
from .cover_attempts import CoverGenerationAttempt, create_attempt, latest_success
from .cover_gateway import CoverGatewayError, CoverParameters, MAX_IMAGE_BYTES


class CoverRegressionRun(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cover_regression_runs"
    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), primary_key=True)
    baseline_set_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("baseline_sets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"))
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), index=True)
    config_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40))
    last_dispatched: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="", server_default="")


class CoverRegressionItem(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cover_regression_items"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("run_id", "baseline_item_id", name="uq_cover_run_baseline_item"),)
    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), primary_key=True)
    run_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("cover_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True)
    baseline_item_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("baseline_set_items.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"))
    asset_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("assets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"))
    original_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500))
    input_error: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)


class CoverRegressionReview(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cover_regression_reviews"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("item_id", "revision", name="uq_cover_review_revision"),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    item_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("cover_regression_items.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True)
    attempt_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("cover_generation_attempts.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"))
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    verdict: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24))
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    actor: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40))


def create_round(db: Session, *, baseline_set_id: int, prompt: str, parameters: CoverParameters,
                 actor: str, settings, request_id: str) -> CoverRegressionRun:
    from .models import Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem
    from .nas_storage import resolve_asset_path
    from .cover_gateway import _request_id
    _request_id(request_id)
    parameters.validate()
    if not prompt.strip() or len(prompt) > 8000:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_prompt")
    config = json.dumps({"prompt": prompt, "parameters": vars(parameters),
                         "mode": "image_edit", "provider": "cover-image-lan-v1"}, sort_keys=True)
    previous = db.get(CoverRegressionRun, request_id)
    if previous:
        if previous.baseline_set_id != baseline_set_id or previous.config_json != config:
            raise CoverGatewayError("request_conflict")
        return previous
    baseline = db.get(BaselineSet, baseline_set_id)
    if baseline is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("baseline_not_found")
    samples = db.scalars(select(BaselineSetItem).where(BaselineSetItem.baseline_set_id == baseline_set_id)
                         .order_by(BaselineSetItem.id)).all()
    if not samples:
        raise CoverGatewayError("baseline_empty")
    run = CoverRegressionRun(id=request_id, baseline_set_id=baseline.id, category_key=baseline.category_key,
        name=baseline.name, status="running", config_json=config, created_by=actor,
        created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    for sample in samples:
        asset = db.get(Asset, sample.asset_id)
        if asset is None or asset.category_key != baseline.category_key:
            raise CoverGatewayError("sample_category_mismatch")
        item = CoverRegressionItem(id=uuid.uuid4().hex, run_id=run.id, baseline_item_id=sample.id,
                                  asset_id=asset.id, original_name=asset.original_name)
        db.add(item)
        try:
            source = resolve_asset_path(asset, settings)
            with source.open("rb") as file:
                image = file.read(MAX_IMAGE_BYTES + 1)
            create_attempt(db, sample_key=item.id, sequence_no=1, image=image, prompt=prompt,
                           parameters=parameters, input_directory=settings.data_dir / "stock-images" / "inputs")
        except (CoverGatewayError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
            item.input_error = getattr(exc, "code", "input_unavailable")
    db.flush()
    return run


def change_status(db: Session, run_id: str, action: str) -> CoverRegressionRun:
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    run = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionRun).where(CoverRegressionRun.id == run_id).with_for_update())
    if run is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("round_not_found")
    if action == "pause" and run.status in {"running", "paused"}:
        run.status = "paused"
    elif action == "resume" and run.status in {"paused", "running"}:
        run.status = "running"
    elif action == "cancel":
        run.status = "cancelled"
    else:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_round_action")
    return run


def review_item(db: Session, *, item_id: str, attempt_id: str, expected_revision: int,
                verdict: str, reason: str, actor: str) -> CoverRegressionReview:
    if verdict not in {"adopt", "regenerate", "uncertain"}:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_review_verdict")
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    item = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.id == item_id).with_for_update())
    if item is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("item_not_found")
    attempt = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
    if attempt is None or attempt.sample_key != item_id or attempt.state != "succeeded":
        raise CoverGatewayError("review_result_unavailable")
    old = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionReview).where(CoverRegressionReview.item_id == item_id)
                    .order_by(CoverRegressionReview.revision.desc()).limit(1).with_for_update())
    if (old.revision if old else 0) != expected_revision:
        raise CoverGatewayError("review_revision_conflict")
    row = CoverRegressionReview(item_id=item_id, attempt_id=attempt_id,
        revision=expected_revision + 1, verdict=verdict, reason=reason, actor=actor,
        created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def regenerate_item(db: Session, *, item_id: str, input_directory: Path) -> CoverGenerationAttempt:
    """Regeneration is explicit; ambiguous old attempts must first be reconciled."""
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    item = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.id == item_id).with_for_update())
    if item is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("item_not_found")
    run = db.get(CoverRegressionRun, item.run_id)
    if run.status == "cancelled":
        raise CoverGatewayError("round_cancelled")
    last = db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key == item_id)
                     .order_by(CoverGenerationAttempt.sequence_no.desc()).limit(1).with_for_update())
    if last is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("input_snapshot_missing")
    if last.state not in {"succeeded", "failed"}:
        raise CoverGatewayError("original_attempt_not_reconciled")
    frozen = json.loads(last.input_json)
    source = input_directory / frozen["image_sha256"]
    with source.open("rb") as file:
        image = file.read(MAX_IMAGE_BYTES + 1)
    import hashlib
    if hashlib.sha256(image).hexdigest() != frozen["image_sha256"]:
        raise CoverGatewayError("input_snapshot_corrupted")
    return create_attempt(db, sample_key=item_id, sequence_no=last.sequence_no + 1,
        image=image, prompt=frozen["prompt"], parameters=CoverParameters(**frozen["parameters"]),
        input_directory=input_directory)


def item_payload(db: Session, item: CoverRegressionItem) -> dict:
    attempts = db.scalars(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key == item.id)
                          .order_by(CoverGenerationAttempt.sequence_no.desc())).all()
    review = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionReview).where(CoverRegressionReview.item_id == item.id)
                       .order_by(CoverRegressionReview.revision.desc()).limit(1))
    successful = next((a for a in attempts if a.state == "succeeded"), None)
    # A confirmed human selection remains authoritative after later AI generation.
    effective = next((a for a in attempts if review and a.id == review.attempt_id), successful)
    return {"id": item.id, "asset_id": item.asset_id, "name": item.original_name,
        "input_error": item.input_error, "state": attempts[0].state if attempts else "input_error",
        "effective_attempt_id": effective.id if effective else None,
        "review": {"revision": review.revision, "verdict": review.verdict,
                   "reason": review.reason, "attempt_id": review.attempt_id} if review else None,
        "attempts": [{"id": a.id, "sequence": a.sequence_no, "state": a.state,
                      "error": a.error_code, "result": json.loads(a.result_json) if a.result_json else None}
                     for a in attempts]}

===== FILE backend/app/cover_regression_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from typing import Callable, Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy import select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .authz import has_permission, require_categories
from .config import get_settings
from .database import get_db
from .cover_attempts import CoverGenerationAttempt
from .cover_gateway import CoverGatewayError, CoverParameters
from .cover_regression import (CoverRegressionRun, CoverRegressionItem, change_status,
                               create_round, item_payload, regenerate_item, review_item)


class CreateCoverRound(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    request_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=64, pattern=r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$")
    prompt: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=8000)
    width: int = 512
    height: int = 512
    steps: int = Field(default=20, ge=1, le=40)
    seed: int = Field(default=20261019, ge=0, lt=2**63)
    cfg: float = Field(default=4.0, ge=1, le=8)


class CoverReviewRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    attempt_id: str
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=0)
    verdict: Literal["adopt", "regenerate", "uncertain"]
    reason: str = Field(default="", max_length=4000)


def build_cover_regression_router(current_user: Callable) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(tags=["stock-image-generation"])

    def writable(user, category):
        if not has_permission(user, "reviews:write"):
            raise HTTPException(403, "需要该业务的纠偏权限")
        require_categories(user, {category})

    def run_row(db, run_id):
        row = db.get(CoverRegressionRun, run_id)
        if row is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, "生图回归不存在")
        return row

    def item_row(db, run_id, item_id):
        item = db.get(CoverRegressionItem, item_id)
        if item is None or item.run_id != run_id:
            raise HTTPException(404, "样本不属于本轮")
        return item

    def summary(row):
        return {"id": row.id, "baseline_set_id": row.baseline_set_id, "name": row.name,
                "category_key": row.category_key, "status": row.status,
                "config": json.loads(row.config_json), "created_at": row.created_at}

    @router.get("/api/cover-regressions/capabilities")
    def capabilities(user=Depends(current_user)):
        return {"image_edit": bool(os.environ.get("COVER_GATEWAY_URL")), "text_to_image": False,
                "validated_sizes": [512], "human_review_required": False,
                "downstream_write": False, "estimated_seconds_per_image": 490,
                "cost": None}

    @router.post("/api/baseline-sets/{baseline_set_id}/cover-regressions")
    def create(baseline_set_id: int, payload: CreateCoverRound, user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        from .models import BaselineSet
        baseline = db.get(BaselineSet, baseline_set_id)
        if baseline is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, "基准集不存在")
        writable(user, baseline.category_key)
        if not os.environ.get("COVER_GATEWAY_URL"):
            raise HTTPException(503, "尚未配置生图网关")
        try:
            row = create_round(db, baseline_set_id=baseline_set_id, prompt=payload.prompt,
                parameters=CoverParameters(**payload.model_dump(exclude={"request_id", "prompt"})),
                actor=user.username, settings=get_settings(), request_id=payload.request_id)
            db.commit()
            return summary(row)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(409, exc.code) from exc

    @router.get("/api/cover-regressions")
    def listing(baseline_set_id: int | None = None, user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        query = select(CoverRegressionRun)
        if baseline_set_id is not None:
            query = query.where(CoverRegressionRun.baseline_set_id == baseline_set_id)
        return {"items": [summary(row) for row in db.scalars(query.order_by(CoverRegressionRun.created_at.desc()).limit(100))]}

    @router.get("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}")
    def detail(run_id: str, offset: int = Query(0, ge=0), limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=100),
               user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        row = run_row(db, run_id)
        total = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id == run_id))
        items = db.scalars(select(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id == run_id)
                          .order_by(CoverRegressionItem.baseline_item_id).offset(offset).limit(limit)).all()
        return {**summary(row), "total": total, "offset": offset, "items": [item_payload(db, i) for i in items]}

    @router.post("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/control/{action}")
    def control(run_id: str, action: Literal["pause", "resume", "cancel"],
                user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        writable(user, run_row(db, run_id).category_key)
        try:
            row = change_status(db, run_id, action)
            db.commit()
            return summary(row)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(409, exc.code) from exc

    @router.post("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/review")
    def review(run_id: str, item_id: str, payload: CoverReviewRequest,
               user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        writable(user, run_row(db, run_id).category_key)
        item = item_row(db, run_id, item_id)
        try:
            review_item(db, item_id=item_id, actor=user.username, **payload.model_dump())
            db.commit()
            return item_payload(db, item)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(409, exc.code) from exc

    @router.post("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/regenerate")
    def regenerate(run_id: str, item_id: str, user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        writable(user, run_row(db, run_id).category_key)
        item = item_row(db, run_id, item_id)
        try:
            regenerate_item(db, item_id=item_id, input_directory=get_settings().data_dir / "stock-images" / "inputs")
            db.commit()
            return item_payload(db, item)
        except (CoverGatewayError, OSError) as exc:
            raise HTTPException(409, getattr(exc, "code", "input_snapshot_unavailable")) from exc

    @router.get("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/image")
    def image(run_id: str, item_id: str, attempt_id: str,
              user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        item_row(db, run_id, item_id)
        attempt = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
        if attempt is None or attempt.sample_key != item_id or attempt.state != "succeeded":
            raise HTTPException(404, "图片尚未生成")
        result = json.loads(attempt.result_json)
        import re
        digest = result.get("sha256", "")
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", digest):
            raise HTTPException(409, "图片存储记录异常")
        path = get_settings().data_dir / "stock-images" / "outputs" / f"{digest}.png"
        if not path.is_file():
            raise HTTPException(404, "图片文件不可用")
        return FileResponse(path, media_type="image/png", headers={"Cache-Control": "private, max-age=3600"})

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/cover_worker.py =====
"""Opt-in short-tick image worker. No in-memory generation queue.

The global advisory lock serializes short HTTP interactions, not GPU execution.
The gateway retains at most two submitted jobs; remaining work stays in SQL.
"""
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import os
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy import select, text, or_, and_
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import NullPool
from sqlalchemy import create_engine

from .cover_attempts import CoverGenerationAttempt, advance, recover_local_stage
from .cover_gateway import CoverGateway
from .cover_regression import CoverRegressionItem, CoverRegressionRun
from .database import reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction


@contextmanager
def scheduler_lock(engine, directory: Path):
    """Never steal a live worker's lock. Connection/process death releases it."""
    if engine.dialect.name == "mysql":
        lock_engine = create_engine(engine.url, poolclass=NullPool)
        with lock_engine.connect() as connection:
            acquired = connection.execute(text("SELECT GET_LOCK('stock-cover-worker', 0)")).scalar() == 1
            try:
                yield acquired
            finally:
                if acquired:
                    connection.execute(text("SELECT RELEASE_LOCK('stock-cover-worker')"))
        lock_engine.dispose()
        return
    if engine.dialect.name != "sqlite":
        raise RuntimeError("cover worker requires verified SQLite or MySQL locking")
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with (directory / "worker.lock").open("a+b") as file:
        file.seek(0, 2)
        if file.tell() == 0:
            file.write(b"0"); file.flush()
        file.seek(0)
        acquired = False
        try:
            if os.name == "nt":
                import msvcrt
                msvcrt.locking(file.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_NBLCK, 1)
            else:
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(file.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            acquired = True
        except OSError:
            pass
        try:
            yield acquired
        finally:
            if acquired:
                file.seek(0)
                if os.name == "nt":
                    msvcrt.locking(file.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
                else:
                    fcntl.flock(file.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)


def tick(engine, *, directory: Path, gateway: CoverGateway) -> bool:
    with scheduler_lock(engine, directory) as acquired:
        if not acquired:
            return False
        with Session(engine) as db:
            # Holding the global worker lock proves no older worker is uploading
            # or downloading. It does NOT prove a submitted GPU job has stopped.
            recovering = db.scalars(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(
                CoverGenerationAttempt.state.in_(["uploading", "downloading"]))).all()
            for row in recovering:
                recover_local_stage(db, row.id)
            threshold = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=5)).isoformat()
            pending = db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(
                CoverGenerationAttempt.state.in_(["submitting", "submitted", "queued", "running"]),
                CoverGenerationAttempt.updated_at <= threshold
            ).order_by(CoverGenerationAttempt.updated_at).limit(1))
            if pending:
                advance(db, pending.id, gateway=gateway, input_directory=directory / "inputs",
                        output_directory=directory / "outputs", allow_submit=False)
                pending.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
                db.commit()
                return True
            # Unknown jobs reserve capacity until reconciled; don't fill the
            # gateway with replacements while old tasks may still be running.
            in_flight = db.scalars(select(CoverGenerationAttempt.id).where(
                or_(CoverGenerationAttempt.state.in_(["submitting", "submitted", "queued", "running"]),
                    and_(CoverGenerationAttempt.state == "needs_review",
                         CoverGenerationAttempt.error_code.in_(["submission_unknown", "history_unavailable", "job_not_found", "gateway_invalid_job", "gateway_invalid_response"])))
            )).all()
            if len(in_flight) >= 2:
                return False
            candidate = db.execute(select(CoverRegressionRun.id, CoverGenerationAttempt.id)
                .join(CoverRegressionItem, CoverRegressionItem.run_id == CoverRegressionRun.id)
                .join(CoverGenerationAttempt, CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key == CoverRegressionItem.id)
                .where(CoverRegressionRun.status == "running", CoverGenerationAttempt.state.in_(["prepared", "ready"]))
                .order_by(CoverRegressionRun.last_dispatched, CoverRegressionRun.id,
                          CoverGenerationAttempt.created_at).limit(1)).first()
            if not candidate:
                return False
            run_id, attempt_id = candidate
            db.rollback()  # lock using a current read, not an earlier MySQL snapshot
            reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
            run = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionRun).where(CoverRegressionRun.id == run_id)
                            .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
            if run.status != "running":
                return False
            run.last_dispatched = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
            db.flush()
            advance(db, attempt_id, gateway=gateway, input_directory=directory / "inputs",
                    output_directory=directory / "outputs", allow_submit=True)
            db.commit()
            return True


def process_cover_tick() -> bool:
    url = os.environ.get("COVER_GATEWAY_URL", "").strip()
    if not url:  # no production behavior change until explicitly configured
        return False
    from .config import get_settings
    from .database import engine
    with CoverGateway(url) as gateway:
        return tick(engine, directory=get_settings().data_dir / "stock-images", gateway=gateway)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import logging
    import time
    if not os.environ.get("COVER_GATEWAY_URL"):
        raise SystemExit("COVER_GATEWAY_URL must be configured before starting this worker")
    while True:
        try:
            process_cover_tick()
        except Exception:
            logging.exception("生图任务处理失败，保留账本并等待下一轮")
        time.sleep(2)

===== FILE backend/app/daily_review_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Callable
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .authz import has_permission
from .daily_review_quota import (
    business_date_at,
    build_daily_review_batch,
    resume_incremental_after_review_overflow,
)
from .database import get_db
from .models import (
    ConnectorPauseState,
    HumanReviewDailyBatch,
    HumanReviewDailyBatchItem,
    HumanReviewOverflowFact,
    HumanReviewQuotaPolicy,
    User,
)


BUCKET_LABELS = {
    "risk_error": "风险与异常",
    "cold_start_drift": "新机制与变化",
    "stratified_random": "分层抽查",
    "golden_sentinel": "黄金样本哨兵",
}
STATE_LABELS = {
    "selected_pending": "待审核",
    "selected_reviewed": "已审核",
    "not_selected": "未抽查",
}


class ReviewCapacityResumeRequest(BaseModel):
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)


def _actor(user: User) -> str:
    return str(user.username or user.display_name or "review-operator")[:80]


def _json_object(raw: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(raw or "{}")
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return {}
    return value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}


def _json_array(raw: str) -> list[str]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(raw or "[]")
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return []
    return [str(item) for item in value if isinstance(item, str)] if isinstance(value, list) else []


def _aware(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    return value if value.tzinfo is not None else value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _evaluation_id_from_key(source_result_key: str) -> int | None:
    prefix = "evaluation-result:"
    if not source_result_key.startswith(prefix):
        return None
    try:
        evaluation_id = int(source_result_key.removeprefix(prefix))
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return evaluation_id if evaluation_id > 0 else None


def _pause_payload(row: ConnectorPauseState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "category_key": row.category_key,
        "mechanism_key": row.mechanism_key,
        "state": row.state,
        "state_label": "已暂停" if row.state == "paused" else "已恢复",
        "reason_code": row.reason_code,
        "reason_label": (
            "严重风险超过今日人审容量"
            if row.reason_code == "human_review_severe_overflow"
            else "人工暂停"
        ),
        "revision": row.revision,
        "paused_at": row.paused_at,
        "resumed_at": row.resumed_at,
    }


def _payload(
    db: Session,
    business_date: date,
    *,
    current: datetime,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    policy = db.scalar(
        select(HumanReviewQuotaPolicy).where(
            HumanReviewQuotaPolicy.active_slot == "global"
        )
    )
    capacity = policy.total_capacity if policy is not None else 100
    capacities = {
        "risk_error": policy.risk_error_capacity if policy is not None else 60,
        "cold_start_drift": (
            policy.cold_start_drift_capacity if policy is not None else 20
        ),
        "stratified_random": (
            policy.stratified_random_capacity if policy is not None else 15
        ),
        "golden_sentinel": (
            policy.golden_sentinel_capacity if policy is not None else 5
        ),
    }
    batch = db.scalar(
        select(HumanReviewDailyBatch).where(
            HumanReviewDailyBatch.business_date == business_date
        )
    )
    if batch is None:
        bucket_counts = {key: 0 for key in BUCKET_LABELS}
        items: list[HumanReviewDailyBatchItem] = []
        overflow_counts = {"ordinary": 0, "severe": 0}
        batch_payload = None
    else:
        bucket_counts = {
            key: int(_json_object(batch.bucket_counts_json).get(key) or 0)
            for key in BUCKET_LABELS
        }
        items = list(
            db.scalars(
                select(HumanReviewDailyBatchItem)
                .where(HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.batch_id == batch.id)
                .order_by(
                    HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.selection_state,
                    HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.bucket,
                    HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.stable_rank,
                )
            ).all()
        )
        overflow_counts = {
            severity: int(
                db.scalar(
                    select(func.count(HumanReviewOverflowFact.id)).where(
                        HumanReviewOverflowFact.batch_id == batch.id,
                        HumanReviewOverflowFact.severity == severity,
                    )
                )
                or 0
            )
            for severity in ("ordinary", "severe")
        }
        batch_payload = {
            "id": batch.id,
            "status": batch.status,
            "capacity": batch.capacity,
            "selected_count": batch.selected_count,
            "overflow_count": batch.overflow_count,
            "selection_hash": batch.selection_hash,
            "category_coverage": _json_object(batch.category_coverage_json),
            "created_at": batch.created_at,
            "finalized_at": batch.finalized_at,
        }
    pending = sum(item.selection_state == "selected_pending" for item in items)
    reviewed = sum(item.selection_state == "selected_reviewed" for item in items)
    oldest_pending = min(
        (_aware(item.created_at) for item in items if item.selection_state == "selected_pending"),
        default=None,
    )
    pauses = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(ConnectorPauseState)
            .where(
                ConnectorPauseState.reason_code == "human_review_severe_overflow",
                ConnectorPauseState.state == "paused",
            )
            .order_by(ConnectorPauseState.category_key, ConnectorPauseState.mechanism_key)
        ).all()
    )
    return {
        "business_date": business_date.isoformat(),
        "timezone": policy.timezone_name if policy is not None else "Asia/Shanghai",
        "capacity": capacity,
        "reviewer_count": policy.reviewer_count if policy is not None else 1,
        "per_reviewer_capacity": (
            policy.per_reviewer_capacity if policy is not None else 100
        ),
        "batch": batch_payload,
        "buckets": [
            {
                "key": key,
                "label": BUCKET_LABELS[key],
                "capacity": capacities[key],
                "selected": bucket_counts[key],
            }
            for key in BUCKET_LABELS
        ],
        "progress": {"pending": pending, "reviewed": reviewed},
        "overflow": overflow_counts,
        "oldest_wait_seconds": (
            max(0, int((_aware(current) - oldest_pending).total_seconds()))
            if oldest_pending is not None
            else 0
        ),
        "not_selected_label": "未抽查",
        "items": [
            {
                "id": item.id,
                "source_result_key": item.source_result_key,
                "evaluation_id": _evaluation_id_from_key(item.source_result_key),
                "source_run_id": item.source_run_id,
                "category_key": item.category_key,
                "mechanism_version_id": item.mechanism_version_id,
                "bucket": item.bucket,
                "bucket_label": BUCKET_LABELS[item.bucket],
                "stable_rank": item.stable_rank,
                "reason_codes": _json_array(item.reason_codes_json),
                "selection_state": item.selection_state,
                "state_label": STATE_LABELS[item.selection_state],
                "created_at": item.created_at,
                "reviewed_at": item.reviewed_at,
            }
            for item in items
        ],
        "pauses": [_pause_payload(row) for row in pauses],
    }


def build_daily_review_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
    *,
    now: Callable[[], datetime] | None = None,
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/review-capacity", tags=["review-capacity"])
    clock = now or (lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))

    @router.get("/today")
    def today(
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        current = clock()
        return _payload(db, business_date_at(current), current=current)

    @router.post("/{business_date}/generate")
    def generate(
        business_date: date,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not has_permission(user, "reviews:write"):
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=403,
                detail={
                    "code": "review_capacity_write_forbidden",
                    "message": "当前账号不能生成人工抽查批次",
                },
            )
        try:
            build_daily_review_batch(db, business_date, _actor(user))
            db.commit()
        except ValueError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=422,
                detail={"code": "review_capacity_request_invalid", "message": str(exc)},
            ) from exc
        return _payload(db, business_date, current=clock())

    @router.post("/pauses/{pause_id}/resume")
    def resume(
        pause_id: int,
        payload: ReviewCapacityResumeRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not has_permission(user, "review_capacity:manage"):
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=403,
                detail={
                    "code": "review_capacity_manage_forbidden",
                    "message": "只有系统管理员可以恢复被容量保护暂停的增量评测",
                },
            )
        try:
            row = resume_incremental_after_review_overflow(
                db,
                pause_id,
                _actor(user),
                payload.reason,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
        except ValueError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=422,
                detail={"code": "review_capacity_resume_invalid", "message": str(exc)},
            ) from exc
        return _pause_payload(row)

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/daily_review_quota.py =====
"""Transactional materialization of the Asia/Shanghai daily review batch."""

from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document

import hashlib
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from typing import Iterable
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from sqlalchemy import func, select, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .daily_review_selector import (
    ReviewCandidate,
    ReviewQuotaPolicy,
    select_daily_review_candidates,
)
from .models import (
    ConnectorPauseState,
    EvaluationResult,
    HumanReview,
    HumanReviewDailyBatch,
    HumanReviewDailyBatchItem,
    HumanReviewOverflowFact,
    HumanReviewQuotaPolicy,
    ProductionRun,
    RuntimeAuditEvent,
    SampleSet,
    SampleSetItem,
)
from .review_sampling import build_review_sampling, daily_review_bucket


DEFAULT_TIMEZONE = "Asia/Shanghai"
INCREMENTAL_ALL_TARGETS = "*"
SEVERE_OVERFLOW_REASON = "human_review_severe_overflow"
_REDLINE_REASON_CODES = frozenset(
    {
        "redline",
        "redline_hit",
        "hard_redline",
        "redline_overflow",
    }
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OverflowDecision:
    batch_id: int
    overflow_count: int
    severe_count: int
    paused_scope_ids: tuple[int, ...]


def _canonical_json(value: object) -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        value,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )


def _sha256(value: object) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(_canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def business_date_at(
    now: datetime,
    timezone_name: str = DEFAULT_TIMEZONE,
) -> date:
    aware = now if now.tzinfo is not None else now.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return aware.astimezone(ZoneInfo(timezone_name)).date()


def _policy_payload() -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "timezone_name": DEFAULT_TIMEZONE,
        "reviewer_count": 1,
        "per_reviewer_capacity": 100,
        "total_capacity": 100,
        "risk_error_capacity": 60,
        "cold_start_drift_capacity": 20,
        "stratified_random_capacity": 15,
        "golden_sentinel_capacity": 5,
        "severe_overflow_threshold": 1,
        "severe_overflow_rules": {"redline_overflow": True},
    }


def require_active_review_policy(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
) -> HumanReviewQuotaPolicy:
    dialect = db.get_bind().dialect.name
    if dialect in {"mysql", "mariadb", "postgresql"}:
        # Version 84 is an always-present singleton row after this table is
        # available.  Locking it closes the first-policy creation race without
        # introducing a separate mutable lock table.
        db.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT version FROM schema_migrations "
                "WHERE version = 84 FOR UPDATE"
            )
        ).scalar_one()
    policy = db.scalar(
        select(HumanReviewQuotaPolicy)
        .where(HumanReviewQuotaPolicy.active_slot == "global")
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if policy is not None:
        return policy
    version = int(
        db.scalar(select(func.max(HumanReviewQuotaPolicy.version))) or 0
    ) + 1
    payload = _policy_payload()
    policy = HumanReviewQuotaPolicy(
        version=version,
        timezone_name=str(payload["timezone_name"]),
        reviewer_count=int(payload["reviewer_count"]),
        per_reviewer_capacity=int(payload["per_reviewer_capacity"]),
        total_capacity=int(payload["total_capacity"]),
        risk_error_capacity=int(payload["risk_error_capacity"]),
        cold_start_drift_capacity=int(payload["cold_start_drift_capacity"]),
        stratified_random_capacity=int(payload["stratified_random_capacity"]),
        golden_sentinel_capacity=int(payload["golden_sentinel_capacity"]),
        severe_overflow_threshold=int(payload["severe_overflow_threshold"]),
        severe_overflow_rules_json=_canonical_json(
            payload["severe_overflow_rules"]
        ),
        status="active",
        active_slot="global",
        version_hash=_sha256(payload),
        created_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(policy)
    db.flush()
    return policy


def _quota_policy(row: HumanReviewQuotaPolicy) -> ReviewQuotaPolicy:
    return ReviewQuotaPolicy(
        total_capacity=row.total_capacity,
        risk_error_capacity=row.risk_error_capacity,
        cold_start_drift_capacity=row.cold_start_drift_capacity,
        stratified_random_capacity=row.stratified_random_capacity,
        golden_sentinel_capacity=row.golden_sentinel_capacity,
    )


def _json_object(raw: str) -> dict[str, object]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(raw or "{}")
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return {}
    return value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}


def _json_array(raw: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(raw or "[]")
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return ()
    if not isinstance(value, list):
        return ()
    return tuple(str(item) for item in value if isinstance(item, str) and item)


def _mechanism_key(version_id: int | None) -> str:
    return f"workflow-version:{version_id}" if version_id is not None else ""


def _pause_severe_scope(
    db: Session,
    *,
    batch_id: int,
    category_key: str,
    mechanism_version_id: int | None,
    actor: str,
) -> ConnectorPauseState:
    mechanism_key = _mechanism_key(mechanism_version_id)
    pause = db.scalar(
        select(ConnectorPauseState)
        .where(
            ConnectorPauseState.category_key == category_key,
            ConnectorPauseState.mechanism_key == mechanism_key,
            ConnectorPauseState.target_contract_key == INCREMENTAL_ALL_TARGETS,
        )
        .with_for_update()
    )
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    changed = pause is None or pause.state != "paused"
    if pause is None:
        pause = ConnectorPauseState(
            category_key=category_key,
            mechanism_key=mechanism_key,
            target_contract_key=INCREMENTAL_ALL_TARGETS,
            state="paused",
            reason_code=SEVERE_OVERFLOW_REASON,
            paused_by=actor,
            paused_at=now,
            revision=1,
        )
        db.add(pause)
        db.flush()
    elif changed:
        pause.state = "paused"
        pause.reason_code = SEVERE_OVERFLOW_REASON
        pause.paused_by = actor
        pause.paused_at = now
        pause.revision += 1
        db.flush()
    if changed:
        db.add(
            RuntimeAuditEvent(
                event_key=(
                    f"daily-review-overflow-pause:{batch_id}:{pause.id}:{pause.revision}"
                ),
                entity_type="incremental_review_capacity",
                entity_key=str(pause.id),
                action="incremental_review_overflow_paused",
                actor=actor,
                details_json=_canonical_json(
                    {
                        "batch_id": batch_id,
                        "category_key": category_key,
                        "mechanism_key": mechanism_key,
                        "reason_code": SEVERE_OVERFLOW_REASON,
                        "revision": pause.revision,
                    }
                ),
            )
        )
    return pause


def apply_review_overflow_policy(
    db: Session,
    batch_id: int,
    actor: str,
) -> OverflowDecision:
    """Persist overflow facts and fail closed for severe red-line overflow."""

    normalized_actor = actor.strip()
    if not normalized_actor:
        raise ValueError("操作人不能为空")
    batch = db.get(HumanReviewDailyBatch, batch_id)
    if batch is None:
        raise ValueError("每日人审批次不存在")
    existing_facts = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(HumanReviewOverflowFact).where(
                HumanReviewOverflowFact.batch_id == batch_id
            )
        ).all()
    )
    if existing_facts:
        return OverflowDecision(
            batch_id=batch_id,
            overflow_count=len(existing_facts),
            severe_count=sum(item.severity == "severe" for item in existing_facts),
            paused_scope_ids=tuple(
                sorted(
                    {
                        item.pause_state_id
                        for item in existing_facts
                        if item.pause_state_id is not None
                    }
                )
            ),
        )
    policy = db.get(HumanReviewQuotaPolicy, batch.policy_id)
    if policy is None:
        raise ValueError("每日人审批次缺少配额策略")
    rules = _json_object(policy.severe_overflow_rules_json)
    redline_enabled = bool(rules.get("redline_overflow"))
    items = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(HumanReviewDailyBatchItem)
            .where(
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.batch_id == batch_id,
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.selection_state == "not_selected",
            )
            .order_by(HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.id)
        ).all()
    )
    severe_groups: dict[tuple[str, int | None], list[HumanReviewDailyBatchItem]] = defaultdict(list)
    if redline_enabled:
        for item in items:
            if set(_json_array(item.reason_codes_json)) & _REDLINE_REASON_CODES:
                severe_groups[(item.category_key, item.mechanism_version_id)].append(item)
    qualifying_scopes = {
        scope: values
        for scope, values in severe_groups.items()
        if len(values) >= policy.severe_overflow_threshold
    }
    pauses = {
        scope: _pause_severe_scope(
            db,
            batch_id=batch_id,
            category_key=scope[0],
            mechanism_version_id=scope[1],
            actor=normalized_actor,
        )
        for scope in qualifying_scopes
    }
    severe_count = 0
    for item in items:
        scope = (item.category_key, item.mechanism_version_id)
        pause = pauses.get(scope)
        is_severe = (
            pause is not None
            and bool(set(_json_array(item.reason_codes_json)) & _REDLINE_REASON_CODES)
        )
        severe_count += int(is_severe)
        reasons = (*_json_array(item.reason_codes_json), "not_selected_capacity")
        db.add(
            HumanReviewOverflowFact(
                batch_id=batch_id,
                source_result_key=item.source_result_key,
                category_key=item.category_key,
                mechanism_version_id=item.mechanism_version_id,
                severity="severe" if is_severe else "ordinary",
                reason_codes_json=_canonical_json(reasons),
                pause_state_id=pause.id if is_severe else None,
            )
        )
    db.flush()
    return OverflowDecision(
        batch_id=batch_id,
        overflow_count=len(items),
        severe_count=severe_count,
        paused_scope_ids=tuple(sorted(pause.id for pause in pauses.values())),
    )


def resume_incremental_after_review_overflow(
    db: Session,
    pause_id: int,
    actor: str,
    reason: str,
) -> ConnectorPauseState:
    normalized_actor = actor.strip()
    normalized_reason = reason.strip()
    if not normalized_actor:
        raise ValueError("操作人不能为空")
    if not normalized_reason:
        raise ValueError("恢复原因不能为空")
    pause = db.scalar(
        select(ConnectorPauseState)
        .where(ConnectorPauseState.id == pause_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if pause is None:
        raise ValueError("增量评测暂停记录不存在")
    if (
        pause.reason_code != SEVERE_OVERFLOW_REASON
        or pause.target_contract_key != INCREMENTAL_ALL_TARGETS
    ):
        raise ValueError("该暂停记录不属于人审容量保护")
    if pause.state == "active":
        return pause
    pause.state = "active"
    pause.resumed_by = normalized_actor
    pause.resume_reason = normalized_reason
    pause.resumed_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    pause.revision += 1
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        RuntimeAuditEvent(
            event_key=f"daily-review-overflow-resume:{pause.id}:{pause.revision}",
            entity_type="incremental_review_capacity",
            entity_key=str(pause.id),
            action="incremental_review_overflow_resumed",
            actor=normalized_actor,
            details_json=_canonical_json(
                {
                    "category_key": pause.category_key,
                    "mechanism_key": pause.mechanism_key,
                    "reason": normalized_reason,
                    "revision": pause.revision,
                }
            ),
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return pause


def collect_incremental_review_candidates(
    db: Session,
    business_date: date,
) -> tuple[ReviewCandidate, ...]:
    golden_asset_ids = set(
        db.scalars(
            select(SampleSetItem.asset_id)
            .join(SampleSet, SampleSet.id == SampleSetItem.sample_set_id)
            .where(SampleSet.kind == "golden", SampleSet.status == "locked")
        ).all()
    )
    results = db.scalars(
        select(EvaluationResult)
        .where(EvaluationResult.production_run_id.is_not(None))
        .order_by(EvaluationResult.created_at, EvaluationResult.id)
    ).all()
    mechanism_counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    candidates: list[ReviewCandidate] = []
    for result in results:
        run = (
            db.get(ProductionRun, result.production_run_id)
            if result.production_run_id is not None
            else None
        )
        if run is None:
            continue
        try:
            snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
        runtime_context = (
            snapshot.get("runtime_context")
            if isinstance(snapshot, dict)
            else None
        )
        if not isinstance(runtime_context, dict) or runtime_context.get("run_mode") != "incremental":
            continue
        first_seen = result.created_at
        if business_date_at(first_seen) != business_date:
            continue
        mechanism_id = result.workflow_version_id or run.workflow_version_id
        mechanism_key = f"workflow-version:{mechanism_id}"
        mechanism_counts[mechanism_key] += 1
        combination_index = mechanism_counts[mechanism_key]
        is_golden = result.asset_id in golden_asset_ids
        decision = build_review_sampling(
            result,
            is_golden=is_golden,
            combination_index=combination_index,
        )
        reasons = decision.get("reasons")
        reason_codes = tuple(
            str(item.get("code"))
            for item in reasons or []
            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("code")
        )
        normalized = _json_object(result.normalized_result_json or "{}")
        result_stratum = str(
            result.level
            or normalized.get("status")
            or ("needs_review" if result.needs_review else "structured")
        )
        candidates.append(
            ReviewCandidate(
                source_result_key=f"evaluation-result:{result.id}",
                run_mode="incremental",
                category_key=str(run.category_key or result.job.category_key),
                mechanism_version_key=mechanism_key,
                result_stratum=result_stratum,
                business_content_key=f"asset:{result.asset_id}",
                preferred_bucket=daily_review_bucket(
                    decision,
                    is_golden=is_golden,
                    cold_start_or_drift=combination_index <= 5,
                ),
                risk_severity=int(decision.get("priority") or 0),
                confidence=result.confidence,
                impact_score=1.0,
                first_seen_at=(
                    first_seen
                    if first_seen.tzinfo is not None
                    else first_seen.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                ),
                reason_codes=reason_codes,
                source_run_id=run.id,
                mechanism_version_id=mechanism_id,
                already_reviewed=decision.get("tier") == "reviewed",
            )
        )
    return tuple(candidates)


def build_daily_review_batch(
    db: Session,
    business_date: date,
    actor: str,
    *,
    candidates: Iterable[ReviewCandidate] | None = None,
) -> HumanReviewDailyBatch:
    # The active policy row is the cross-dialect singleton lock for batch
    # materialization.  Recheck the business date only after holding it so
    # concurrent MySQL/PostgreSQL builders cannot all observe an empty date.
    policy = require_active_review_policy(db, actor=actor)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(HumanReviewDailyBatch)
        .where(HumanReviewDailyBatch.business_date == business_date)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    frozen_candidates = tuple(
        candidates
        if candidates is not None
        else collect_incremental_review_candidates(db, business_date)
    )
    selection = select_daily_review_candidates(
        _quota_policy(policy),
        business_date,
        frozen_candidates,
    )
    category_coverage: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    for item in selection.selected:
        category_coverage[item.candidate.category_key] += 1
    batch = HumanReviewDailyBatch(
        business_date=business_date,
        policy_id=policy.id,
        candidate_set_hash=selection.candidate_set_hash,
        selection_hash=selection.selection_hash,
        capacity=policy.total_capacity,
        selected_count=len(selection.selected),
        overflow_count=len(selection.unselected),
        bucket_counts_json=_canonical_json(selection.bucket_counts),
        category_coverage_json=_canonical_json(dict(category_coverage)),
        status="finalized",
        created_by=actor,
        finalized_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )
    db.add(batch)
    db.flush()
    for selected, state in (
        (selection.selected, "selected_pending"),
        (selection.unselected, "not_selected"),
    ):
        for item in selected:
            candidate = item.candidate
            db.add(
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem(
                    batch_id=batch.id,
                    business_date=business_date,
                    source_result_key=candidate.source_result_key,
                    source_run_id=candidate.source_run_id,
                    category_key=candidate.category_key,
                    mechanism_version_id=candidate.mechanism_version_id,
                    bucket=item.bucket,
                    stable_rank=item.stable_rank,
                    reason_codes_json=_canonical_json(candidate.reason_codes),
                    selection_state=state,
                )
            )
    db.flush()
    apply_review_overflow_policy(db, batch.id, actor)
    return batch


def get_or_create_today_review_batch(
    db: Session,
    now: datetime,
    actor: str,
) -> HumanReviewDailyBatch:
    policy = db.scalar(
        select(HumanReviewQuotaPolicy).where(
            HumanReviewQuotaPolicy.active_slot == "global"
        )
    )
    timezone_name = policy.timezone_name if policy is not None else DEFAULT_TIMEZONE
    return build_daily_review_batch(
        db,
        business_date=business_date_at(now, timezone_name),
        actor=actor,
    )


def mark_daily_review_completed(
    db: Session,
    *,
    evaluation_id: int,
    review_id: int,
    reviewed_at: datetime | None = None,
) -> tuple[HumanReviewDailyBatchItem, ...]:
    """Link a finalized human review to every still-pending daily queue fact.

    A review may finish shortly after the Shanghai business-day boundary.  If
    the same result was already frozen into the next day's batch, both pending
    queue facts must close so an operator is never asked to review it twice.
    Already-closed items remain immutable and keep their original review link.
    """

    review = db.get(HumanReview, review_id)
    if review is None:
        raise ValueError("人工审核事实不存在")
    if review.evaluation_id != evaluation_id:
        raise ValueError("人工审核事实与评测结果不匹配")
    source_result_key = f"evaluation-result:{evaluation_id}"
    items = tuple(
        db.scalars(
            select(HumanReviewDailyBatchItem)
            .where(
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.source_result_key == source_result_key,
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.selection_state == "selected_pending",
            )
            .order_by(HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.business_date, HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.id)
            .with_for_update()
        ).all()
    )
    completed_at = reviewed_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    for item in items:
        item.selection_state = "selected_reviewed"
        item.review_id = review_id
        item.reviewed_at = completed_at
    if items:
        db.flush()
    return items

===== FILE backend/app/daily_review_selector.py =====
"""Deterministic daily human-review capacity selection.

The selector is pure: it does not read the database, create review tasks, or
interpret an unselected result as approval. Persistence is handled by the
daily-review quota service after the candidate set and hashes are frozen.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from collections import defaultdict, deque
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from typing import Iterable


BUCKET_ORDER = (
    "risk_error",
    "cold_start_drift",
    "stratified_random",
    "golden_sentinel",
)


def _canonical_json(value: object) -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        value,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )


def _sha256(value: object) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(_canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReviewQuotaPolicy:
    total_capacity: int
    risk_error_capacity: int
    cold_start_drift_capacity: int
    stratified_random_capacity: int
    golden_sentinel_capacity: int

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        capacities = (
            self.risk_error_capacity,
            self.cold_start_drift_capacity,
            self.stratified_random_capacity,
            self.golden_sentinel_capacity,
        )
        if self.total_capacity < 1 or any(value < 0 for value in capacities):
            raise ValueError("人审容量必须是非负整数且总容量至少为 1")
        if sum(capacities) != self.total_capacity:
            raise ValueError("四个人审桶容量之和必须等于每日总容量")

    def bucket_capacity(self, bucket: str) -> int:
        return {
            "risk_error": self.risk_error_capacity,
            "cold_start_drift": self.cold_start_drift_capacity,
            "stratified_random": self.stratified_random_capacity,
            "golden_sentinel": self.golden_sentinel_capacity,
        }[bucket]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReviewCandidate:
    source_result_key: str
    run_mode: str
    category_key: str
    mechanism_version_key: str
    result_stratum: str
    business_content_key: str
    preferred_bucket: str
    risk_severity: int
    confidence: float | None
    impact_score: float
    first_seen_at: datetime
    reason_codes: tuple[str, ...]
    source_run_id: int | None = None
    mechanism_version_id: int | None = None
    already_reviewed: bool = False

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for value, label in (
            (self.source_result_key, "结果标识"),
            (self.category_key, "类目"),
            (self.mechanism_version_key, "机制版本"),
            (self.result_stratum, "结果分层"),
