            (self.business_content_key, "业务内容标识"),
        ):
            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(f"{label}不能为空")
        if self.preferred_bucket not in BUCKET_ORDER:
            raise ValueError("候选所属人审桶不受支持")
        if self.first_seen_at.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError("候选首次出现时间必须带时区")

    def canonical_payload(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        return {
            "source_result_key": self.source_result_key,
            "run_mode": self.run_mode,
            "category_key": self.category_key,
            "mechanism_version_key": self.mechanism_version_key,
            "result_stratum": self.result_stratum,
            "business_content_key": self.business_content_key,
            "preferred_bucket": self.preferred_bucket,
            "risk_severity": self.risk_severity,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "impact_score": self.impact_score,
            "first_seen_at": self.first_seen_at.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            "reason_codes": list(self.reason_codes),
            "source_run_id": self.source_run_id,
            "mechanism_version_id": self.mechanism_version_id,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReviewSelectionItem:
    candidate: ReviewCandidate
    bucket: str
    stable_rank: int
    reason_code: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReviewSelection:
    business_date: date
    selected: tuple[ReviewSelectionItem, ...]
    unselected: tuple[ReviewSelectionItem, ...]
    bucket_counts: dict[str, int]
    candidate_set_hash: str
    selection_hash: str
    duplicate_count: int
    excluded_count: int


def _priority_key(candidate: ReviewCandidate) -> tuple[object, ...]:
    confidence = candidate.confidence
    normalized_confidence = 1.0 if confidence is None else confidence
    return (
        -candidate.risk_severity,
        normalized_confidence,
        -candidate.impact_score,
        candidate.first_seen_at.astimezone(timezone.utc),
        _sha256(candidate.canonical_payload()),
    )


def _random_key(business_date: date, candidate: ReviewCandidate) -> str:
    return _sha256(
        {
            "business_date": business_date.isoformat(),
            "category_key": candidate.category_key,
            "mechanism_version_key": candidate.mechanism_version_key,
            "result_stratum": candidate.result_stratum,
            "business_content_key": candidate.business_content_key,
            "source_result_key": candidate.source_result_key,
        }
    )


def _stratified_order(
    business_date: date,
    candidates: Iterable[ReviewCandidate],
) -> list[ReviewCandidate]:
    groups: dict[tuple[str, str, str], deque[ReviewCandidate]] = defaultdict(deque)
    for candidate in candidates:
        stratum = (
            candidate.category_key,
            candidate.mechanism_version_key,
            candidate.result_stratum,
        )
        groups[stratum].append(candidate)
    for stratum, values in list(groups.items()):
        groups[stratum] = deque(
            sorted(values, key=lambda item: _random_key(business_date, item))
        )
    ordered_strata = sorted(
        groups,
        key=lambda value: _sha256(
            {
                "business_date": business_date.isoformat(),
                "stratum": list(value),
            }
        ),
    )
    ordered: list[ReviewCandidate] = []
    while any(groups.values()):
        for stratum in ordered_strata:
            if groups[stratum]:
                ordered.append(groups[stratum].popleft())
    return ordered


def _deduplicate(
    candidates: Iterable[ReviewCandidate],
) -> tuple[list[ReviewCandidate], int, int]:
    eligible: dict[str, list[ReviewCandidate]] = defaultdict(list)
    excluded_count = 0
    for candidate in candidates:
        if candidate.run_mode != "incremental" or candidate.already_reviewed:
            excluded_count += 1
            continue
        eligible[candidate.source_result_key].append(candidate)
    duplicate_count = sum(max(0, len(values) - 1) for values in eligible.values())
    unique = [
        min(values, key=lambda item: _canonical_json(item.canonical_payload()))
        for _, values in sorted(eligible.items())
    ]
    return unique, duplicate_count, excluded_count


def select_daily_review_candidates(
    policy: ReviewQuotaPolicy,
    business_date: date,
    candidates: Iterable[ReviewCandidate],
) -> ReviewSelection:
    unique, duplicate_count, excluded_count = _deduplicate(candidates)
    by_bucket = {
        bucket: [
            candidate
            for candidate in unique
            if candidate.preferred_bucket == bucket
        ]
        for bucket in BUCKET_ORDER
    }
    for bucket in ("risk_error", "cold_start_drift", "golden_sentinel"):
        by_bucket[bucket].sort(key=_priority_key)
    by_bucket["stratified_random"] = _stratified_order(
        business_date,
        by_bucket["stratified_random"],
    )

    selected: list[ReviewSelectionItem] = []
    unselected: list[ReviewSelectionItem] = []
    bucket_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    carry = 0
    for bucket in BUCKET_ORDER:
        available = policy.bucket_capacity(bucket) + carry
        values = by_bucket[bucket]
        chosen = values[:available]
        deferred = values[available:]
        bucket_counts[bucket] = len(chosen)
        selected.extend(
            ReviewSelectionItem(
                candidate=candidate,
                bucket=bucket,
                stable_rank=index,
                reason_code=bucket,
            )
            for index, candidate in enumerate(chosen, start=1)
        )
        unselected.extend(
            ReviewSelectionItem(
                candidate=candidate,
                bucket=bucket,
                stable_rank=index,
                reason_code="not_selected_capacity",
            )
            for index, candidate in enumerate(deferred, start=available + 1)
        )
        carry = max(0, available - len(chosen))

    candidate_set_hash = _sha256(
        [
            candidate.canonical_payload()
            for candidate in sorted(unique, key=lambda item: item.source_result_key)
        ]
    )
    selection_payload = [
        {
            "source_result_key": item.candidate.source_result_key,
            "bucket": item.bucket,
            "stable_rank": item.stable_rank,
        }
        for item in selected
    ]
    return ReviewSelection(
        business_date=business_date,
        selected=tuple(selected),
        unselected=tuple(unselected),
        bucket_counts=bucket_counts,
        candidate_set_hash=candidate_set_hash,
        selection_hash=_sha256(selection_payload),
        duplicate_count=duplicate_count,
        excluded_count=excluded_count,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/dashboard_summary.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import timezone

from sqlalchemy import and_, case, func, or_, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import EvaluationResult, HumanReview, ReviewPanel, ProductionRun, EvaluationJob


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DashboardEvaluationSummary:
    result_count: int
    needs_review: int
    levels: dict[str, int]


def _review_sort_key(review: object) -> tuple[float, int]:
    created_at = getattr(review, "created_at", None)
    if created_at is None:
        timestamp = 0.0
    else:
        if created_at.tzinfo is None:
            created_at = created_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        timestamp = created_at.timestamp()
    return timestamp, int(getattr(review, "id", 0) or 0)


def dashboard_final_level(result: EvaluationResult) -> str | None:
    ordered_reviews = sorted(result.reviews, key=_review_sort_key)
    panel = result.review_panel
    current_round_reviews = (
        [
            review
            for review in ordered_reviews
            if review.panel_id == panel.id
            and review.review_round == panel.review_round
        ]
        if panel is not None
        else []
    )
    visible_reviews = (
        ordered_reviews
        if panel is None or panel.status == "completed"
        else [
            review
            for review in ordered_reviews
            if not (
                review.panel_id == panel.id
                and review.review_round == panel.review_round
            )
        ]
    )
    review_for_display = (
        current_round_reviews[-1]
        if current_round_reviews and panel is not None and panel.status == "completed"
        else visible_reviews[-1] if visible_reviews else None
    )
    if review_for_display and review_for_display.decision == "corrected":
        return review_for_display.corrected_level or result.level
    return result.level


def dashboard_evaluation_summary(db: Session) -> DashboardEvaluationSummary:
    # Rank narrow projections, not multi-KB prompts / raw responses / JSON.
    ranked = select(
        EvaluationResult.id.label("result_id"), EvaluationResult.level,
        EvaluationResult.needs_review,
        func.row_number().over(
            partition_by=EvaluationResult.asset_id,
            order_by=(EvaluationResult.created_at.desc(), EvaluationResult.id.desc()),
        ).label("result_rank"),
    ).subquery()
    latest = select(ranked).where(ranked.c.result_rank == 1).subquery()
    current_round = and_(HumanReview.panel_id == ReviewPanel.id,
                         HumanReview.review_round == ReviewPanel.review_round)
    current_completed = and_(ReviewPanel.status == "completed", current_round)
    visible = or_(ReviewPanel.id.is_(None), ReviewPanel.status == "completed",
                  ~func.coalesce(current_round, False))
    reviews = select(
        HumanReview.evaluation_id, HumanReview.decision, HumanReview.corrected_level,
        func.row_number().over(
            partition_by=HumanReview.evaluation_id,
            order_by=(case((current_completed, 1), else_=0).desc(),
                      HumanReview.created_at.desc(), HumanReview.id.desc()),
        ).label("review_rank"),
    ).join(latest, latest.c.result_id == HumanReview.evaluation_id).outerjoin(
        ReviewPanel, ReviewPanel.evaluation_id == HumanReview.evaluation_id
    ).where(visible).subquery()
    final_level = case(
        (reviews.c.decision == "corrected", func.coalesce(func.nullif(reviews.c.corrected_level, ""), latest.c.level)),
        else_=latest.c.level,
    ).label("final_level")
    rows = db.execute(select(final_level, func.count(),
        func.sum(case((latest.c.needs_review.is_(True), 1), else_=0)))
        .select_from(latest).outerjoin(reviews, and_(reviews.c.evaluation_id == latest.c.result_id,
                                                   reviews.c.review_rank == 1))
        .group_by(final_level)).all()
    return DashboardEvaluationSummary(
        result_count=sum(int(row[1]) for row in rows),
        needs_review=sum(int(row[2] or 0) for row in rows),
        levels={level: int(count) for level, count, _ in rows if level},
    )


def runtime_run_summary(db: Session) -> dict:
    """One row per native material run, separated from old EvaluationJob data."""
    groups: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {"incremental": {}, "stock_regression": {}, "other": {}}
    for mode, status, count in db.execute(select(ProductionRun.run_mode, ProductionRun.status,
            func.count()).group_by(ProductionRun.run_mode, ProductionRun.status)):
        bucket = groups[mode if mode in groups else "other"]
        bucket[status] = bucket.get(status, 0) + int(count)
    totals: dict[str, int] = {}
    for bucket in groups.values():
        for status, count in bucket.items():
            totals[status] = totals.get(status, 0) + count
    legacy = dict(db.execute(select(EvaluationJob.status, func.count()).where(
        EvaluationJob.production_run_id.is_(None)).group_by(EvaluationJob.status)).all())
    return {"scope": "native_material_runs", "statuses": totals, "by_mode": groups,
            "queued": sum(totals.get(key, 0) for key in ("planned", "queued", "retryable")),
            "running": totals.get("running", 0), "waiting_external": totals.get("waiting_external", 0),
            "paused": totals.get("paused", 0), "failed": totals.get("failed", 0),
            "incremental_failed": groups["incremental"].get("failed", 0),
            "legacy_jobs": legacy}

===== FILE backend/app/database.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
from contextlib import contextmanager
from typing import Any, Generator

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, inspect
from sqlalchemy.engine import make_url
from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Session, sessionmaker

from .config import get_settings
from .database_capabilities import DatabaseCapabilities, DatabaseDialectError


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


settings = get_settings()


def engine_options(
    database_url: str,
    *,
    pool_size: int = 2,
    max_overflow: int = 0,
) -> dict[str, object]:
    dialect = make_url(database_url).get_backend_name()
    DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(dialect)
    if dialect == "sqlite":
        return {
            "connect_args": {"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 10},
            "pool_pre_ping": True,
        }
    if dialect == "postgresql":
        return {
            "pool_pre_ping": True,
            "pool_recycle": 1800,
            "pool_size": pool_size,
            "max_overflow": max_overflow,
        }
    if dialect == "mysql":
        return {
            "pool_pre_ping": True,
            "pool_recycle": 900,
            "pool_reset_on_return": "rollback",
            "pool_size": pool_size,
            "max_overflow": max_overflow,
        }
    raise DatabaseDialectError(f"不支持的数据库方言：{dialect}")


engine = create_engine(
    settings.database_url,
    **engine_options(
        settings.database_url,
        pool_size=settings.database_pool_size,
        max_overflow=settings.database_max_overflow,
    ),
)
_ENGINE_DIALECT = engine.dialect.name


def _busy_timeout_ms() -> int:
    """SQLite 锁等待毫秒数，可用 SQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS 覆盖。"""
    raw = os.getenv("SQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS")
    if raw and raw.strip():
        try:
            value = int(raw.strip())
        except ValueError:
            return 30_000
        if value > 0:
            return value
    return 30_000


def _sqlite_version_tuple() -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    parts = sqlite3.sqlite_version.split(".")[:3]
    return tuple(int(part) for part in parts)  # type: ignore[return-value]


@event.listens_for(engine, "connect")
def _configure_sqlite(
    dbapi_connection: sqlite3.Connection,
    _connection_record: object,
) -> None:
    if not isinstance(dbapi_connection, sqlite3.Connection):
        return
    cursor = dbapi_connection.cursor()
    cursor.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    # 锁等待上限。测试服跑 250 条真实回归时实测锁等待达 38-48 秒，10 秒明显不
    # 够——尤其在 SQLite < 3.51.3 只能用 delete 日志模式（全库写锁串行）时。
    cursor.execute(f"PRAGMA busy_timeout={_busy_timeout_ms()}")
    cursor.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL")
    # 2026 年 SQLite 官方修复了 WAL reset 竞态。旧运行时使用默认日志模式更稳妥。
    if _sqlite_version_tuple() >= (3, 51, 3):
        cursor.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
    cursor.close()


def configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(dbapi_connection: Any) -> None:
    cursor = dbapi_connection.cursor()
    try:
        cursor.execute("SET SESSION time_zone = '+00:00'")
        cursor.execute(
            "SET SESSION TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL READ COMMITTED"
        )
        cursor.execute("SET NAMES utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_as_cs")
        cursor.execute(
            "SET SESSION sql_mode = CONCAT_WS(',', @@SESSION.sql_mode, "
            "'STRICT_TRANS_TABLES', 'ERROR_FOR_DIVISION_BY_ZERO', "
            "'NO_ENGINE_SUBSTITUTION')"
        )
    finally:
        cursor.close()


@event.listens_for(engine, "connect")
def _configure_mysql(
    dbapi_connection: Any,
    _connection_record: object,
) -> None:
    if _ENGINE_DIALECT != "mysql":
        return
    configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(dbapi_connection)


SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False, autoflush=False)


def dispose_inherited_database_pool() -> None:
    """Replace a connection pool inherited from a launcher process."""
    engine.dispose(close=False)


_TRANSIENT_SQLITE_CLAIM_MARKERS = (
    "database is locked",
    "database is busy",
    "cannot start a transaction within a transaction",
)


def reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db: Session) -> None:
    """Reserve the SQLite write lock unless this driver is already in a transaction."""
    if db.get_bind().dialect.name != "sqlite":
        return
    DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect("sqlite").reserve_claim_transaction(db)


def is_transient_sqlite_claim_error(exc: BaseException) -> bool:
    """Recognize only SQLite failures that are safe at a short claim boundary."""
    current: BaseException | None = exc
    while current is not None:
        if isinstance(current, sqlite3.OperationalError) and any(
            marker in str(current).lower()
            for marker in _TRANSIENT_SQLITE_CLAIM_MARKERS
        ):
            return True
        if isinstance(current, OperationalError):
            current = current.orig
            continue
        current = current.__cause__ or current.__context__
    return False


def init_database() -> None:
    from . import models  # noqa: F401
    from .migrations import (
        assert_mysql_schema_version,
        create_mysql_baseline,
        create_postgres_baseline,
        run_migrations,
        upgrade_mysql_schema,
    )
    from .migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS

    if engine.dialect.name == "postgresql":
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            create_postgres_baseline(connection)
        return
    if engine.dialect.name == "mysql":
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            if inspect(connection).get_table_names():
                upgrade_mysql_schema(
                    connection,
                    expected=max(item.version for item in MIGRATIONS),
                )
                assert_mysql_schema_version(
                    connection,
                    expected=max(item.version for item in MIGRATIONS),
                )
            else:
                create_mysql_baseline(connection)
        return
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)


def get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()


@contextmanager
def session_scope() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
        db.commit()
    except Exception:
        db.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        db.close()

===== FILE backend/app/database_capabilities.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection
from sqlalchemy.sql import Select


class DatabaseDialectError(ValueError):
    """Raised when code would otherwise silently use the wrong SQL dialect."""


MYSQL_REQUIRED_SERIES = (8, 4)
MYSQL_REQUIRED_COLLATION = "utf8mb4_0900_as_cs"
MYSQL_REQUIRED_SQL_MODES = frozenset(
    {
        "STRICT_TRANS_TABLES",
        "ERROR_FOR_DIVISION_BY_ZERO",
        "NO_ENGINE_SUBSTITUTION",
    }
)
MYSQL_TRANSIENT_TRANSACTION_ERROR_CODES = frozenset({1205, 1213})
POSTGRES_TRANSIENT_TRANSACTION_SQLSTATES = frozenset({"40001", "40P01"})


def parse_mysql_version(value: str) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    normalized = value.strip()
    match = re.match(r"^(\d+)\.(\d+)\.(\d+)", normalized)
    if match is None:
        raise DatabaseDialectError(
            "平台主库必须使用 Oracle MySQL 8.4.x；服务端版本无法识别"
        )
    parsed = tuple(int(part) for part in match.groups())
    if parsed[:2] != MYSQL_REQUIRED_SERIES or "mariadb" in normalized.lower():
        raise DatabaseDialectError(
            f"平台主库必须使用 Oracle MySQL 8.4.x；实际版本为 {normalized}"
        )
    return parsed  # type: ignore[return-value]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DatabaseCapabilities:
    dialect: str
    serializes_claims: bool

    @classmethod
    def for_dialect(cls, name: str) -> "DatabaseCapabilities":
        normalized = name.strip().lower()
        if normalized == "sqlite":
            return cls(dialect="sqlite", serializes_claims=True)
        if normalized == "postgresql":
            return cls(dialect="postgresql", serializes_claims=False)
        if normalized == "mysql":
            return cls(dialect="mysql", serializes_claims=False)
        raise DatabaseDialectError(f"不支持的数据库方言：{name or '空值'}")

    def claim_lock_statement(self, selectable: Select[Any]) -> Select[Any]:
        if self.dialect in {"mysql", "postgresql"}:
            return selectable.with_for_update(skip_locked=True)
        return selectable

    def reserve_claim_transaction(self, db: Any) -> None:
        if not self.serializes_claims:
            return
        connection = db.connection()
        driver_connection = connection.connection.driver_connection
        if driver_connection.in_transaction:
            return
        connection.exec_driver_sql("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")


def _exception_chain(error: BaseException):
    pending: list[BaseException] = [error]
    visited: set[int] = set()
    while pending:
        current = pending.pop()
        identity = id(current)
        if identity in visited:
            continue
        visited.add(identity)
        yield current
        for linked in (
            getattr(current, "orig", None),
            current.__cause__,
            current.__context__,
        ):
            if isinstance(linked, BaseException):
                pending.append(linked)


def is_transient_database_transaction_error(error: BaseException) -> bool:
    """Return true only for transaction conflicts safe to retry in full."""

    for current in _exception_chain(error):
        arguments = getattr(current, "args", ())
        if (
            arguments
            and isinstance(arguments[0], int)
            and arguments[0] in MYSQL_TRANSIENT_TRANSACTION_ERROR_CODES
        ):
            return True
        sqlstate = (
            getattr(current, "sqlstate", None)
            or getattr(current, "pgcode", None)
        )
        if sqlstate in POSTGRES_TRANSIENT_TRANSACTION_SQLSTATES:
            return True
    return False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DatabaseHealth:
    dialect: str
    server_version: str
    read_only: bool
    claim_strategy: str


def assert_mysql_runtime_contract(connection: Connection) -> DatabaseHealth:
    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise DatabaseDialectError("MySQL 运行合同只能核验 MySQL 连接")
    # Keep every contract check, but read connection/server facts together.
    # Serving probes run frequently; separate SELECTs added thirteen network
    # round trips per request and amplified latency during module replacement.
    (
        version, version_comment, read_only_value, storage_engine, isolation,
        time_zone, charset_client, charset_connection, charset_results,
        sql_mode, database_name, collation,
    ) = connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SELECT VERSION(), @@version_comment, @@global.read_only, "
        "@@session.default_storage_engine, @@session.transaction_isolation, "
        "@@session.time_zone, @@character_set_client, @@character_set_connection, "
        "@@character_set_results, @@session.sql_mode, DATABASE(), @@collation_database"
    ).one()
    version = str(version)
    normalized_comment = str(version_comment).strip().lower()
    rejected_products = ("mariadb", "percona", "aurora", "tidb", "oceanbase")
    recognized_oracle_distribution = (
        "mysql" in normalized_comment or normalized_comment == "homebrew"
    )
    if (
        not recognized_oracle_distribution
        or any(product in normalized_comment for product in rejected_products)
    ):
        raise DatabaseDialectError(
            "平台主库必须使用 Oracle MySQL 8.4.x；检测到非 Oracle 产品"
        )
    parse_mysql_version(version)
    read_only = str(read_only_value).strip().lower() in {"on", "true", "1"}
    if read_only:
        raise DatabaseDialectError("MySQL 主库处于只读状态")

    if str(storage_engine).strip().lower() != "innodb":
        raise DatabaseDialectError("MySQL 默认存储引擎必须为 InnoDB")

    if str(isolation).strip().upper().replace("_", "-") != "READ-COMMITTED":
        raise DatabaseDialectError("MySQL 会话隔离级别必须为 READ COMMITTED")

    if str(time_zone).strip() != "+00:00":
        raise DatabaseDialectError("MySQL 会话时区必须为 UTC（+00:00）")

    character_sets = {
        str(value).strip().lower()
        for value in (charset_client, charset_connection, charset_results)
    }
    if character_sets != {"utf8mb4"}:
        raise DatabaseDialectError(
            "MySQL 客户端、连接和结果字符集必须为 utf8mb4"
        )

    active_modes = {
        item.strip().upper()
        for item in str(sql_mode).split(",")
        if item.strip()
    }
    if not MYSQL_REQUIRED_SQL_MODES <= active_modes:
        raise DatabaseDialectError("MySQL SQL mode 缺少平台必需的严格模式")

    if database_name is not None and str(database_name).strip():
        if str(collation).strip().lower() != MYSQL_REQUIRED_COLLATION:
            raise DatabaseDialectError(
                "MySQL 目标 schema 必须使用区分大小写的 collation"
            )
        non_innodb_tables = int(
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.TABLES "
                "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() AND TABLE_TYPE = 'BASE TABLE' "
                "AND ENGINE <> 'InnoDB'"
            ).scalar_one()
        )
        if non_innodb_tables:
            raise DatabaseDialectError(
                "MySQL 目标 schema 的业务表必须全部使用 InnoDB"
            )

    return DatabaseHealth(
        dialect="mysql",
        server_version=version,
        read_only=False,
        claim_strategy="for_update_skip_locked",
    )


def database_health(connection: Connection) -> DatabaseHealth:
    dialect = connection.dialect.name
    DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(dialect)
    if dialect == "sqlite":
        version = str(
            connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT sqlite_version()").scalar_one()
        )
        read_only = bool(
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA query_only").scalar_one()
        )
        return DatabaseHealth(
            dialect="sqlite",
            server_version=version,
            read_only=read_only,
            claim_strategy="begin_immediate",
        )
    if dialect == "mysql":
        return assert_mysql_runtime_contract(connection)
    version = str(connection.exec_driver_sql("SHOW server_version").scalar_one())
    read_only_value = str(
        connection.exec_driver_sql("SHOW transaction_read_only").scalar_one()
    )
    return DatabaseHealth(
        dialect="postgresql",
        server_version=version,
        read_only=read_only_value.lower() in {"on", "true", "1"},
        claim_strategy="for_update_skip_locked",
    )

===== FILE backend/app/design_case_contracts.py =====
"""Shared versioned output contracts for the native design-case workflow."""

from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
from typing import Any, Mapping

from .prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract, validate_output_payload


_CATEGORY_ONE = (
    "建筑设计",
    "居住空间",
    "商业空间",
    "办公空间",
    "酒店空间",
    "公共空间",
    "景观设计",
    "规划设计",
    "工业设计",
    "其他",
)


def design_case_text_properties() -> dict[str, Any]:
    string_array = {
        "type": "array",
        "items": {"type": "string"},
        "minItems": 1,
        "maxItems": 5,
    }
    return {
        "score": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100},
        "reason": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 50},
        "seo_title": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 28},
        "category_one": {"type": "string", "enum": list(_CATEGORY_ONE)},
        "category_two": {"type": "string"},
        "style": {"type": "string"},
        "matcraft": deepcopy(string_array),
        "designtactic": deepcopy(string_array),
        "spaceshape": deepcopy(string_array),
        "clientInfo": {"type": "string"},
        "projattr": {"type": "string"},
        "designer": {"type": "string"},
        "region_type": {"type": "string", "enum": ["国内", "国外"]},
        "location_detail": {"type": "string"},
        "scale": {"type": "string"},
        "dtime": {"type": "string"},
        "btime": {"type": "string"},
    }


def design_case_text_output_contract() -> dict[str, Any]:
    properties = design_case_text_properties()
    return {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "design_case_evaluation",
        "description": "案例素材综合严格输出",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": properties,
            "required": list(properties),
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }


def validate_design_case_truth_review(review: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Validate a partial human truth against the exact model field schemas."""

    all_properties = design_case_text_properties()
    selected = {key: deepcopy(all_properties[key]) for key in review}
    contract = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "design_case_truth_review",
        "description": "案例综合输出的逐字段人工真值",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": selected,
            "required": list(selected),
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }
    validate_output_payload(parse_output_contract(contract), dict(review))

===== FILE backend/app/design_case_dimension_import.py =====
"""从 NAS 交付目录解析案例的两维度样本。

运营的交付形状（2026-09-08 起，`案例-普通样本/测试`）：

    01好/ 02中等/ 03中差/ 04极差/ 05过滤/      ← 案例档位，五档
      好_899288/                              ← 目录名 = 案例档位_案例cloud_id
        899288.txt                            ← 案例正文与素材维度统计
        中差_15775794.png                     ← 文件名 = 图片档位_图片cloud_id
        ...

一个目录同时喂两条基准：案例正文与聚合统计给素材维度
（``design_case.material_aggregate.v1``），逐图档位给图片维度
（``design_case.dimension_grade.v1``）。图片档位只有四档——运营的过滤档是案例级的，
单张图片不进过滤。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
import re
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping

from .baseline_truth import (
    DESIGN_CASE_GRADE_TO_LEVEL,
    DESIGN_CASE_IMAGE_LEVELS,
)
# 计数文本只有一套解析口径。素材维度已经在 2000 条真实样本上验证过它，
# 这里再写一份必然分叉——同一份交付会解析出两个不一样的真值。
from .design_case_sample_import import (
    _field_for_label,
    _summary_slot,
    parse_count_text as _parse_count_entries,
)


# 目录前缀里的序号只是排序用，档位以中文名为准。
TIER_DIRECTORIES: dict[str, str] = {
    "01好": "好",
    "02中等": "中等",
    "03中差": "中差",
    "04极差": "极差",
    "05过滤": "过滤",
}
IMAGE_SUFFIXES = (".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".gif")
# 只有表现图会被模型打分；技术图、分析图等没有分数，档位靠人工单独给。
PERFORMANCE_TRAITS = frozenset({"实景照片", "3D数字效果图", "AI图"})
# 运营表格里「其他」和平台枚举的「其它」是同一个值（2026-09-08 定案）。
TRAIT_ALIASES = {"其他": "其它"}
# 上一版提示词没返回类型时占位用的记号，不是真实取值，直接剔除。
DROPPED_TRAITS = frozenset({"未返回trait"})

_CASE_DIR = re.compile(r"^(好|中等|中差|极差|过滤)_(\d+)$")
_IMAGE_NAME = re.compile(r"^(好|中等|中差|极差)_(\d+)\.[A-Za-z0-9]+$")
_FIELD_LINE = re.compile(r"^\s*([^：:]{1,20})[：:](.*)$")


class DesignCaseDimensionError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ParsedImage:
    cloud_id: str
    grade: str
    filename: str

    @property
    def level(self) -> str:
        return DESIGN_CASE_GRADE_TO_LEVEL[self.grade]


@dataclass
class ParsedCase:
    cloud_id: str
    grade: str
    directory: Path
    text_fields: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    images: list[ParsedImage] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def level(self) -> str:
        return DESIGN_CASE_GRADE_TO_LEVEL[self.grade]

    @property
    def case_name(self) -> str:
        return self.text_fields.get("素材标题", "").strip()


def normalize_trait(value: str) -> str | None:
    """把运营写的类型名归一到平台枚举；占位记号返回 None 表示剔除。"""

    text = str(value).strip()
    if not text or text in DROPPED_TRAITS:
        return None
    return TRAIT_ALIASES.get(text, text)


def parse_count_text(text: str) -> dict[str, int]:
    """解析「实景照片有15张,技术图有1张」这类计数文本，合并同名项。

    交付里还有一种写法：``分析图,3D数字效果图有1张`` —— 一张图带两个类型标签。
    既有口径把省略计数的片段当 1 张，拆出「分析图 1 张 + 3D数字效果图 1 张」，
    与平台 ``aggregate_case`` 对多值特征逐个计数的做法一致，所以逐图张数之和
    可以大于图片总数（一张图被两个类型各记一次）。
    """

    return {
        entry["value"]: entry["count"] for entry in _parse_count_entries(text or "")
    }


def _read_text_fields(path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    fields: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    active_key: str | None = None
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as handle:
        for line in handle:
            line = line.rstrip("\r\n")
            matched = _FIELD_LINE.match(line)
            key = matched.group(1).strip() if matched else None
            if key is not None and (
                _field_for_label(key) is not None or _summary_slot(key) is not None
            ):
                # 只把合同登记过的标签当作字段边界。重复字段整块忽略，
                # 防止其续行混入首次字段或前一个字段。
                active_key = key if key not in fields else None
                if active_key is not None:
                    fields[active_key] = [matched.group(2).strip()]
            elif active_key is not None:
                # 创作思想、URL 等含冒号的正文，以及空行和缩进，原样保留。
                fields[active_key].append(line)
    return {key: "\n".join(lines).strip() for key, lines in fields.items()}


def _guard_delivery_path(path: Path, root: Path) -> None:
    """Check directories and text before traversal, as well as image entries."""
    try:
        relative = path.relative_to(root)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
            "design_case_path_escape", "交付路径超出样本根目录"
        ) from exc
    current = root
    for part in (None, *relative.parts):
        if part is not None:
            current = current / part
        if current.is_symlink():
            raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
                "design_case_symlink_rejected", "交付目录和文件不能经过符号链接"
            )
    if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root.resolve()):
        raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
            "design_case_path_escape", "交付路径超出样本根目录"
        )


def parse_case_directory(
    directory: Path, *, grade: str, boundary_root: Path | None = None
) -> ParsedCase:
    boundary_root = boundary_root or directory
    _guard_delivery_path(directory, boundary_root)
    matched = _CASE_DIR.match(directory.name)
    if matched is None:
        raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
            "design_case_dir_name_invalid",
            f"案例目录名必须是「档位_案例编号」：{directory.name}",
        )
    dir_grade, cloud_id = matched.group(1), matched.group(2)
    if dir_grade != grade:
        raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
            "design_case_dir_grade_mismatch",
            f"案例 {cloud_id} 在 {grade} 目录下却写着 {dir_grade}",
        )
    case = ParsedCase(cloud_id=cloud_id, grade=grade, directory=directory)
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for entry in sorted(directory.iterdir(), key=lambda item: item.name):
        _guard_delivery_path(entry, boundary_root)
        if not entry.is_file():
            continue
        if entry.suffix.lower() == ".txt":
            if not case.text_fields:
                case.text_fields = _read_text_fields(entry)
            continue
        if entry.suffix.lower() not in IMAGE_SUFFIXES:
            continue
        image_matched = _IMAGE_NAME.match(entry.name)
        if image_matched is None:
            raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
                "design_case_image_name_invalid",
                f"图片文件名必须是「档位_图片编号」：{entry.name}",
            )
        image_grade, image_cloud_id = image_matched.group(1), image_matched.group(2)
        if DESIGN_CASE_GRADE_TO_LEVEL[image_grade] not in DESIGN_CASE_IMAGE_LEVELS:
            raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
                "design_case_image_level_invalid",
                f"图片 {entry.name} 的档位不在 L1-L4 内",
            )
        if image_cloud_id in seen:
            raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
                "design_case_image_duplicate",
                f"案例 {cloud_id} 内图片编号重复：{image_cloud_id}",
            )
        seen.add(image_cloud_id)
        case.images.append(
            ParsedImage(cloud_id=image_cloud_id, grade=image_grade, filename=entry.name)
        )
    if not case.images:
        raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
            "design_case_no_images",
            f"案例 {cloud_id} 目录里没有可识别的图片",
        )
    if not case.text_fields:
        raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
            "design_case_text_missing",
            f"案例 {cloud_id} 目录里没有正文 txt",
        )
    return case


def parse_sample_root(root: Path) -> list[ParsedCase]:
    """遍历交付根目录，按档位子目录解析出所有案例。"""

    _guard_delivery_path(root, root)
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
            "design_case_sample_root_missing",
            f"样本根目录不存在：{root}",
        )
    cases: list[ParsedCase] = []
    seen_cases: dict[str, Path] = {}
    for tier_dir in sorted(root.iterdir(), key=lambda item: item.name):
        _guard_delivery_path(tier_dir, root)
        if not tier_dir.is_dir():
            continue
        grade = TIER_DIRECTORIES.get(tier_dir.name)
        if grade is None:
            raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
                "design_case_tier_dir_unknown",
                f"未知的档位目录：{tier_dir.name}",
            )
        for case_dir in sorted(tier_dir.iterdir(), key=lambda item: item.name):
            _guard_delivery_path(case_dir, root)
            if not case_dir.is_dir():
                continue
            case = parse_case_directory(case_dir, grade=grade, boundary_root=root)
            # 一张图只会出现在一个案例内（运营 2026-09-08 确认），
            # 案例本身自然也只能出现一次；重复即为交付事故。
            if case.cloud_id in seen_cases:
                raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
                    "design_case_duplicate",
                    f"案例 {case.cloud_id} 在 {seen_cases[case.cloud_id]} 与 {case_dir} 重复出现",
                )
            seen_cases[case.cloud_id] = case_dir
            cases.append(case)
    if not cases:
        raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
            "design_case_sample_empty",
            f"样本根目录下没有解析到任何案例：{root}",
        )
    return cases


def build_dimension_truth(case: ParsedCase) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """产出 ``design_case.dimension_grade.v1`` 的 expected_result。"""

    return {
        "level": case.level,
        "images": [
            {"cloud_id": image.cloud_id, "level": image.level}
            for image in sorted(case.images, key=lambda item: item.cloud_id)
        ],
    }


def build_aggregate_truth(case: ParsedCase) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """产出 ``design_case.material_aggregate.v1`` 的三行原话真值。

    计数交给 baseline_truth 按同一套解析规则反解，这里只负责把运营原话
    原样带过去——两处各解析一次会埋下口径分叉。
    """

    trait_text = case.text_fields.get("所有图片的类型", "")
    kept = {
        normalized: count
        for label, count in parse_count_text(trait_text).items()
        if (normalized := normalize_trait(label)) is not None
    }
    return {
        "image_total_text": case.text_fields.get("图片总张数", ""),
        "score_distribution_text": case.text_fields.get("所有表现图的分数", ""),
        "feature_texts": {
            "trait": ",".join(f"{label}有{count}张" for label, count in kept.items())
        },
    }


def summarize(cases: Iterable[ParsedCase]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """给导入预检用的整体统计。"""

    cases = list(cases)
    case_levels: dict[str, int] = {}
    image_levels: dict[str, int] = {}
    traits: dict[str, int] = {}
    dropped_traits = 0
    total_images = 0
    for case in cases:
        case_levels[case.level] = case_levels.get(case.level, 0) + 1
        for image in case.images:
            image_levels[image.level] = image_levels.get(image.level, 0) + 1
            total_images += 1
        for label, count in parse_count_text(
            case.text_fields.get("所有图片的类型", "")
        ).items():
            normalized = normalize_trait(label)
            if normalized is None:
                dropped_traits += count
                continue
            traits[normalized] = traits.get(normalized, 0) + count
    return {
        "cases": len(cases),
        "images": total_images,
        "case_levels": dict(sorted(case_levels.items())),
        "image_levels": dict(sorted(image_levels.items())),
        "traits": dict(sorted(traits.items())),
        "dropped_trait_images": dropped_traits,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/design_case_dimension_service.py =====
"""把 NAS 上的案例两维度交付目录落成平台素材。

一个交付目录同时喂两条基准：

* 图片维度（``design_case.dimension_grade.v1``）——每张图一个人工档位，
  需要每张图在平台有真实资产身份，所以逐图建 NAS 资产。
* 素材维度（``design_case.material_aggregate.v1``）——案例正文里的三行聚合统计，
  沿用已上线的口径，不重复解析。

两者共用同一个案例记录：一个案例 = 一个 ``case_record`` 资产 = 一次工作流执行。
逐图档位与逐图资产身份落在 ``design_case_samples.image_grades_json``，
建基准集时直接冻结成 ``workflow_input.case.images`` 和真值里的 ``images``。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable
import hashlib
import uuid

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .design_case_dimension_import import (
    DesignCaseDimensionError,
    ParsedCase,
    build_aggregate_truth,
    build_dimension_truth,
    parse_sample_root,
    summarize,
)
from .design_case_sample_service import (
    CASE_MIME_TYPE,
    CASE_STORAGE_BACKEND,
    DesignCaseSampleServiceError,
)
from .models import Asset, DesignCaseSample, MaterialPackage, MaterialPackageItem
from .nas_storage import (
    NasStorageError,
    inspect_nas_file,
    normalize_nas_uri,
    resolve_nas_uri,
)

DIMENSION_PACKAGE_KEY_PREFIX = "case-dimension:"
IMAGE_GRADES_SCHEMA_VERSION = "design-case-image-grades-v1"


@dataclass
class ImagePlan:
    cloud_id: str
    level: str
    uri: str
    sha256: str
    mime_type: str
    size_bytes: int
    width: int | None
    height: int | None
    original_name: str


@dataclass
class CasePlan:
    cloud_id: str
    level: str
    case_name: str
    case: dict[str, Any]
    image_summary: dict[str, Any]
    images: list[ImagePlan] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)


def _case_payload(case: ParsedCase) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """案例文本字段：只带走素材维度合同登记过的键，其余留在交付文件里。"""

    mapping = {
        "cloud_id": case.cloud_id,
        "case_name": case.case_name,
        "class_name": case.text_fields.get("分类", ""),
        "own_keyword": case.text_fields.get("标签", ""),
        "style_tag": case.text_fields.get("风格", ""),
        "origin_publish_time": case.text_fields.get("采集源发布时间", ""),
        "designer_company": case.text_fields.get("设计师/设计公司", ""),
        "case_text": case.text_fields.get("案例正文", ""),
        "country": case.text_fields.get("城市", ""),
        "design_highlights": case.text_fields.get("设计亮点/设计说明", ""),
        "other_important_info": case.text_fields.get("其他重要信息", ""),
    }
    return {key: value for key, value in mapping.items() if value}


def build_import_plan(
    root_uri: str,
    *,
    nas_root: Path,
) -> tuple[list[CasePlan], dict[str, Any]]:
    """读一遍 NAS 交付目录，产出可预览的导入计划。

    这一步会为每张图算一次哈希（跨 SMB 读全文件），是整个导入里最慢的部分；
    干跑和真正导入共用它，运营在页面上看到的统计与最终落库的完全一致。
    """

    normalized_root = normalize_nas_uri(root_uri)
    root_path = resolve_nas_uri(normalized_root, nas_root)
    nas_root = nas_root.expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
    cases = parse_sample_root(root_path)
    plans: list[CasePlan] = []
    seen_images: dict[str, str] = {}
    for case in cases:
        plan = CasePlan(
            cloud_id=case.cloud_id,
            level=case.level,
            case_name=case.case_name or f"案例 {case.cloud_id}",
            case=_case_payload(case),
            image_summary=build_aggregate_truth(case),
        )
        for image in case.images:
            relative = (case.directory / image.filename).relative_to(nas_root)
            uri = normalize_nas_uri(f"nas://maps/{relative.as_posix()}")
            try:
                info = inspect_nas_file(uri, nas_root)
            except NasStorageError as exc:
                raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
                    "design_case_image_unreadable",
                    f"案例 {case.cloud_id} 的图片 {image.filename} 读不出来：{exc}",
                ) from exc
            # 交付身份是 cloud_id，不是内容哈希。不同编号可以有相同字节、
            # 不同人工档位；只复用底层资产，不合并逐图真值或 foreach 子项。
            owner = seen_images.get(image.cloud_id)
            if owner is not None and owner != case.cloud_id:
                raise DesignCaseDimensionError(
                    "design_case_image_cross_case_duplicate",
                    f"图片 {image.filename} 同时出现在案例 {owner} 与 {case.cloud_id}",
                )
            seen_images[image.cloud_id] = case.cloud_id
            plan.images.append(
                ImagePlan(
                    cloud_id=image.cloud_id,
                    level=image.level,
                    uri=info.uri,
                    sha256=info.sha256,
                    mime_type=info.mime_type,
                    size_bytes=info.size_bytes,
                    width=info.width,
                    height=info.height,
                    original_name=info.original_name,
                )
            )
        plans.append(plan)
    stats = summarize(cases)
    stats["root_uri"] = normalized_root
    return plans, stats


def _image_grades_payload(plan: CasePlan, asset_ids: dict[str, int]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "schema_version": IMAGE_GRADES_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "case_level": plan.level,
        "images": [
            {
                "cloud_id": image.cloud_id,
                "level": image.level,
                "asset_id": asset_ids[image.cloud_id],
                "asset_sha256": image.sha256,
                "source_uri": image.uri,
            }
            for image in sorted(plan.images, key=lambda item: item.cloud_id)
        ],
    }


def _upsert_image_asset(
    db: Session,
    image: ImagePlan,
    *,
    category_key: str,
) -> Asset:
    """按内容哈希复用已在库的同一张图，避免重复导入把资产刷成两份。"""

    existing = db.scalar(
        select(Asset).where(
            Asset.sha256 == image.sha256,
            Asset.category_key == category_key,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    asset = Asset(
        original_name=image.original_name[:500],
        stored_name=f"nas-{uuid.uuid4().hex}{Path(image.original_name).suffix.lower()}",
        storage_backend="nas_maps",
        source_uri=image.uri,
        mime_type=image.mime_type,
        size_bytes=image.size_bytes,
        width=image.width,
        height=image.height,
        sha256=image.sha256,
        category_key=category_key,
        status="uploaded",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    return asset


def _dimension_content_payload(plan: CasePlan) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """冻结完整内容身份；来源路径变化不改变同一交付的身份。"""
    return {
        "schema_version": IMAGE_GRADES_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "case": dict(plan.case),
        "image_summary": dict(plan.image_summary),
        "case_level": plan.level,
        "images": [
            {"cloud_id": image.cloud_id, "sha256": image.sha256, "level": image.level}
            for image in sorted(plan.images, key=lambda item: item.cloud_id)
        ],
    }


def import_dimension_samples(
    db: Session,
    *,
    plans: Iterable[CasePlan],
    category_key: str,
    package_name: str | None,
    actor: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把导入计划落库，返回可直接展示给运营的结果。"""

    plans = list(plans)
    if not plans:
        raise DesignCaseSampleServiceError(
            "design_case_dimension_empty",
            "没有可导入的案例",
        )
    # Reject source replacement before creating any package or sample. Asset URI
    # identity is unique; rewriting the old row would invalidate frozen history.
    uri_hashes: dict[str, str] = {}
    for plan in plans:
        for image in plan.images:
            old_hash = uri_hashes.get(image.uri)
            if old_hash is None:
                old_hash = db.scalar(select(Asset.sha256).where(
                    Asset.source_uri == image.uri,
                    Asset.category_key == category_key,
                ))
            if old_hash is not None and old_hash != image.sha256:
                raise DesignCaseSampleServiceError(
                    "design_case_image_content_changed",
                    f"图片 {image.original_name} 的 NAS 路径已有不同内容；"
                    "请把本次交付放到版本化的新路径后重新导入，历史素材不会被覆盖。",
                )
            uri_hashes[image.uri] = image.sha256
    # Content identity includes the independent human truth and the exact image
    # manifest. Legacy text-only fingerprints intentionally do not collide.
    payloads = [canonical_json(_dimension_content_payload(plan)).encode("utf-8") for plan in plans]
    fingerprints = [hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest() for payload in payloads]
    package_digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
        "category_key": category_key, "samples": sorted(set(fingerprints)),
    }).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    package_key = f"{DIMENSION_PACKAGE_KEY_PREFIX}{package_digest}"
    package = db.scalar(select(MaterialPackage).where(MaterialPackage.package_key == package_key))
    package_exists = package is not None
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if package is None:
        package = MaterialPackage(
            package_key=package_key,
            name=(package_name or f"案例两维度样本 {now.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}").strip(),
            # 与案例样本包同属 manual_upload，靠 package_key 前缀区分。
            source="manual_upload",
            category_key=category_key,
            created_by=actor,
        )
        db.add(package)
        db.flush()

    created: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    duplicates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    reused_assets = 0
    new_assets = 0
    for position, plan in enumerate(plans, start=1):
        content_hash = fingerprints[position - 1]
        existing = db.scalar(
            select(DesignCaseSample).where(
                DesignCaseSample.category_key == category_key,
                DesignCaseSample.content_hash == content_hash,
            )
        )
        if existing is not None:
            if not package_exists:
                db.add(MaterialPackageItem(
                    package_id=package.id,
                    asset_id=existing.asset_id,
                    original_name=existing.case_name,
                    duplicate=False,
                    position=position,
                ))
            duplicates.append(
                {
                    "cloud_id": plan.cloud_id,
                    "reason": "素材库已存在相同案例",
                    "asset_id": existing.asset_id,
                }
            )
            continue
        asset_ids: dict[str, int] = {}
        for image in plan.images:
            before = db.scalar(
                select(Asset.id).where(
                    Asset.sha256 == image.sha256,
                    Asset.category_key == category_key,
                )
            )
            asset = _upsert_image_asset(db, image, category_key=category_key)
            asset_ids[image.cloud_id] = asset.id
            if before is None:
                new_assets += 1
            else:
                reused_assets += 1
        payload = payloads[position - 1]
        case_asset = Asset(
            original_name=plan.case_name[:500],
            stored_name=f"case-{content_hash[:32]}.json",
            storage_backend=CASE_STORAGE_BACKEND,
            source_uri=None,
            mime_type=CASE_MIME_TYPE,
            size_bytes=len(payload),
            width=None,
            height=None,
            sha256=content_hash,
            category_key=category_key,
            status="uploaded",
        )
        db.add(case_asset)
        db.flush()
        db.add(
            MaterialPackageItem(
                package_id=package.id,
                asset_id=case_asset.id,
                original_name=case_asset.original_name,
                duplicate=False,
                position=position,
            )
        )
        record = DesignCaseSample(
            asset_id=case_asset.id,
            category_key=category_key,
            cloud_id=plan.cloud_id[:120],
            case_name=plan.case_name[:500],
            input_kind="aggregated",
            case_json=canonical_json(dict(plan.case)),
            image_summary_json=canonical_json(dict(plan.image_summary)),
            image_grades_json=canonical_json(_image_grades_payload(plan, asset_ids)),
            source_package_id=package.id,
            source_row_number=position,
            content_hash=content_hash,
            created_by=actor,
        )
        db.add(record)
        db.flush()
        created.append(
            {
                "asset_id": case_asset.id,
                "sample_id": record.id,
                "cloud_id": plan.cloud_id,
                "case_name": record.case_name,
                "case_level": plan.level,
                "image_count": len(plan.images),
            }
        )
    return {
        "schema_version": "design-case-dimension-import-v1",
        "package": {
            "id": package.id,
            "package_key": package.package_key,
            "name": package.name,
            "category_key": package.category_key,
            "created_by": package.created_by,
        },
        "summary": {
            "created_count": len(created),
            "duplicate_count": len(duplicates),
            "image_asset_created": new_assets,
            "image_asset_reused": reused_assets,
        },
        "items": created,
        "duplicates": duplicates,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/design_case_image_grade.py =====
"""图片维度：把逐图模型分投影成档位，并与人工逐图真值比对。

两条口径分开，谁都不许推导谁（2026-09-09 运营定案）：

* **真值**按人工标注为准。运营交付的样本里，每张图的档位是人工纠偏过的结果，
  不从分数反推——反推出来的档位只是分数的另一种写法，拿它当真值等于自己考自己。
* **预测**按运营给的阈值把模型分映射成档位：优 85-100、中 72-84、中差 60-71、
  极差 0-59。图片维度没有过滤档，所以最低一档必须兜住 0 分。

两者的差距就是要度量的模型能力。用交付样本实测，这套阈值的逐图准确率是
39.1%（939 张秩对齐样本），错误全部是「预测高一档」，没有一例反向——
说明模型分与人工档位严格同序，只是刻度不同。这个数字是基线，不是缺陷。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
import math
from typing import Any


# 运营 2026-09-09 给的图片维度映射。写成「下界 + 档位」的降序表，
# 落库时随结果一起冻结，事后复盘不用猜当时用的是哪套刻度。
DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS: tuple[tuple[str, float], ...] = (
    ("L1", 85.0),
    ("L2", 72.0),
    ("L3", 60.0),
    ("L4", 0.0),
)
IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS: tuple[str, ...] = ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4")
IMAGE_GRADE_SCHEMA_VERSION = "workflow-case-image-grade-v1"
IMAGE_GRADE_COMPARISON_SCHEMA_VERSION = "design-case-image-grade-comparison-v1"


class DesignCaseImageGradeError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _fail(code: str, message: str) -> None:
    raise DesignCaseImageGradeError(code, message)


def normalize_grade_bands(definition: Any) -> tuple[tuple[str, float], ...]:
    """校验一套档位阈值：档位唯一、下界严格递减、最低一档兜住 0 分。

    运营改口径时只改这张表，不改代码；但表本身必须能给任意分数唯一定档，
    否则回归会在半路上抛错，还不如导入时就拒绝。
    """

    if definition is None:
        return DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS
    if not isinstance(definition, Sequence) or isinstance(definition, (str, bytes)):
        _fail("image_grade_bands_invalid", "档位阈值必须是「档位 + 下界」的数组")
    bands: list[tuple[str, float]] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for raw in definition:
        if isinstance(raw, Mapping):
            level = raw.get("level")
            minimum = raw.get("min_inclusive")
        elif isinstance(raw, Sequence) and not isinstance(raw, (str, bytes)):
            if len(raw) != 2:
                _fail("image_grade_bands_invalid", "档位阈值项必须是「档位, 下界」两元组")
            level, minimum = raw[0], raw[1]
        else:
            _fail("image_grade_bands_invalid", "档位阈值项必须是对象或两元组")
        if level not in IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS:
            _fail(
                "image_grade_bands_invalid",
                f"档位 {level!r} 不在 L1-L4 内——图片维度没有过滤档",
            )
        if level in seen:
            _fail("image_grade_bands_invalid", f"档位 {level} 重复")
        seen.add(level)
        if isinstance(minimum, bool) or not isinstance(minimum, (int, float)):
            _fail("image_grade_bands_invalid", f"档位 {level} 的下界必须是数值")
        value = float(minimum)
        if not math.isfinite(value):
            _fail("image_grade_bands_invalid", f"档位 {level} 的下界必须是有限数值")
        bands.append((str(level), value))
    if not bands:
        _fail("image_grade_bands_invalid", "档位阈值不能为空")
    ordered = sorted(bands, key=lambda entry: -entry[1])
    for higher, lower in zip(ordered, ordered[1:]):
        if higher[1] <= lower[1]:
            _fail("image_grade_bands_invalid", "档位下界必须严格递减")
    if [level for level, _ in ordered] != list(IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS):
        _fail(
            "image_grade_bands_invalid",
            "档位阈值必须完整覆盖 L1-L4，且下界顺序与档位一致",
        )
    if ordered[-1][1] > 0:
        _fail(
            "image_grade_bands_invalid",
            "最低一档的下界必须是 0——图片维度没有过滤档，0 分也要有去处",
        )
    return tuple(ordered)


def grade_for_score(score: float, bands: Iterable[tuple[str, float]]) -> str:
    for level, minimum in bands:
        if score >= minimum:
            return level
    # normalize_grade_bands 已保证最低档兜住 0；负分才会走到这里。
    raise DesignCaseImageGradeError(
        "image_grade_unmatched", f"分数 {score} 未被任何档位覆盖"
    )


def bands_payload(bands: Iterable[tuple[str, float]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [{"level": level, "min_inclusive": minimum} for level, minimum in bands]


def build_image_grade_projection(
    aggregate: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    bands: Any = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把案例聚合结果里的逐图分数投影成逐图档位。

    ``aggregate`` 是 :func:`workflow_case_aggregation.aggregate_case` 的产物，
    它的 ``results[]`` 每项都带 ``item_key``（工作流按 ``$.cloud_id`` 冻结的图片
    身份）和 ``score``。没有分数的子项（技术图、分析图等非表现图，以及失败/跳过
    的子项）不猜档位，单独落在 ``ungraded`` 里——凭空补一个档位会把「模型没答」
    伪装成「模型答错」，两者在纠偏时的处置完全不同。
    """

    if not isinstance(aggregate, Mapping):
        _fail("image_grade_aggregate_invalid", "案例聚合结果必须是对象")
    resolved = normalize_grade_bands(bands)
    results = aggregate.get("results")
    if not isinstance(results, list):
        _fail("image_grade_aggregate_invalid", "案例聚合结果缺少逐图子项数组")

    images: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    ungraded: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    distribution = {level: 0 for level in IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS}
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for entry in results:
        if not isinstance(entry, Mapping):
            _fail("image_grade_aggregate_invalid", "逐图子项必须是对象")
        item_key = entry.get("item_key")
        if not isinstance(item_key, str) or not item_key:
            _fail("image_grade_item_key_invalid", "逐图子项缺少稳定身份")
        if item_key in seen:
            _fail("image_grade_item_key_duplicate", f"逐图子项身份重复：{item_key}")
        seen.add(item_key)
        score = entry.get("score")
        if entry.get("status") != "succeeded":
            ungraded.append({"cloud_id": item_key, "reason": str(entry.get("status") or "unknown")})
            continue
        if isinstance(score, bool) or not isinstance(score, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(float(score)):
            ungraded.append({"cloud_id": item_key, "reason": "no_score"})
            continue
        level = grade_for_score(float(score), resolved)
        distribution[level] += 1
        images.append({"cloud_id": item_key, "score": float(score), "level": level})

    images.sort(key=lambda image: image["cloud_id"])
    ungraded.sort(key=lambda image: image["cloud_id"])
    return {
        "schema_version": IMAGE_GRADE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "bands": bands_payload(resolved),
        "images": images,
        "level_distribution": distribution,
        "graded_images": len(images),
        "ungraded": ungraded,
    }


def _grade_index(value: Any) -> int | None:
    if isinstance(value, str) and value in IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS:
        return IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS.index(value)
    return None


def compare_image_grades(
    expected_images: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]],
    actual_images: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """按 cloud_id 对齐人工逐图真值与模型投影档位，产出混淆矩阵。

    只统计两边都有档位的图片。真值有、预测没有的记 ``missing``（模型没答，
    多半是非表现图无分数）；预测有、真值没有的记 ``unexpected``（真值没覆盖到，
    通常说明冻结的图片清单和这一轮跑的对不上，属于数据问题而非模型问题）。
    """

    expected_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    for image in expected_images:
        if not isinstance(image, Mapping):
            continue
        cloud_id = image.get("cloud_id")
        level = image.get("level")
        if isinstance(cloud_id, str) and cloud_id and isinstance(level, str):
            expected_map[cloud_id] = level
    actual_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    for image in actual_images:
        if not isinstance(image, Mapping):
            continue
        cloud_id = image.get("cloud_id")
        level = image.get("level")
        if isinstance(cloud_id, str) and cloud_id and isinstance(level, str):
            actual_map[cloud_id] = level

    matrix: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {
        row: {column: 0 for column in IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS} for row in IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS
    }
    compared = correct = 0
    over = under = 0
    mismatches: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for cloud_id in sorted(set(expected_map) & set(actual_map)):
        truth = expected_map[cloud_id]
        predicted = actual_map[cloud_id]
        if truth not in matrix or predicted not in matrix[truth]:
            continue
        matrix[truth][predicted] += 1
        compared += 1
        if truth == predicted:
            correct += 1
            continue
        truth_index = _grade_index(truth)
        predicted_index = _grade_index(predicted)
        if truth_index is not None and predicted_index is not None:
            # 下标越小档位越好：预测下标更小 = 模型给高了。
            if predicted_index < truth_index:
                over += 1
            else:
                under += 1
        mismatches.append(
            {"cloud_id": cloud_id, "expected": truth, "actual": predicted}
        )
    missing = sorted(set(expected_map) - set(actual_map))
    unexpected = sorted(set(actual_map) - set(expected_map))
    return {
        "schema_version": IMAGE_GRADE_COMPARISON_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "compared": compared,
        "correct": correct,
        "accuracy": (correct / compared) if compared else 0.0,
        "over_graded": over,
        "under_graded": under,
        "confusion": matrix,
        "mismatches": mismatches,
        "missing": missing,
        "unexpected": unexpected,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/design_case_sample_import.py =====
"""素材（案例）维度样本导入解析。

运营的素材维度样本是「一行一个案例」：既有案例自身的文本字段（标题/分类/风格/
正文…），也有由案例图片聚合出来的三行统计（总张数、分数分布、类型分布）。
2026-09-04 老大定：两种输入都支持，按表格里出现的列自动判别。

- 文本输入（text_only）：只给案例文本，图片统计缺失或为空。跑回归时走案例文本
  评测，不需要逐图。
- 聚合回灌（aggregated）：带图片统计三项，反解成平台的聚合结构作为冻结输入，
  可跳过逐图与聚合节点，直接验证下游。

解析只认字段，不写库；调用方拿到结果自行决定建包/建集。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping

from .workflow_case_aggregation import CASE_SUMMARY_SCHEMA_VERSION

SCHEMA_VERSION = "design-case-sample-v1"

TEXT_ONLY = "text_only"
AGGREGATED = "aggregated"


class DesignCaseSampleError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


# 表格里的中文标签 → 平台案例输入字段。左侧允许出现的写法都列全，
# 避免运营换个说法就静默丢字段。
_FIELD_ALIASES: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "case_name": ("素材标题", "案例标题", "项目名称", "案例名称"),
    "parent_class_name": ("一级分类", "父分类"),
    "class_name": ("分类", "二级分类", "类目"),
    "own_keyword": ("标签", "关键词"),
    "style_tag": ("风格", "风格标签"),
    "origin_publish_time": ("采集源发布时间", "发布时间"),
    "designer_company": ("设计师/设计公司", "设计公司", "设计师"),
    "case_text": ("案例正文", "正文"),
    "case_text_head": ("案例正文前500个字", "正文前500字"),
    "case_text_tail": ("案例正文后500个字", "正文后500字"),
    "country": ("城市", "国家", "地区"),
    "design_highlights": ("设计亮点/设计说明", "设计亮点", "设计说明"),
    "dtime": ("设计时间",),
    "btime": ("建成时间", "完工时间"),
    "scale": ("项目规模", "面积", "建筑面积"),
    "other_important_info": ("其他重要信息", "备注"),
    "cloud_id": ("cloud_id", "素材ID", "案例ID", "云ID"),
    "project_id": ("project_id", "项目ID"),
}

_IMAGE_TOTAL_LABELS = ("图片总张图", "图片总张数", "图片数量")
_SCORE_DIST_LABELS = ("所有表现图的分数", "表现图分数", "图片分数分布")
_TYPE_DIST_LABELS = ("所有图片的类型", "图片类型", "图片类型分布")

_LABEL_LINE = re.compile(r"^\s*([^：:]{1,40})[：:](.*)$")
_COUNT_ENTRY = re.compile(r"^(.*?)有(\d+)张$")
_TOTAL_TEXT = re.compile(r"^(\d+)\s*张?$")


@dataclass
class ParsedCaseSample:
    row_number: int
    input_kind: str
    case: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    image_summary: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    issues: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def ok(self) -> bool:
        return not self.issues


def _canonical_label(label: str) -> str:
    return label.strip().replace("　", "").replace(" ", "")


def _field_for_label(label: str) -> str | None:
    normalized = _canonical_label(label)
    for key, aliases in _FIELD_ALIASES.items():
        if normalized in {_canonical_label(a) for a in aliases}:
            return key
    return None


def has_case_marker(text: str) -> bool:
    """判断一格文本是不是案例块：首个标签行能对上素材标题。"""

    for line in str(text or "").splitlines():
        matched = _LABEL_LINE.match(line)
        if not matched:
            continue
        return _field_for_label(matched.group(1)) == "case_name"
    return False


def _summary_slot(label: str) -> str | None:
    normalized = _canonical_label(label)
    for slot, labels in (
        ("image_total_text", _IMAGE_TOTAL_LABELS),
        ("score_distribution_text", _SCORE_DIST_LABELS),
        ("type_distribution_text", _TYPE_DIST_LABELS),
    ):
        if normalized in {_canonical_label(x) for x in labels}:
            return slot
    return None


def parse_count_text(text: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """把「82分有3张,84分有3张」解析成 [{value, count}]。

    真实样本里有两种写法混用（2026-09-04 实测 2000 条）：
    - 「实景照片有15张」带计数；
    - 「实景照片」省略计数，表示 1 张，且同一类型可能分多段出现。
    同名条目合并计数，顺序按首次出现，保证「总数=各项之和」可核对。
    """

    ordered: list[str] = []
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for chunk in re.split(r"[,，、]", text or ""):
        item = chunk.strip()
        if not item:
            continue
        matched = _COUNT_ENTRY.match(item)
        if matched is None:
            label, count = item.rstrip("分"), 1
        else:
            label, count = matched.group(1).strip().rstrip("分"), int(matched.group(2))
        if not label:
            raise DesignCaseSampleError(
                "design_case_sample_count_invalid",
                f"无法解析计数片段「{item}」，应形如「82分有3张」",
            )
        if label not in counts:
            ordered.append(label)
            counts[label] = 0
        counts[label] += count
    return [{"value": label, "count": counts[label]} for label in ordered]


def _parse_total(text: str) -> int:
    matched = _TOTAL_TEXT.match((text or "").strip())
    if matched is None:
        raise DesignCaseSampleError(
            "design_case_sample_total_invalid",
            f"图片总张数「{text}」无法解析，应形如「16张」",
        )
    return int(matched.group(1))


def parse_case_block(text: str, *, row_number: int) -> ParsedCaseSample:
    """解析一行（一个案例）的多行「标签：值」文本。"""

    sample = ParsedCaseSample(row_number=row_number, input_kind=TEXT_ONLY)
    raw_summary: dict[str, str] = {}
    current_key: str | None = None
    for line in (text or "").splitlines():
        matched = _LABEL_LINE.match(line)
        if matched is None:
            # 正文换行：并入上一个字段，保留原始换行
            if current_key and current_key in sample.case:
                sample.case[current_key] = f"{sample.case[current_key]}\n{line.strip()}"
            continue
        label, value = matched.group(1), matched.group(2).strip()
        slot = _summary_slot(label)
        if slot is not None:
            raw_summary[slot] = value
            current_key = None
            continue
        key = _field_for_label(label)
        if key is None:
            current_key = None
            continue
        # 正文分段：前/后 500 字并入正文，不单列
        if key in {"case_text_head", "case_text_tail"}:
            existing = sample.case.get("case_text") or ""
            sample.case["case_text"] = f"{existing}\n{value}".strip() if existing else value
            current_key = "case_text"
            continue
        sample.case[key] = value
        current_key = key

    if raw_summary.get("image_total_text") or raw_summary.get("score_distribution_text"):
        sample.input_kind = AGGREGATED
        try:
            sample.image_summary = build_image_summary(raw_summary)
        except DesignCaseSampleError as exc:
            sample.issues.append(str(exc))
            sample.input_kind = TEXT_ONLY
    if not sample.case.get("case_name"):
        sample.issues.append("缺少素材标题")
    # 正文为空但标题、分类、标签齐全的案例在真实样本里占约 1.6%，
    # 它们仍可用于案例文本评测（标题与标签本身就是可评信息），只作提醒不拒绝。
    if not sample.case.get("case_text"):
        sample.warnings.append("案例正文为空，仅凭标题与标签评测")
    return sample


def build_image_summary(raw: Mapping[str, str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把三行统计文本反解成与平台聚合结果同口径的结构。"""

    total_text = (raw.get("image_total_text") or "").strip()
    total = _parse_total(total_text) if total_text else 0
    scores = parse_count_text(raw.get("score_distribution_text") or "")
    types = parse_count_text(raw.get("type_distribution_text") or "")
    scored = sum(entry["count"] for entry in scores)
    if scores and total and scored > total:
        raise DesignCaseSampleError(
            "design_case_sample_total_mismatch",
            f"分数分布合计 {scored} 张超过图片总数 {total} 张",
        )
    distribution: dict[str, int] = {}
    for entry in scores:
        label = str(entry["value"])
        # 分数分布的取值必须是数字。省略计数的写法（「实景照片」=1 张）只对
        # 类型分布成立，套到分数上会把「八十二分三张」这类写法静默存成一个
        # 名叫「八十二分三张」的分档。
        try:
            float(label)
        except ValueError:
            raise DesignCaseSampleError(
                "design_case_sample_score_invalid",
                f"分数分布里的「{label}」不是分数，应形如「82分有3张」",
            ) from None
        distribution[label] = distribution.get(label, 0) + entry["count"]
    return {
        "schema_version": CASE_SUMMARY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "image_total": total,
        "image_total_text": total_text or f"{total}张",
        "score_distribution_text": raw.get("score_distribution_text") or "",
        "score_distribution": distribution,
        "scored_images": scored,
        "feature_texts": (
            {"trait": raw.get("type_distribution_text") or ""}
            if raw.get("type_distribution_text")
            else {}
        ),
        "feature_counts": {"trait": {str(e["value"]): e["count"] for e in types}} if types else {},
    }


def parse_case_rows(rows: Iterable[str | tuple[int, str]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """解析整张表，返回可直接展示给运营的导入计划。"""

    samples: list[ParsedCaseSample] = []
    for index, row in enumerate(rows):
        # 支持直接传文本，也支持传 (表格行号, 文本)，后者让运营能按行对账。
        if isinstance(row, (tuple, list)):
            row_number, text = int(row[0]), row[1]
        else:
            row_number, text = index + 1, row
        samples.append(parse_case_block(text, row_number=row_number))
    kinds: dict[str, int] = {}
    for sample in samples:
        kinds[sample.input_kind] = kinds.get(sample.input_kind, 0) + 1
    return {
        "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "total_rows": len(samples),
        "usable_rows": sum(1 for s in samples if s.ok),
        "rejected_rows": sum(1 for s in samples if not s.ok),
        "warned_rows": sum(1 for s in samples if s.warnings),
        "input_kinds": kinds,
        "samples": samples,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/design_case_sample_service.py =====
"""把解析好的素材（案例）维度样本落成案例素材。

案例素材没有图片文件，但走与图片素材相同的链路：进素材库 → 被基准集引用
→ 跑存量回归 → 纠偏。因此仍建 Asset 行（承载身份、去重、类目），案例文本与
图片聚合摘要落在 design_case_samples 表里。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .design_case_sample_import import AGGREGATED, ParsedCaseSample
from .models import Asset, DesignCaseSample, MaterialPackage, MaterialPackageItem

CASE_MIME_TYPE = "application/vnd.3d66.design-case+json"
CASE_STORAGE_BACKEND = "case_record"
CASE_PACKAGE_KEY_PREFIX = "case-sample:"


class DesignCaseSampleServiceError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 422) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


def case_content_hash(case: Mapping[str, Any], image_summary: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> str:
    """案例内容指纹：同一份案例重复导入时可识别，不依赖行号或文件名。"""

    payload = {"case": dict(case), "image_summary": dict(image_summary or {})}
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _case_identity(case: Mapping[str, Any], content_hash: str) -> str:
    cloud_id = str(case.get("cloud_id") or "").strip()
    return cloud_id or f"sha256:{content_hash[:32]}"


def import_case_samples(
    db: Session,
    *,
    samples: Iterable[ParsedCaseSample],
    category_key: str,
    package_name: str | None,
    actor: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """落库并返回可直接展示给运营的导入结果。"""

    usable = [s for s in samples if s.ok]
    if not usable:
        raise DesignCaseSampleServiceError(
            "design_case_sample_empty",
            "没有可导入的案例样本",
        )
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    package = MaterialPackage(
        package_key=f"{CASE_PACKAGE_KEY_PREFIX}{uuid.uuid4().hex}",
        name=(package_name or f"案例样本 {now.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}").strip(),
        # 运营在页面上传表格，与图片素材包同属 manual_upload；
        # 案例包靠 package_key 前缀与 design_case_samples 关联识别，
        # 不新增 source 取值以免动生产的 CHECK 约束。
        source="manual_upload",
        category_key=category_key,
        created_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(package)
    db.flush()

    created: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    duplicates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen_hashes: set[str] = set()
    for sample in usable:
        content_hash = case_content_hash(sample.case, sample.image_summary)
        identity = _case_identity(sample.case, content_hash)
        if content_hash in seen_hashes:
            duplicates.append({"row_number": sample.row_number, "reason": "本次导入内重复"})
            continue
        seen_hashes.add(content_hash)
        existing = db.scalar(
            select(DesignCaseSample).where(
                DesignCaseSample.category_key == category_key,
                DesignCaseSample.content_hash == content_hash,
            )
        )
        if existing is not None:
            duplicates.append(
                {
                    "row_number": sample.row_number,
                    "reason": "素材库已存在相同案例",
                    "asset_id": existing.asset_id,
                }
            )
            continue
        payload = canonical_json(
            {"case": dict(sample.case), "image_summary": dict(sample.image_summary or {})}
        ).encode("utf-8")
        asset = Asset(
            original_name=(sample.case.get("case_name") or identity)[:500],
            stored_name=f"case-{content_hash[:32]}.json",
            storage_backend=CASE_STORAGE_BACKEND,
            source_uri=None,
            mime_type=CASE_MIME_TYPE,
            size_bytes=len(payload),
            width=None,
            height=None,
            sha256=content_hash,
            category_key=category_key,
            status="uploaded",
        )
        db.add(asset)
        db.flush()
        # 案例素材也进素材包条目：素材包列表、张数统计、整包建基准集都靠它。
        db.add(
            MaterialPackageItem(
                package_id=package.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                original_name=asset.original_name,
                duplicate=False,
                position=len(created) + 1,
            )
        )
        record = DesignCaseSample(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            category_key=category_key,
            cloud_id=str(sample.case.get("cloud_id") or "")[:120],
            case_name=(sample.case.get("case_name") or "")[:500],
            input_kind=sample.input_kind,
            case_json=canonical_json(dict(sample.case)),
            image_summary_json=canonical_json(dict(sample.image_summary or {})),
            source_package_id=package.id,
            source_row_number=sample.row_number,
            content_hash=content_hash,
            created_by=actor,
        )
        db.add(record)
        db.flush()
        created.append(
            {
                "asset_id": asset.id,
                "sample_id": record.id,
                "row_number": sample.row_number,
                "case_name": record.case_name,
                "input_kind": record.input_kind,
                "identity": identity,
                "warnings": list(sample.warnings),
            }
        )
    return {
        "schema_version": "design-case-sample-import-v1",
        "package": {
            "id": package.id,
            "package_key": package.package_key,
            "name": package.name,
            "category_key": package.category_key,
            "created_by": package.created_by,
        },
        "summary": {
            "created_count": len(created),
            "duplicate_count": len(duplicates),
            "aggregated_count": sum(1 for c in created if c["input_kind"] == AGGREGATED),
            "warned_count": sum(1 for c in created if c["warnings"]),
        },
        "items": created,
        "duplicates": duplicates,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/design_case_sample_xlsx.py =====
"""从运营上传的 xlsx 里取出案例文本块。

运营给的表格没有固定表头：有的第一行就是数据，有的带一行标题；案例块可能
在任意一列。这里只做定位，不做解析——解析规则在
``design_case_sample_import`` 里，两种输入形态由那边自动判别。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from .design_case_sample_import import DesignCaseSampleError, has_case_marker
from .p0e_safe_import import LoadedTable, XlsxLimits, read_xlsx_table


def _cell_text(value: Any) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return ""
    return str(value).strip()


def locate_case_column(table: LoadedTable) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """挑出案例文本所在列，并判断首行是表头还是数据。"""

    if not table.rows:
        raise DesignCaseSampleError("design_case_sample_no_rows", "表格没有数据行")
    hits: dict[int, int] = {}
    for _, values in table.rows:
        for column, value in values.items():
            if has_case_marker(_cell_text(value)):
                hits[column] = hits.get(column, 0) + 1
    if not hits:
        raise DesignCaseSampleError(
            "design_case_sample_column_not_found",
            "表格里没有找到案例样本列：每格应以“素材标题：”开头",
        )
    # 命中最多的列即案例列；并列时取最靠左的，结果对同一份表格稳定。
    best = max(hits.items(), key=lambda item: (item[1], -item[0]))
    column = best[0]
    header_values = table.rows[0][1]
    header_is_data = has_case_marker(_cell_text(header_values.get(column)))
    return {
        "column_number": column,
        "match_count": best[1],
        "header_is_data": header_is_data,
    }


def extract_case_texts(table: LoadedTable) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """返回按表格顺序排列的案例文本，行号沿用表格自身行号便于运营对账。"""

    located = locate_case_column(table)
    column = located["column_number"]
    rows = table.rows if located["header_is_data"] else table.data_rows
    texts: list[tuple[int, str]] = []
    empty_rows: list[int] = []
    for row_number, values in rows:
        text = _cell_text(values.get(column))
        if not text:
            empty_rows.append(row_number)
            continue
        texts.append((row_number, text))
